## 2026-10-07, P2-U1, Custody core and PersonalAccount (with Step 0 dev fixes)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the Executor (P2-U2), the oracle adapter (P2-U3), the signer (P2-U4), the StrategyVault, the deposit and withdraw UI (P2-U7), and any testnet or mainnet deployment.
Summary: Owners' trading money now has a home: a small, non-upgradeable custody core whose only exits are the owner's always-on withdrawal and an Executor path that starts unset behind a 9-day timelock, PersonalAccount clones per agent and owner, and an AccountFactory with the beta caps and allowlist. Step 0 made "Reveal next as" persistent and targeted, made stuck transactions after a fork reset visible and fixable, and proved the services recover from a fork reset.
- Step 0, reveal steering (2e84d49, D-225): steers in Postgres (migration 0006), aimed at a wallet (its lowest unrevealed agent in the batch, or its next mint) or at one pending agent (with its owner recorded, so a fork reset cannot hand the steer to another wallet's agent #N); settled only after the delivery is mined; the console card lists each pending steer with exactly which agent it applies to, and cancels. Tests: 6 steering rules, the API against Postgres including a simulated restart, the keeper's fork test steering agent #4 by ID and, in a batch of #5 and #6, wallet #6's agent rather than the batch's first.
- Step 0, stuck transactions (79247ea, 20caa1a, ec2515b, 5df10dd, D-226): on the local fork the mint page compares the wallet's fork nonce with anvil's queue and shows a warning (WalletNotice gains a warning tone, on /design first) with both nonces and the wallet reset that fixes it; a mint queued ahead of the fork stops at once with the same text instead of waiting two minutes. The console's Fork page gains "Stuck transactions": the fork nonce and queue for an account, drop the queue, or move the nonce forward. A throwaway anvil showed that anvil never promotes a queued transaction, even after `anvil_setNonce`, so aligning also drops the queue. Tests: detector and receipt-wait units, the mint flow, two mint e2e tests (with captures), the console refusal off the fork, and the devenv tools against an anvil of their own.
- Step 0, fork resets (17f568a): nothing needed fixing. A new fork test runs the real indexer, keeper and reconcile on a fork of their own, restarts the fork while their state describes the old one, and checks that the indexer rewinds on its first step (no waiting for the old watermark), the old agent is deprovisioned, the keeper reports the missing AgentNFT and recovers, and a new mint is indexed, revealed as the Bee and provisioned; a reset found only after the new fork passed the old watermark is a reorg and recovers the same way.
- Contracts (1929c88, 6e6648c, D-227 to D-229): `CustodyCore` (withdraw any token, `withdrawAll` with claimable credits for a token that reverts, `claim` to any address; `executeSwap` and `pullForSwap` for the factory's Executor with backstops of 12% per trade, 45% per non-USDC asset and 1% slippage against the oracle, only tokenIn down and only tokenOut up, reduce-only and paused allowing only swaps into USDC; modes from packages/domain, tightened by the owner, guardian or sentinel key, unpaused only by the owner; storage reentrancy guard; no `receive()`); `PersonalAccount` (one clone per agent and owner, USDC and WMON only, exact-receipt deposits that refuse a fee-on-transfer token, trading only while the owner holds the agent at the intent's epoch, principal for the caps); `AccountFactory` (clones, 100 USDC per account and 2,000 USDC platform caps, allowlist, buy list, Executor, oracle, guardian and sentinel references; instant tightening, 9-day timelocked loosening with a 7-day execution window; no path to account funds). Internal units, `poke()` and the 7-day peak move to P2-U3 with the breaker (D-227).
- Tests (1929c88, df5d7bd, 120dbd7): 45 PersonalAccount and 16 AccountFactory unit tests (every rule above, withdrawal with the factory, AgentNFT, oracle and Executor etched to revert or burn gas, each misbehaving-Executor case caught by the core, MV-S5); a fuzz test of arbitrary calldata from anyone but the owner, and of withdrawal amounts; an invariant suite (48 runs, depth 60) driving the owner, guardian, sentinel, admin, strangers, donations, blacklists and agent sales with the Executor unset, proving funds leave only to the owner's withdrawal recipient, with a probe that runs reach deposits, withdrawals and credits and a mutation that makes it fail (L-102); reentrancy from the owner itself through a token hook during withdraw, withdrawAll, claim and deposit; the real AgentNFT with a sale through the escrow; fork tests with Monad's real USDC and WMON, including Circle's blacklist and pause.
- Deployment and tools (74bd308, D-230): `pnpm deploy:account-factory` (deterministic, asserts the deployed state; deploys AgentNFT first if needed) and `pnpm custody:local` (create, deposit, withdraw, show, demo, refusing an over-cap deposit with the reason); address book entries `account_factory` 0xd2Dc...362d and `personal_account_implementation` 0xcD8c...f35c, local only, checked by `pnpm test:fork`; a domain test holds the Solidity mode enum to `ACCOUNT_MODES`.
- Slither and gas (6e6648c): 41 results, 5 fixed, 36 justified in `evidence/p2-u1/SLITHER.md`, including AgentNFT's reentrancy-eth on `requestReveal` (documented ordering against a trusted Entropy; AgentNFT left unchanged because any edit moves its deterministic address). Gas in `evidence/p2-u1/GAS.md`: create 235,194; deposit 194,096 to 244,224 and withdraw 158,792 with real USDC.
- Also fixed: the design system's select menu ran off short screens (L-101, 6e912a4), and the console fixture's steers leaked between runs (L-103, 1b474bc).
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck; 1,254 vitest tests (none skipped); forge 139 passed, 3 skipped (the fork suites, run by test:fork); test:fork 17 of 17 and the address book (its first run today failed on cold upstream fetches and the classified retry passed; a second invocation failed both attempts on USDC and Entropy reads from the upstream, 6 tests including the existing AgentNFT ones, and the third passed at once: L-75 and L-91's flake, measured at 1 in 3 today); web e2e 150 passed with 2 desktop-only skips (twice after re-baselining, and again after the select fix); console e2e 48 of 48 (twice); web live 7 of 7; My Agents live 1 of 1; the orchestrator live check 25 of 25 (report in evidence/p1-u7). Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped, after checking memory. Every changed capture was looked at at both widths before re-baselining.
- Dev state: I changed the playtest fork by mistake (L-100) and put it back: AgentNFT and agent #1 (owner 0x683e...5f76) are as found; USDC's supply and balances, the test minter's role and the three affected nonces and balances read the same as at block 109670005; the head moved from 109670005 to 109670010 (5 blocks, no lasting effect). No custody contract is deployed on the playtest fork. dev:all was started for orientation and stopped before the heavy suites; anvil, Postgres, Redis and LiteLLM keep running. The development database gets migration 0006 on the next orchestrator start. No test fork, test database, tunnel or sandbox is left.
Notes for the owner:
- Restart dev:all to load the new orchestrator, keeper, web and console code.
- To try custody: `pnpm deploy:account-factory`, then `pnpm custody:local demo` (or `create`, `deposit 25`, `withdraw 10`, `show`, with `--owner 7` for another test wallet). Only anvil accounts 6 to 9 are allowlisted locally (A-33).
- Slither is installed outside the repo (`uv tool install slither-analyzer`); it runs `forge clean`, so run `forge build` after it.
- Q-46 needs a decision before PB-U1: adding a beta tester to the allowlist waits 9 days under the plan's loosening rule.
Suggestions:

- P2-U3: set the oracle on the factory (timelocked, or in the deploy script for local), add internal units, `poke()` and the peak; WMON deposits work once an oracle is set.
- P2-U7: allowlist the owner's real wallet locally (a redeploy with another list, which moves the local address, or a timelocked addition with the fork's clock moved) and show the account's address from `predictPersonalAccount` before it exists.
- The core's backstops are constants; if the plan wants admin-tunable backstops, that needs a decision before the StrategyVault reuses the core.

## 2026-10-07, P2-U3, Oracle adapter and circuit breaker (with Step 0 fixes)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the Executor and venue adapter (P2-U2), the signer (P2-U4), chain tools (P2-U5), UI, and any testnet or mainnet deployment.
Summary: Accounts now have reliable prices and a circuit breaker. An immutable oracle adapter reads Chainlink MON/USD and the v4 pool, refuses an unusable or manipulated price with a reason code, and guards deposits against a USDC depeg; the custody core values an account once per transaction through it and trips REDUCE_ONLY at a 10% drop from the 7-day peak and PAUSED at 20%, on a value-per-unit basis that the owner's own deposits and withdrawals never move. packages/policy mirrors every rule, and one fixture holds the two sides together.
- Step 0, Slither's High in AgentNFT (ed0a55a): a false positive. `requestReveal` writes its pending batch after calling Pyth Entropy, but the only callee is the fixed, trusted Entropy, which never calls back during `requestV2`; three tests with an Entropy that does reenter show it gains nothing (the caller pays one fee, AgentNFT keeps no balance, an early or orphaned number is ignored, a refund reentry finds the batch pending). AgentNFT is unchanged, so its address and the owner's agent stay. Written up in evidence/p2-u1/SLITHER.md, which also corrects "immutable": Entropy is an upgradeable proxy at a fixed address.
- Step 0, Q-46 (60c9b98, D-231): the admin adds a beta depositor at once (`addDepositor`) and removes one at once; raising either cap still waits 9 days, and a test shows an added depositor is still held to both caps. The factory's local address moved.
- Step 0, L-100 (6fd19c4): every devenv fork function takes its URL as a required argument; a test fails if any source regains a default, and a mutation proved it. The console now chooses its fork once (`consoleForkUrl`, following LOCAL_FORK_PORT).
- Contracts (058253d, 3332888, D-232, D-233): `OracleAdapter` (staleness strict per feed, decimals, sign, range, incomplete and future rounds, reverting or short feeds, the exact 2% pool rule from StateView, the 1% depeg guard, never-reverting views for tools); the core reads the price once per transaction, checks the pool on trades, records the value per unit on poke and after trades, refuses new risk in an unpoked drawdown, and resets the peak on the owner's unpause; PersonalAccount mints and burns internal units, and a withdrawal reads no oracle (it values what left at the last price). Every deposit now needs the oracle for the depeg guard (D-228 updated).
- Policy parity (2db1e50): `readFeed`, `readPool`, `poolDeviation`, `usdcPegReason`, the `breaker` model, `ORACLE_REASONS` and the intent-code map. `pnpm policy:parity` writes a fixture of 62 feed, 52 pool and 28 peg cases and 32 breaker sequences (801 steps reaching all three modes); vitest checks the file is what the package answers now, forge replays it on the contracts. Mutations on each side (the burn's rounding in TypeScript, the staleness comparison in Solidity) each failed a test.
- Tests: 25 adapter unit tests; 31 breaker tests on the real adapter (exact 10% and 20%, the 7-day window, flows never lowering the value per unit under fuzzing, a model-checked fuzz of price moves, who may loosen, the sentinel only tightening, 3% pool moves and stale feeds blocking trades but never withdrawals); the P2-U1 suites updated for deposits needing the oracle and for `poke` under reentrancy; 7 fork tests on the real feeds and pool at the pinned block (exact prices, staleness at the bounds, mocked invalid, depegged and moved answers, real USDC and WMON deposited at the real price, the breaker tripped, withdrawal with every feed down).
- Deployment and tools (53ddeef, D-234): `pnpm deploy:account-factory` also deploys the adapter (CREATE2, 0x5377...6161 locally) and proposes it to a new factory through the timelock; address book entry `oracle_adapter`. `pnpm oracle:local prices|demo` runs on a fork of its own (8548); `pnpm custody:local prices` and `prepare-oracle` (explicit: moves that fork's clock 9 days and swaps in settable feeds). The launch pool ID is in packages/domain (`UNISWAP_V4_MON_USDC_POOL`).
- Slither and gas (33da01b): 79 results, 19 AgentNFT's (unchanged), 60 justified in evidence/p2-u3/SLITHER.md, none needing a code change (the High is `day % 8` indexing, not randomness). Gas in evidence/p2-u3/GAS.md: real-token receipts USDC deposit 373,675, WMON deposit 336,978, poke 205,425 to 221,757, withdrawAll 283,120. PersonalAccount is 23,514 bytes, 1,062 under EIP-170.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck; 1,283 vitest tests (none skipped); forge 206 passed (fork suites excluded); test:fork 24 of 24 and the address book (one invocation failed both attempts on upstream fetch timeouts, L-75/L-91, the next passed at once); console build and web build check pass; console e2e 48 of 48 twice after re-baselining the address book captures for the new row (looked at both widths first, 8b19148); web e2e 150 passed with the 2 desktop-only skips. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped, after checking memory. The web and orchestrator live suites were not run (no web or orchestrator change).
- Dev state: I lost the playtest fork's state and rebuilt it (L-106). Stopping dev:all with `pnpm dev:down` also stopped anvil, whose state lives only in memory. Postgres and Redis kept their volumes. Rebuilt: AgentNFT at 0x60ca...Be6E (block 109670001, as before), agent #1 minted to 0x683e...5f76 (block 109670002, as before, sent as that address by impersonation on the fork) and revealed by the keeper as the bee (species 14); the indexer rewound and the orchestrator reprovisioned it (generation 4). Differences from before: the head is 109670005, not 109670010 (P2-U1's five repair blocks had no lasting effect); the wallet's fork MON balance is 99.97 (the mint helper sets 100 MON for gas); its fork nonce is 1, as before. No custody contract is on the playtest fork. dev:all is running. No other fork, worktree or process is left.
Notes for the owner:
- Q-47 needs a decision before PB-U1: the factory starts with no oracle and no Executor and each waits 9 days, so a mainnet beta can take its first deposit 9 days after deployment at the earliest.
- A-34 sets the depeg bound at 1%; the plan named no value.
- Deposits now need the oracle. On the playtest fork that means `pnpm custody:local prepare-oracle`, which moves that fork's clock 9 days; P2-U7 will need the same for its UI, or a keeper that keeps the settable feeds fresh.
Suggestions:
- P2-U7: refresh the local fork's settable feeds on a timer (the orchestrator's keeper or a console button), since they go stale 5 minutes after each refresh.
- P2-U2: the Executor should read `tradablePriceE18` once and poke after trades as the core does; the policy `breaker.breakerModeNow` is the pre-check for an unpoked drawdown.
- `pnpm dev:down` could refuse while dev:all runs, or say that it stops the playtest fork, and dev:up could keep an anvil state dump to restore from (L-106).
- The anvil fork on 8548 stopped answering under a forge gas report's burst of requests; worth measuring before relying on `forge --gas-report` against a local fork.
Bugs: L-104, L-105, L-106, L-107.
Commit: ed0a55a, 60c9b98, 6fd19c4, 058253d, 599e7ba, 3332888, 2db1e50, 5a485f9, 53ddeef, 817f2d7, 33da01b, 8b19148, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-07, P2-U2, Executor and venue adapter (with Step 0)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the signer (P2-U4), chain tools (P2-U5), the trade flow and arming (P2-U6), UI (P2-U7), and any testnet or mainnet deployment.
Summary: Accounts can now trade, and only through the Executor. It takes typed swap intents from the session key the agent's owner registered, checks every launch hard limit in a fixed order and names the one a trade breaks, and swaps through a code-pinned venue adapter that pays the account directly in the same transaction. Real swaps run both ways on the real Uniswap v4 MON/USDC pool on the fork, and packages/policy gives the same verdict as the contract on 195 shared cases.
- Step 0, Q-47 (2eba029, D-235): AccountFactory's constructor takes the oracle and the Executor, so a deployment takes deposits and trades at once; later changes still wait 9 days. The deploy order is oracle adapter, Executor, then the factory. Tests cover both paths.
- Step 0, A-34 (727b048, D-236): the 1% depeg bound is accepted and recorded.
- Step 0, fresh local feeds (58e5445, D-237): `LocalFeed` (never deployed anywhere) is put at the two Chainlink feed addresses with anvil-only methods, and their last answers are re-dated into storage with no transaction. devenv refuses any RPC but the local anvil fork before sending anything (tests: a remote URL, a local non-anvil node, an anvil on chain 143), the orchestrator runs it every minute in the local environment only (null for testnet and beta), and a forge test fails if devenv's copy of the code drifts from the build. The scripts use it instead of P2-U3's test mock, and `custody:local prepare-oracle` (which moved the fork's clock 9 days) became `refresh-feeds`.
- Contracts (df3f27e, 78678ad, D-238, D-239, A-35): `Executor` (session grants keyed to both epochs, a 30-day maximum, revoke and configuration bump; the order: global pause, intent shape, asset list, policy hash, session, epochs, account, replay, deadline, mode, venue, oracle, balance, unpoked drawdown, 10% size, 20 trades and 100% turnover in a 20-slot ring buffer, the oracle floor, and the 40% cap and USDC floor projected at the oracle price; after the fill, the value-loss bound and the cap and floor again; pulls exactly the input, keeps nothing, leaves no allowance; tighten and pause instant, loosen and unpause timelocked); `ProtocolRegistry` (adapter and venue code hashes, active, exit-only and paused); `RiskTimelock` shared by both; the Uniswap v4 MON/USDC 0.05% adapter (no approval at all, USDC straight to the account, WMON unwrapped and rewrapped) and the v3 USDC/WMON 0.3% fallback (exact approval reset to zero), registered paused. Adapters execute rather than build calldata, which v4's callback and the WMON wrap need (recorded in D-238). Four new rejection codes in packages/domain (d0c1d2d).
- Tests (9d8baae, 78678ad, fd8e118, df3f27e): 27 limit tests at, just under and just over each threshold (the 10% USDC floor cannot bind with two assets while the 40% cap holds, so it is tested at a stricter floor); 14 session, epoch and registry tests (a grant never revives when the agent returns); 10 safety tests (a venue paying elsewhere, paying the Executor, paying less, leaving an allowance, reentering the Executor, withdrawing from the account, refunding unused input); a fuzz test of the rolling window over random sequences; an invariant suite (48 runs, depth 60) proving the account holds exactly its flows and measured trade deltas, the Executor and venue keep nothing, no allowance is left, and every executed trade obeyed every limit, with a probe and mutations (the 40% cap and the trade count) that fail it; fork tests swapping both ways through each adapter and through the whole stack on the real v4 pool.
- Policy parity (6591b8a): `executorVerdict` mirrors the order and arithmetic; `pnpm policy:parity` writes `executor-parity.json` (195 cases: every reachable reason and 52 trades that go through); vitest checks it is what the package answers now, forge replays it on the real Executor, account and oracle adapter, and the TypeScript and Solidity policy hashes match. Mutations on each side failed a test.
- Deployment and tools (f36526c, ef549af): `pnpm deploy:account-factory` deploys the oracle adapter, the Executor, both venue adapters, the registry and the factory in order and binds the Executor, asserting each; the address book gains `executor`, `protocol_registry` and the two venues, and the factory, implementation and oracle adapter moved (local only). `pnpm executor:local demo` swaps on the real pool on a fork of its own and prints the reason for each broken limit.
- Slither and gas (b2f2c10): 122 results, 40 new, each justified in evidence/p2-u2/SLITHER.md (the High is the Executor's own kept-nothing balance check), none needing a change. Real swaps cost 951,458 and 1,036,200 gas (receipts); the Executor is 28,590 bytes, under Monad's limit and over EIP-170 (no optimizer, Foundry's default).
- Also fixed: test:fork stalled anvil by blocking the process that drained its log (L-109, f36526c); it now passes 32 of 32 with no retry, three runs in a row.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck; 1,298 vitest tests (none skipped); forge 267 passed (fork suites excluded); test:fork 32 of 32 and the address book, three times; console and web builds; console e2e 48 of 48 twice and web e2e 150 passed with the 2 desktop-only skips twice, after re-baselining the address book and /design captures for the new rows and reason codes (each looked at at both widths, 79af99f, b4643a4); the orchestrator live check 25 of 25 (report in evidence/p1-u7). Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all's services stopped, after checking memory. The web live suites were not run (no web change beyond the reason-code list).
- Dev state: the playtest fork is as found: head 109670005, agent #1 the bee, the Chainlink feeds still their original code, no custody or Executor contract on it. dev:all was running at the start; I stopped its services by signalling the supervisor (L-85, never dev:down, L-106) and left them stopped, because starting them with this unit's orchestrator turns on the feed refresher, which puts LocalFeed at the two feed addresses on the playtest fork (D-237, what the owner asked for). anvil, Postgres, Redis and LiteLLM keep running. No test fork, held fork or worktree is left.
Notes for the owner:
- Start the stack with `pnpm dev:all`. From then on the orchestrator keeps the playtest fork's feeds fresh, so deposits and trades pass the oracle checks there.
- To try the Executor: `pnpm executor:local demo` (its own fork; about two minutes).
- A-35 sets a session grant's maximum at 30 days; the plan named none.
Suggestions:
- Gas: a swap is about 1 million gas. The 20-slot ring buffer and the three oracle reads are the places to save; enabling the optimizer would save more but moves every deterministic address.
- The v3 fallback's trades check the 2% rule against the v4 pool; if it is ever activated, it should check its own pool.
- P2-U4: set a swap's gas limit near 1.1 million, since Monad bills the limit.
- P2-U7: the deposit UI can rely on fresh local feeds under dev:all; `pnpm custody:local refresh-feeds` does it by hand.
Bugs: L-108, L-109, L-110.
Commit: 2eba029, 727b048, 58e5445, df3f27e, 9d8baae, 78678ad, fd8e118, d0c1d2d, 6591b8a, f36526c, ef549af, b2f2c10, 34fd7d1, 79af99f, b4643a4, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-07, P2-U4, Signer and ledger (with Step 0)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: chain tools (P2-U5), the trade flow and arming UI (P2-U6), the portfolio page (P2-U7), real KMS credentials, per-tier gas caps, and any testnet or mainnet deployment.
Summary: Agents can now trade through a signer of their own. Each agent's session key (its funding address) lives behind a key interface and never leaves it; the signer signs only the Executor's swap for the pinned chain, follows every transaction through a Postgres outbox with fenced nonces, treats a lost answer as unknown and resolves it without sending again, and writes a trade to the ledger only after the Executor's event and the account's balances agree. A swap goes through the signer, the Executor and the real Uniswap v4 pool on a fork of its own, and the console's new Trades page does the same on the playtest fork when the owner presses its buttons.
- Step 0 (db753b8, D-240 to D-242, A-36, A-37): 30-day personal grants with a renewal reminder (P2-U6, P2-U7); vault grants without a fixed expiry (P7-U1, P7-U2); refunds follow contributions. Built here (2262c59, 3508fee): contributions are read per sender from the indexed transfers into the funding address; the owner's refund pays only the owner's own share, and each refund records the contributions it consumed so no other contributor's share moves (migration 0007). The summary API gives the owner's refundable share, and My Agents says "your own share" and disables the button when the owner contributed nothing. Unstaking, the cooldown, the proportional payout and "Fund this agent" are assigned to P8-U3, P7-U1, P5-U1 and P7-U4.
- Signer (ce88539, 6cdcf43, D-243 to D-246, A-38): packages/signer. Keys: a local HD provider (the funding seed, the funding path) and an AWS KMS secp256k1 provider tested against a mock that signs like KMS (DER, high s, no recovery ID); KMS is required before the beta (D-244). Policy: only the Executor's swap, no value, no deploy, the intent's chain and agent the key's, chain pins 143143, 10143 and 143, a 1.1M gas limit and fee caps; refusals are recorded with a code before anything is signed. Outbox (migration 0008): accepted, signed, submitted, unknown, confirmed, reconciled, failed; the nonce is allocated with the signed bytes in one database transaction under a per-key writer fence; one transaction in flight per key; a refused broadcast gives its nonce back. The swap is simulated first, so an Executor refusal is recorded with its reason and nothing is signed; a revert on chain has its reason fetched. Reconciliation compares the IntentExecuted event with the account's balance changes across the block and writes a balanced trade entry in the same database transaction. Two providers: receipts and logs to MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY first, failover both ways, both must answer the pinned chain.
- Orchestrator (188c8f4): the signer runs as a worker on the fork and testnet (off on the beta until KMS), the session key equals the funding address (tested), and internal routes serve session keys, the outbox, ledger entries and local test swaps. The live check now funds agents from the owner's wallet, since a mint straight to the funding address has no contributor to refund, and checks the signer runs (42f9286).
- Dev commands (0c34420): `pnpm signer:local demo` (or one step: create-key, register-grant, swap, break-limit, timeout) on a fork of its own (8553) and a throwaway database. Its run: a 5 USDC buy went accepted, signed, submitted, confirmed, reconciled, with the ledger entry printed; each breakable limit refused with its code (SLIPPAGE_TOO_HIGH, TRADE_SIZE_EXCEEDED, DEADLINE_TOO_FAR, DEADLINE_EXPIRED, TARGET_NOT_ALLOWED, CHAIN_NOT_PINNED); a lost broadcast resolved by hash with one send.
- Console Trades page (b2bfe6a, 8e6efcf, f6d3547): the transaction states and the signer's reason codes went into packages/domain, StatusPill, ReasonMessage and /design first. The page opens a chosen wallet's PersonalAccount for its agent, funds it with test USDC and registers the signer's key as the grant (one button each, by impersonation, fork only), sends a test swap or one that breaks a chosen limit, and shows the live outbox with each state, the hash, the reason code, the balances before and after (read from the chain at reconciliation) and the reconciled ledger entry. Nothing changes the fork until a button is pressed.
- Tests: signer unit tests (keys, KMS mock, policy and chain pin on all three environments, provider failover) and 20 outbox tests on Postgres and a fake chain (every state; refusals; Executor refusal unsigned; revert reason; refused broadcast with no gap; unknown resolved by receipt, by a node holding it, by a used nonce after a grace, and as dropped, never resent; a crash between signing and broadcast; nonces across restarts and a lagging provider; one per key; the writer fence; duplicate actions; reconciliation mismatches flagged with no ledger entry; no key, seed or RPC URL in logs, errors, rows or printed objects). Six fork tests through the real v4 pool (key and grant, buy and sell reconciled into the ledger, Executor and signer refusals, a lost answer resolved with one send, nonces across a restart). Orchestrator worker and route tests, devenv and console helper tests, console e2e for the Trades page with a screenshot.
- Also fixed: the full vitest suite started five cold forks at once and three timed out; files that start their own fork now run one at a time after the rest (946ed7e, L-115).
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck; 1,393 vitest tests (none skipped, the fork files included); forge 267 passed (fork suites excluded); test:fork 32 of 32 and the address book; console and web builds; console e2e 54 of 54 and web e2e 150 passed with the 2 desktop-only skips, each run once to re-baseline (the new nav link moved every console capture; /design gained the transaction states and signer reasons; the refund dialog's text changed) and once more to confirm, after looking at the new captures at both widths; the orchestrator live check 26 of 26 (report in evidence/p1-u7). Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all's services stopped, after checking memory.
- Dev state: the playtest fork is as found: head 109670005, the Chainlink feeds at their original code, no custody contract on it. dev:all was stopped at the start of this unit and is still stopped. No test fork, worktree or process is left.
Notes for the owner:
- A-38 sets a swap's gas limit at 1.1M and fee caps of 500 gwei per gas and 10 gwei of tip; the plan named none.
- D-243 makes the session key the funding address, as FINAL_PLAN 4.2.3 says; refunds still sign through the orchestrator's own copy of that key and share the agent's lock with the signer.
- The Trades page needs the trading stack on the playtest fork (`pnpm deploy:account-factory` against it), and the orchestrator running (`pnpm dev:all`).
Suggestions:
- Move refunds and credit settlements into the signer's outbox as their own transaction kinds (with their allowlist entries), so one writer owns every nonce of a funding address.
- P2-U5: build intents through the chain tools and hand them to `Signer.submitSwap`; the console's test-swap builder is a stand-in.
- P2-U6: show the outbox states and the signer's reason codes with the StatusPill and ReasonMessage renderings added here.
- PB-U1: bind the KMS provider to the AWS SDK, record each agent's KMS key ID in signer_keys, and add a replacement policy for a stuck transaction (a same-nonce replacement, never a new nonce).
- The console's mobile outbox table scrolls sideways inside its card; a stacked layout for narrow screens would read better.
Bugs: L-111, L-112, L-113, L-114, L-115, L-116, L-117.
Commit: db753b8, 2262c59, 3508fee, ce88539, 6cdcf43, 5ad0f1f, 554be1e, 659dc8c, 188c8f4, 0c34420, b2bfe6a, 8e6efcf, 0e30fe6, f6d3547, 22073a8, e0cfe58, 946ed7e, 42f9286, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-07, P2-U5, Chain tools (with Step 0)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: arming, approval and sending intents (P2-U6), the portfolio page (P2-U7), P2-EC deployments, templates and goals (Phase 3), and any testnet or mainnet transaction. The FINAL_PLAN 4.4.2 tools the prompt did not list (`whoami`, `get_assets`, `read_contract`, `balance`, `get_code`, `get_pool_depth`, `simulate_rebalance`, `propose_rebalance`, `list_intents`, `cancel_intent`) stay with W-2, and so does the reservation of a trade slot at `pending`.
Summary: Agents can now see their trading account and the market and propose trades. A chain tools server, hosted by the orchestrator behind the gate and in every tier's Hermes configuration, answers seven tools from the environment's own address book; `tradable_now` and `propose_swap` give every rule that blocks a trade, each with the Executor's own reason code and when it clears, and a passing proposal becomes an intent that waits for approval and never carries calldata. A real agent in E2B used all of it end to end. Step 0 recorded the owner's P2-EC answers, generated the P2-EC keys, moved refunds and settlements into the signer's outbox, and stacked the console's outbox on phones.
- Step 0, decisions (72c3e35): D-257 to D-260 record Q-48 (seed our own testnet pool), Q-49 (a 0.45 USDC canary trade), Q-50 (a fresh canary wallet) and Q-51 (no founder reserve for now).
- Step 0, keys (no commit: `.env` is gitignored): ten fresh keys appended to `.env` under new names so the local values stay untouched: `TESTNET_DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TESTNET_GUARDIAN_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TESTNET_SENTINEL_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TESTNET_CLAIM_SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TESTNET_REVEAL_KEEPER_PRIVATE_KEY`, `TESTNET_FUNDING_ADDRESS_SEED`, `TESTNET_FEED_PRIVATE_KEY`, `CANARY_OWNER_PRIVATE_KEY`, `CANARY_GUARDIAN_PRIVATE_KEY`, `CANARY_SESSION_PRIVATE_KEY`, each from Node's CSPRNG, checked against every key already in `.env`; only two public addresses were printed. P2-EC wires them into packages/config (D-254), since the existing testnet claim, keeper and seed variables share their names with local.
- Step 0, refunds and settlements through the signer (4b5205b, 8dc05fb, D-261): the outbox gains `usdc_refund` (to the agent's current owner only, read at acceptance and again at signing) and `usdc_settlement` (to `PLATFORM_TREASURY_ADDRESS` only), migration 0009, the codes RECIPIENT_NOT_ALLOWED and TRANSFER_REVERTED, kind-neutral signer messages, and reconciliation by the Transfer event. The refund service writes its ledger entry and the outbox row in one database transaction and follows the row. The settlement sweep itself stays with W-1.
- Step 0, the outbox on phones (496f5bc): the design system's Table gains `stack` (rows become blocks below 640px, each cell under its column's name), on /design first; the console outbox uses it and names refund rows; its e2e test checks the region never overflows.
- Policy (9c66525): `executorBlockers` lists every rule a trade breaks in the Executor's order; its first answer equals `executorPreCheck` on every shared fixture case.
- Chain tools (7392944, 3f77090, D-262, A-42 to A-44): `services/chain-tools` with `get_portfolio`, `get_prices`, `get_quote`, `get_limits`, `tradable_now` (new in the registry), `propose_swap` and `get_intent_status`. Addresses come from the address book, the pool from the venue adapter on chain, quotes from the v4 Quoter; nothing environment-specific is in the code. Reads are free, logged and limited to 60 calls per run; market data and quotes are reused for 3 seconds. Intents (migration 0010, `platform.intents`): idempotent within a run, at most 3 waiting per agent, expiring after 30 minutes, kept as `rejected` with every code when a pre-check fails. The orchestrator routes `/mcp/chain` through the gate, adds the chain server to every tier's Hermes config, narrates each proposal under the number validator, expires intents every 30 seconds, and runs a new `chain_check` task.
- Console (ccf6b71, D-263): intent states in packages/domain, StatusPill and /design first; the agents panel runs the chain check and shows its result, the latest portfolio reading and the intents with their states and reasons in a stacked table.
- Tests: 19 chain tools unit tests (every tool, every blocker and when it clears, identity from the token with a forged header, rate limit, idempotency, expiry, open limit, no calldata, not deployed); 6 orchestrator tests on Postgres behind the real gate (logging, intents per agent, the narrator's entry with a rejected invented number, the open limit and expiry, the call limit, the database's intent states); signer transfer tests (8) and policy tests; 12 refund tests on a real Signer; console unit and e2e tests with captures. A fork test on its own fork (8555, 888be44) runs every read tool on the real stack and holds the pre-check's first reason to the real Executor's for size, balance, reduce-only, paused, a stale price, a 3% pool move and a full 24-hour window filled by 20 real swaps; it ran twice. The orchestrator live check gains the chain check (566a1f6): a real agent in E2B read its portfolio, prices, limits and `tradable_now` and proposed a 1 USDC buy that waits for approval, with a narrated activity entry and no swap sent.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-118 (a missing price read as an invalid intent; found by the fork test), L-120 (a refund waited forever for a session key only the console creates; found by the live check, b77fe72), L-122 (a USDC refund ran out of gas under Monad's gas model: about 100,300 gas against a 100,000 limit, now 150,000 with a real-token fork test, 14c737b; found by the live check). Process slips: L-119 (test slips, and a re-edit committed after the scan), L-121 (b77fe72 went in while typecheck failed; 6cf9f1e fixes it, so b77fe72 alone does not typecheck). One more rule slip: I used `git restore --staged LESSONS.md` to unstage a file; it only unstaged and discarded nothing, but CLAUDE.md lists `git restore` among the commands not to run.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck; the full vitest run, 1,465 tests in 102 files (none skipped, the fork files included); the console build and the web build check; console e2e 56 of 56 and web e2e 150 passed with the 2 desktop-only skips, each once to re-baseline (looked at at both widths) and once more to confirm with no change; the orchestrator live check 29 of 29 on its third run (the first two found L-120 and L-122; all three reports in evidence/p1-u7). Not run: forge and `pnpm test:fork`, since no contract changed. Heavy suites ran one at a time with no dev server, after checking memory.
- Dev state: the playtest fork is as found (head 109670015, agent #1 owned by 0x683e...5f76, AgentNFT and AccountFactory deployed, LocalFeed at the MON/USD address); nothing in this unit sent to 8545. dev:all was not running and was not started. The development database gets migrations 0009 and 0010 on the next orchestrator start. No test fork, worktree or sandbox is left.
Notes for the owner:

## 2026-10-07, P2-U6, Trade flow (with Step 0)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the portfolio page and its arming card (P2-U7; the web's arming flow module is ready for it), templates and goals, the settlement sweep, P2-EC deployments, and any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: Agent proposals now become real trades. The owner arms an agent once from their own wallet, with a session grant to its funding address for at most 30 days, then approves its first proposed trade. After that, proposals within the hard limits are approved on their own, re-checked at submission, sent through the signer and the Executor, and settled only after reconciliation. Arming ends by itself on expiry, a sale, a configuration change or a revoke, and at once when the owner disarms. Every blocked trade is stored with its reasons, narrated, and served as "why the agent did not trade". A real agent in E2B did all of it on a fork against the real v4 pool.
- Step 0 (76ac2c2): CLAUDE.md allows `git restore --staged`; discarding work stays forbidden.
- States first (6c48e1c, D-265): packages/domain's intent states gain confirmed and reconciled (settled is split; migration 0011 maps old rows), plus arming states, arming end reasons and the trade flow's reason codes; StatusPill, ReasonMessage and /design show them. Migration 0011 adds the intents' trade columns (blockers, approval, submission, minimum out, deadline, action ID, amount out, failure) and `platform.arming`, with at most one open arming per agent.
- Chain tools (ad98910): waiting, approved and submitted intents reserve trade slots; a passing proposal that lacks only the session grant waits for the owner instead of being rejected; blockers have their own column; `assessTrade` is exported so submission re-runs the same checks. The narrator gains arming, trade and blocked entries under the same number validator.
- packages/trading (d669b0f, 7a217a6, D-264, A-45): the pure arming rules (every way arming ends, the grant that may arm an agent, the renewal reminder), the store for arming and intent moves (each move conditional on the state it leaves), the owner's actions (confirm arming, approve, disarm, and the grant and revoke wallet calls), JSON views, and a chain reader for the control API.
- Trade flow worker (45f2b81, 06c1391, D-266, D-267, A-46, A-47): every 2 seconds it ends, renews or reminds open armings from a fresh chain read; approves an armed agent's waiting intents; re-runs every pre-check at submission (reserved slots for older and sent intents, the intent's epochs, the funding address's MON against a swap's most gas cost; the fork tops gas up); builds the minimum output from a fresh quote within the slippage limit, never under the oracle floor, with the deadline set then; sends the swap through the signer with an action ID derived from the intent; and follows the outbox to confirmed, then reconciled, waiting for the finalized tag off the fork.
- Control API (7a217a6, D-268): owner routes tied to the owner session and ownership epoch: GET and POST `/v1/agents/:id/arming` (the grant call, then the on-chain check and record), POST `/disarm` (with the revoke call), GET `/intents`, POST `/intents/:intentId/approve`; and the public GET `/why-not-traded`.
- Web (d1b9b00): `runArm` and `runDisarm` in apps/web/src/agent/arming-flow.ts drive the owner's wallet: network guard, the API's call, the receipt, then the API's record. The card that uses them is P2-U7.
- Dev console (34f9061, 3f2dd7c, af6d76b, 4e48985): local-only arm (the owner's grant impersonated on the fork), disarm (with the revoke) and approve routes in the orchestrator; on the agents page, the arming state with Arm and Disarm, an Approve (or "Approve and arm") button on a waiting intent, a local-only "Propose over-limit trade" that records a proposal of half the account's USDC (d8ad22b), intents through every state with what each got or why it was blocked and who clears it, refreshing while any intent is moving.
- Tests: trading package 14 (pure arming rules; the store on Postgres); trade flow worker 12 on Postgres (the first approval arms; automatic execution when armed; the swap built from fresh reads; re-checks at submission; a pre-sale intent refused; slot reservation and release; GAS_UNFUNDED and the fork's top-up; not armed at submission and a signer refusal; settlement only after reconciliation and finality; a failed send; arming ends on expiry, sale, configuration change and revoke; disarm and the revoke landing; the renewal reminder and a renewal); control API 6 (the grant call, the on-chain check and renewal, approval and arming, disarm, every owner route refused to another wallet and to a stale session, why-not-traded); orchestrator API, chain tools, narrator, web flow (7) and console unit tests. A fork test on its own fork (8556; 1eea46d, cf7f2c3) arms, approves, executes on the real v4 pool and settles, trades on its own once armed, refuses an over-limit and a no-gas trade with nothing sent, and ends arming on the real chain for each reason. The live check (1eea46d, e48d6a5, f7d7c9a): the owner arms; a real agent's proposal waits; the owner's approval arms it; the swap executes through the signer, the Executor and the real v4 pool and settles; trade and arming entries pass the validator; an over-limit proposal is blocked at submission with its reason, served by why-not-traded.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-123 (the submission slot check let no queued trade take the last slots; found by the Postgres test), L-126 (the agent's first real trade was refused because its session key was never recorded; found by the live check), L-127 (the live check raced the scheduler, and a real agent proposed its whole account; found by the live check). Process and test slips: L-125 (a role query matched "Disarm", a row type and two type aliases caught by the gate, a sale simulated without the escrow, and formatting with the wrong tool). L-124: for about an hour the fork's upstream served no copy of the pinned block, and I first recorded that as permanent; it came back (C-80, Q-52).
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck and the secrets scan; vitest 1,489 tests in 100 files outside the fork project, plus the chain tools (8) and trade flow (3, run twice) fork files; the console build and the web build check; console e2e 58 of 58 (re-baselined and confirmed again after d8ad22b) and web e2e 150 with the 2 desktop-only skips, each re-baselined (captures looked at at both widths) and confirmed with no change; the orchestrator live check 34 of 34 on its fourth run (the first three found L-126, L-127 and L-127; all four reports in evidence/p1-u7). Not run: the rest of the fork project and forge, since no contract changed. Heavy suites ran one at a time with no dev server, after checking memory.
- Dev state: the playtest fork is as found (head 109670015); nothing in this unit sent to 8545. dev:all was not running and was not started. The development database gets migration 0011 on the next orchestrator start. No test fork, worktree or sandbox is left.
Notes for the owner:
- To try it: `pnpm dev:all`, deploy the trading stack on the playtest fork and fund agent #1's PersonalAccount (the console's Trades page), then on the Agents page: Run chain check, Arm, "Approve and arm" on the waiting intent, and watch it go approved, submitted, confirmed, settled. Then press "Propose over-limit trade": the armed agent's proposal is approved on its own and refused at submission with TRADE_SIZE_EXCEEDED; read why in the intents table or at `GET /v1/agents/1/why-not-traded` on the control API. Disarm revokes the grant.
- Q-52: a second, archive-capable RPC as `MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY` would ride out outages like L-124's; forks already alternate to it.
Suggestions:
- P2-U7: the portfolio page's arming card on `runArm` and `runDisarm`, with the renewal reminder and why-not-traded.
- Retry a trade refused at submission when every reason clears by waiting (A-46 makes refusals final for now).
- Prune the trade flow's in-memory list of narrated keys (it grows by a few keys per intent in a long-running process).
- `orchestrator.intents()` (chain-store's list) is now used only by the live check; fold it into the trade store.
Bugs: L-123, L-124, L-125, L-126, L-127.
Commit: 76ac2c2, 6c48e1c, ad98910, d669b0f, 45f2b81, 7a217a6, 34f9061, d1b9b00, 3f2dd7c, 1eea46d, af6d76b, 4e48985, cf7f2c3, be7ed3d, 06c1391, e48d6a5, f7d7c9a, d8ad22b, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-08, P2-U7, Portfolio page
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: P2-EC deployments, strategy templates and goals (Phase 3), trade history beyond the recent list, profit and loss charts, the vault, and any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: The owner manages the agent's trading money from the app with their own wallet. Each agent's card on My Agents shows its trading account's value and links to its portfolio, where the owner opens the account, deposits with an exact approval, sees positions and the account's health, arms the agent, approves or rejects its proposals, watches its trades settle, reads why it did not trade, and withdraws at any time, including while paused. Every deposit block is named before anything is sent. Another wallet sees none of it.
- Backend (30babef, aff51c2, D-269, D-271, D-272): `GET /v1/agents/:id/portfolio` (owner only) reads the chain fresh through a new `rpcPortfolioReader` in packages/trading: the account or where it will be created, free balances, claimable credits, mode, breaker and 7-day peak, allowlist and caps, the oracle's prices with their age, and the wallet's balances. `POST /v1/agents/:id/intents/:intentId/reject` cancels a waiting intent and frees its slot. The arming route now dates the grant from the chain's clock (L-129, a P2-U6 bug the live run found). `pnpm custody:allow 0xWallet` adds a wallet to the local fork's deposit allowlist in one command (D-231).
- Web, pure layer (da762ca, D-270): the custody ABIs with every revert in the owner's words; positions, shares, price age and cap room; every deposit block checked before sending; a step-by-step wallet transaction flow (checking, waiting in the wallet, confirming, confirmed, declined, failed) used by every action.
- Design system first (3e6f103): `PositionsPanel`, `CapsPanel`, `ArmingCard`, `ApprovalCard` (every financial field from the intent and the address book), `RecentTrades`, `WhyNotTraded`, `WalletActionStatus`, and the trading account in `FundAgentPanel`, all on /design in every state.
- The page (cd1eadb, cd64b2b, A-48): `/agents/:id/portfolio`, with the owner session shared with the card (`useOwnerSession`), `usePortfolio` re-reading every 5 seconds (2 while something moves), and arming through P2-U6's `runArm` and `runDisarm`.
- Tests: 16 web unit tests (positions, every deposit block, withdrawals in every mode, credits, amounts; the wallet flow's every state and the contract's revert named); 8 ui component tests; control API tests for the portfolio (owner only, no read for another wallet, not deployed), reject, and the grant dated by chain time; a store test for reject. The portfolio e2e suite (20 tests at 1440px and 380px) runs on a fake trading backend that follows the mock wallet's own transactions: six states captured and scanned with axe, exact approvals decoded from the sent calls, the caps and the depeg guard named with nothing sent, a declined deposit, a withdrawal while paused with no price, a claim, the arming lifecycle with renewal, reject and disarm, another wallet refused, and the card's link. The live run (`pnpm test:web:live:portfolio`, its own fork on 8546 with the trading contracts, real orchestrator, LiteLLM and E2B): mint, the allowlist notice, `custody:allow`, open the account, deposit 20 USDC, arm, a real agent's chain check proposes, approve and arm, the swap settles on the real v4 pool, an over-limit proposal is blocked and explained, disarm, withdraw all USDC and WMON, and another wallet refused (passed on its second run; the first found L-129).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-129 (the grant's expiry from the wall clock; found by the live run), L-130 (buttons disabled while a trade settled, address checksums, the runner's flag, three visual slips found in the capture review). Process: L-128 (a new test file was committed before the secrets scan saw it; two public token addresses recorded as false positives).
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck and the secrets scan, staged; vitest 1,526 tests in 103 files outside the fork project; web e2e 170 with the 2 desktop-only skips and console e2e 58 of 58, the web suite re-baselined once (captures looked at at both widths) and confirmed with no change; the web build check (in the web suite) and the console build; the portfolio live run. Not run: the fork project and forge (no contract or chain code changed). Heavy suites ran one at a time with no dev server, after checking memory.
- Dev state: the playtest fork is as found (head 109670015); nothing in this unit sent to 8545. No test fork, worktree or sandbox is left.
Notes for the owner:
- Your wallet joins the local deposit allowlist with `pnpm custody:allow 0xYourWallet` (local fork only).
- The portfolio needs the trading stack on the playtest fork (`pnpm deploy:account-factory`), as P2-U5 and P2-U6 did.

- P5-U1 reuses `WhyNotTraded` on the agent profile page.

## 2026-10-08, Phase 2 local playtest and tuning (P2-T)

The portfolio opens with an allocation donut and summary tiles; cards show a compact trading summary. ## 2026-10-08, P2-EC part 1, Early chain check on Monad testnet (with Step 0)
Status: done (Monad testnet). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the mainnet canary (part 2, its own session), any mainnet transaction, Phase 3, and the MetaMask and OKX checks (the owner's playtest, next).
Summary: The platform ran on a real chain for the first time. Everything is deployed to Monad testnet as a labeled throwaway, verified on Sourcify, and the whole stack runs against it with one command. A test wallet went through the full flow there: mint, a real Pyth Entropy reveal, provisioning, credits, a Scan, an account, a deposit, arming, the agent's trade settled at the finalized tag on our pool, a blocked trade with its reasons, disarm and withdraw everything. Every real-chain checklist item is measured in evidence/p2-ec/REAL_CHAIN.md, and the six differences from the fork that needed a fix are fixed and recorded as lessons.
- Step 0 (1b401ae, 470ddd9, 7b75bfe): the owner's answers to Q-53 to Q-58 (D-297 to D-302), D-281, D-284 and D-292 accepted and P2-EC split in two (D-303); testnet's MON at an operator price of 1.00 USD (D-304), the lower deploy gas multiplier (D-306), and the lean budget of D-307 (feeds refreshed on demand, a 0.5 MON and 0.5 USDC pool, one reveal batch, one test agent).
- Preflight: chain 10143 at block 69,268,394 on the keyed RPC; all TESTNET_* keys set; the deployer funded to 15 MON and 20 USDC by the owner; every D-248 fact re-checked unchanged (code sizes, decimals, Entropy's provider and fee, the testnet PoolManager's v4 ABI).
- Contracts (b9db7a1): TestnetFeed and PoolSeeder as script contracts (testnet only, clean-room, settling by transfer and value), DeployTestnetMarket (feeds at 1.00 USD, the hookless MON/USDC 0.05% pool seeded about ±1%), and DeployScope: the fork keeps the v1 salts and testnet requires `p2ec.testnet` (D-249). DeployAccountFactory runs on testnet without the v3 fallback. `pnpm test:testnet-fork` runs the real scripts and a mint, deposit, both swaps, a refused and a stale trade and a withdrawal on forge's fork of testnet (4 of 4).
- Configuration (95279e2, 0e615f2): on testnet the claim signer, keeper, funding seed, second RPC and the two service secrets come only from their TESTNET_ variables; testnet refuses anvil's keys, a key equal to its local counterpart or to another role's, the local database and the local Redis database 0; a non-local web build needs CONTROL_API_URL and builds into .next-testnet; the wallet guard's fork hints show only on local; devenv's sendAs has its own guard (D-254).
- Deployment (bfde472, ff2e6bf, 261770e): `pnpm deploy:testnet` sent 13 transactions (4.15 MON with the pool seed) and every state assertion passed; `pnpm verify:testnet` verified all ten contracts as exact matches on Sourcify; the testnet address book records each contract's block, transaction, code hash and size and links (evidence/p2-ec/ADDRESSES.md). Code sizes equal the fork's.
- The stack on testnet (849904b, e330ff2, 04102f5): `pnpm testnet:up` (own database, Redis database 1, the local ports, refuses while dev:all runs, never touches anvil), `testnet:down`, `testnet:to-local`, `testnet:gas` (C-79) and `testnet:propose [--over-limit]`. TestnetFeeds are re-dated on demand before a market read, a submission or a deposit (a new owner-only `POST /v1/agents/:id/prices/fresh`). The chain check and the over-limit proposal became operator actions on local and testnet (D-311). Scheduled Scans are off on testnet.
- The flow (c2df44e): `pnpm testnet:e2e` logs the test wallet in to Privy by Sign-In with Ethereum and drives the real control API (D-313). Transactions: mint 0xf7f03e59...6c0d, Entropy request 0xeba06523...3de7, Pyth's callback 0x572bb5cc...1f16, reveal 0x54bcfe7c...8e0d, credits 0x483e9cd3...c516, account 0xa969c491...2ff5, approve 0xccf45186...aa9c, deposit 0xf7e41c82...857d, arm 0x0df2bc61...f8b0, the agent's swap 0x74d82a41...6ff8 (0.025 USDC for 0.02498 WMON, reconciled at finalized), revoke 0x50d0ad55...20a0, withdraw all 0x4b7d7611...57be. The over-limit proposal was refused with TRADE_SIZE_EXCEEDED and CONCENTRATION_CAP, nothing sent, and why-not-traded explains it. It passed on its third run; the first two found L-135 and L-137, and all three reports are kept.
- Measurements (c2df44e): finality over 14 transactions (receipt 0.6 to 1.0 s, safe 0.7 to 1.4 s, finalized 1.2 to 1.7 s, no receipt changed), block time 302 ms, no clock skew beyond whole-second timestamps, Entropy's callback 4 blocks (about 1 s) after the request, credit lag 3.7 s with the paced indexer, a nonce-gap send accepted and invisible, the keyed provider capped at 10-block eth_getLogs and rate-limited near 19 requests a second, the public RPC at 100 blocks. `pnpm testnet:local-only` tried all 17 local-only features on testnet: all refused.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-134 (the indexer kept asking for a refused range), L-135 (fork-speed polling rate-limited on testnet), L-136 (receipts report the gas limit; forge's record kept stale limits), L-137 (a per-start orchestrator secret made stored keys unreadable), L-138 (the swap's 1.1M limit left 3,000 gas on a first swap; now 1.3M, D-308), L-139 (the 0.05 MON warning did not cover one deposit; now 0.1 MON, D-309). Process: L-140 (slips in this unit's own scripts).
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan, staged; vitest 1,578 tests in 108 files and the fork project 33 of 33; forge 271 with the fork suites skipped, `pnpm test:fork` 32 of 32 with the local AgentNFT and AccountFactory at their unchanged v1 addresses and the address book matching; `pnpm test:testnet-fork` 4 of 4; web e2e 182 with the 2 desktop-only skips and no capture changed (the pages touched changed no pixels). Heavy suites ran one at a time with no dev server, after checking memory. Not run: console e2e (no console change).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 is as found (head 109670022, AgentNFT and agent #1 unchanged); the local database was only read. The testnet stack is stopped. On testnet the deployer holds 9.07 MON and 17.94 USDC, the test wallet 0.42 MON and its withdrawn USDC and WMON, agent 1's funding address about 0.39 MON. .env gained TESTNET_TEST_WALLET_PRIVATE_KEY, TESTNET_ORCHESTRATOR_SECRET and TESTNET_API_SESSION_SECRET (testnet only). The owner's Privy app has one throwaway test user for the test wallet.
Notes for the owner:
- To play: `pnpm testnet:up` (stop dev:all first with Ctrl-C), open http://localhost:3000 with your wallet on Monad Testnet (chain 10143, RPC https://testnet-rpc.monad.xyz). Your three playtest wallets are on the testnet mint allowlist and the AccountFactory allowlist. Each wallet needs testnet MON for gas (about 0.15) and Circle testnet USDC. After minting, run `pnpm testnet:gas <yourAgentId>` so the agent's session key can pay for swaps, and `pnpm testnet:propose <yourAgentId>` to have it propose a trade. Keep deposits near 0.5 USDC: the pool is tiny (D-307), so trades are about 0.05 USDC. `pnpm testnet:to-local` switches back to the fork.
- Q-59: the keyed testnet RPC is a free plan (10-block logs, a rate limit that stopped the stack once); a second provider is recommended before the Rehearsal and PB-U1.
Suggestions:
- Part 2 (the canary): reuse DeployScope with a `p2ec.canary` scope, chainTransaction for every receipt, and the 1.3M swap limit; budget 0.134 MON per swap.
- Log the HTTP status of RPC failures in the orchestrator (viem's "HTTP request failed" hid the 429s).
- The control API binds 0.0.0.0 off local; for a stack on the owner's machine, 127.0.0.1 would be safer until hosting is chosen (Q-37).
- The testnet feed job could follow the mainnet price for research realism (BUILD_PLAN Phase 3 gaps), within the budget.
- Phase 3's runner should keep the paced polling (D-310) for its 60-second loop's reads.
Bugs: L-134, L-135, L-136, L-137, L-138, L-139, L-140.
Commit: 1b401ae, 470ddd9, 7b75bfe, b9db7a1, 95279e2, bfde472, ff2e6bf, 261770e, 849904b, e330ff2, 57d1f55, 20cb18d, 04102f5, 7062c87, 350f772, 0e615f2, c2df44e, ce0ed42, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-08, P2-EC part 1 follow-up: the second RPC provider
Status: done. Out of scope: a second mainnet provider (none was added), the control API's reads (still one provider, suggested below).
Summary: The owner's second provider was a QuickNode endpoint that serves Monad testnet, saved under the mainnet fallback's name, so nothing used it. It is now the testnet fallback, every chain-facing service fails over to its chain's second provider, a provider on the wrong chain is refused, and Q-52 and Q-59 are closed with measurements.
- Found: of the three RPC entries in .env, MONAD_RPC_URL (Alchemy) serves 143, MONAD_TESTNET_RPC_URL (Alchemy) 10143, and MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY (QuickNode) 10143. It was renamed MONAD_TESTNET_RPC_URL_SECONDARY in .env (value untouched, no URL printed). MONAD_API_KEY is not an RPC entry.
- Measured (`pnpm rpc:probe`, ac255ca, evidence/rpc-providers.json): mainnet Alchemy archive, 10-block logs, no 429 at 19 a second; testnet Alchemy 10-block logs, no 429 at 19 a second, no state 9M blocks back; testnet QuickNode 5-block logs, 429 to about a fifth of requests at 19 a second (and 413 for logs over its range), state 9M back but not 39M. Alchemy stays the testnet primary and QuickNode is the fallback (D-314).
- Failover (68dfb5a): fork upstreams are chain-checked; the indexer, chain tools, keeper, feeds and refunds fail over through `rpcTransport` or the indexer's own fallback; the signer starts when only its secondary answers; the orchestrator refuses a fallback on another chain. Live with each primary blocked (evidence/rpc-failover.json): a fork start (36 s, through the mainnet provider used as second upstream), the chain tools' market and agent reads, the signer's chain check, nonce and fees, and the indexer (about 11 blocks a second) all worked through the second provider.
- Tests: upstream chain check, indexer failover (each failure kind, the one-minute return, no failover on a range refusal, both chains reported, no URL in errors), rpcTransport between two local servers, the signer's start with a down primary; 128 tests across the touched packages, format, lint, typecheck and the secrets scan.
- Decisions: D-314; Q-52 resolved (option B: one archive-capable mainnet provider), Q-59 resolved (option A: the second testnet provider). Lesson L-141.
Suggestions:
- Add a second mainnet provider as MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY before PB-U1; the chain check now guarantees it is used only if it serves 143.
- Give the control API's portfolio and arming reads the same fallback.
- Make the indexer's --once stop on SIGTERM.
Bugs: L-141.
Commit: 68dfb5a, ac255ca, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-08, P2-EC part 1 follow-up: the minted agent the app did not show
Status: done (testnet). The owner's agent 2 shows on /configure and /agents, and the reveal link opens it.
Summary: The owner minted agent 2 on testnet and the app offered "Mint an agent" again. On chain the mint and the reveal were fine; the index was hours behind because of the pacing added for the rate limit, and the pages trusted the index alone. The indexer now catches up fast, and the pages read the chain for anything the index has not seen, so a wallet that minted is never offered the mint.
- Diagnosis: agent 2 owned by 0x683e...5f76, revealed (species 5); the keeper requested at 17:18:21 and revealed at 17:18:27; the indexer had no errors, 429s or refused ranges but was about 5,000 blocks behind (5 blocks a second against the chain's 3.3, after an hour of downtime); the API's owner and minter lists were empty and /v1/agents/2 answered 404; /configure and /agents read only the index.
- Indexer (cbc31b8): steps every 0.4 s while more than a range behind the head, paced at 2 s only at the head; a 30-block lag test failed with the spacing ignored. Restarted on the live stack, it caught up at about 14 blocks a second and indexed agent 2.
- Web (7826a8a, 5d4e8e5): `owned-agents.ts` completes the index from the chain (totalMinted past the index's count, ownerOf, speciesOf, tbaOf, ownerEpoch, hasMinted; a contract revert only means "no such agent"); `useOwnedAgents` falls back to the index alone if the chain cannot be read; the mint flow watches the reveal on chain first; the design system gains `PendingAgentNotice` on /design; /configure shows it with the agent while indexing; /agents shows a light card for an agent the platform has no record of yet; a wallet whose minted agent left it sees "The agent this wallet minted is no longer in it", never the mint.
- Tests: 8 unit tests of the chain fallback (the testnet case, an unrevealed agent, a reveal the index has not seen, caught up with no chain scan, other wallets' agents, a wallet whose agent left it, the scan limit, a chain failure surfacing), the page-state and component tests, and e2e tests with a fake API whose index lags (the reveal link, /configure, /agents, catching up). Full web e2e 190 passed with the 2 desktop-only skips; the /design captures were re-baselined for the new specimen after looking at both widths, and a second run matched. Format, lint, typecheck and the secrets scan on every commit.
- Dev state: the testnet stack was stopped for the e2e suite (the Heavy work rule) and restarted; the API lists agent 2 for the owner's wallet.
Suggestions:
- Let the indexer report its lag (head minus watermark) on /health, and the app show it when large.
- Give the control API's single-agent and summary routes the same chain fallback, so owner controls work before indexing.
Bugs: L-142.
Commit: cbc31b8, 7826a8a, 5d4e8e5, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-08, P2-EC testnet tuning: every asset an agent holds

Summary: The owner sent testnet MON to one of agent 2's addresses and could not see it in the app. Pages showed only the balances they use; nothing read the token-bound account, MON at the funding address, or other tokens. Every agent now shows "All holdings": each of its three addresses in plain words, every balance there, what it does, and, for what does nothing, a plain statement and, where the owner can, a move to their wallet signed from the connected wallet (D-315).

Checked against testnet: agent 2's funding address 0.5 MON (gas) and 10 USDC (credits), token-bound account 3 MON and 5 USDC (movable), PersonalAccount 5 USDC (trading). - Tests (5ff031a): unit tests for the classification and the call (5), the route's owner-only, 503 and answer cases, the panel (5) and the hook's mapping, kept outcome, declined request and wrong network; e2e at 1440px and 380px for the /agents and portfolio panels with axe (no serious or critical violations), the owner's MON move (the decoded execute to the owner, then the line gone and "Moved MON to your wallet."), a declined wallet request (nothing sent, the button usable again) and another wallet (no panel, no holdings call answered). Full web e2e: 183 passed, 2 skipped (desktop-only), and 15 capture differences, all from the panel showing an error where the fixtures have no reader; after the fix the my-agents (28), portfolio (26), holdings (8) and design (10) specs passed, the last with its captures re-baselined for the new specimen after looking at both widths. ## 2026-10-08, P2-EC part 2, the mainnet canary
Status: done (Monad mainnet, real funds). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: AgentNFT, agents, sandboxes and the web app on mainnet, any user but the canary owner, and Phase 3.
Summary: Agent trading works on real Monad mainnet. A labeled throwaway canary of the trading contracts was deployed to chain 143, verified on Sourcify, and used with real funds: 5 USDC deposited, a 23.8-hour session grant, a 0.45 USDC buy of WMON and the sale back through the signer and the Executor on the real Uniswap v4 launch pool with the real Chainlink feeds, both reconciled into the canary ledger and settled at `finalized`; an oversized buy and a swap while paused refused before anything was signed; the guardian's pause, a withdrawal while paused, the grant revoked and everything withdrawn. The canary spent 2.883 MON (about $0.071) of its 10 MON budget and lost 0.000501 USDC to the round trip.
- Preflight (read only): MONAD_RPC_URL serves 143; no second mainnet provider; the canary owner held 40 MON and 5 USDC, the guardian and session key nothing; the three CANARY_ keys present and distinct, no EIP-7702 code on any. The owner chose to keep the 40 MON and treat 10 MON as a spending limit (D-316, amending D-252), funds left in the canary owner at the end, Sourcify only.
- Configuration (423e7f4): a fourth environment, `canary` (chain 143, label mainnet-canary, D-251): only a service that declares itself the canary runner may load it, so the orchestrator, indexer, control API, console and web app refuse it at start; signing needs CANARY_SIGNING_ENABLED=true (refused anywhere else); the canary keys are refused if they are anvil keys, shared between roles, or reused from local or testnet; the address book and the signer's chain pin know the canary.
- Contracts (78aac4b): CanaryAgent, a script contract with only IAgentNFTView, one agent owned by the canary owner, no ERC-721 surface, refusing any chain but 143 (D-250); DeployScope accepts 143 only with `DEPLOY_SALT_SCOPE=p2ec.canary` and CANARY_SIGNING_ENABLED=true; `pnpm test:canary-fork` runs the real canary scripts and the whole run on forge's in-memory fork of mainnet, so no local chain ever answers 143 (2 of 2, run before deploying and again before the run).
- Deploy path (346b966): `pnpm deploy:canary` re-checks the mainnet facts first (code sizes as recorded, decimals, both feeds in bounds, the launch pool initialized with liquidity at fee 500), checks the chain before each step and holds the deployment to 4 MON; `pnpm verify:canary` shares one Sourcify helper with `verify:testnet` (testnet re-verified 10 of 10 through it).
- Signer (ce8fc6f): CanaryKeyProvider, the one raw session key for agent 1 (D-252), and migration 0013 allowing it in signer_keys.
- The run (2568c6c): `pnpm canary:mainnet` in its own database `alpha_agents_canary`: every owner, guardian and sweep transaction is checked against chain 143, simulated from its sender, limited to 110% of its estimate with A-38's fee caps and held to the budget before it is signed; swaps go through packages/signer; anything unexpected stops the run and nothing is resent; a rerun continues from the last finished step. `pnpm canary:local-only` tried 12 local-only features in the canary: all refused.
- On chain: deployment 8 transactions for 2.455 MON (88308ae), every contract an exact match on Sourcify, recorded in the address book as mainnet-canary and throwaway (6aaa8e3, evidence/p2-ec/ADDRESSES.md). Run 14 transactions for 0.428 MON (72b402d): buy 0.45 USDC for 18.2148 WMON (12.24 bps from Chainlink, floor 18.1460), sale for 0.449499 USDC (1.13 bps better than Chainlink); refusals TRADE_SIZE_EXCEEDED and PAUSED at about 0.28 s with no nonce and no hash.
- Measurements (e0d5658, evidence/p2-ec/REAL_CHAIN.md part 2): gas per transaction against testnet and the mainnet fork (an account's first swap uses 1,094,684 gas in the call, so the 1.3M limit keeps about 14%), fees at 102 gwei in MON and dollars, slippage, the pool 7.25 bps above Chainlink at the swaps, MON/USD every 30 s and USDC/USD every 3,606 s, receipts 0.56 to 0.89 s and `finalized` 1.15 to 1.71 s (A-40 holds), the outbox's own timings, no unknown outcome, no RPC error.
- Final balances: canary owner 37.112378866 MON and 4.999499 USDC; guardian and session key 0.00231 MON each (dust, L-145); the PersonalAccount empty. The Executor is left paused and the grant revoked.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan on every commit; vitest 1,671 tests in 121 files; forge 276 with the fork suites skipped; `pnpm test:fork` 32 of 32; `pnpm test:testnet-fork` 4 of 4; `pnpm test:canary-fork` 2 of 2. Not run: the web and console e2e suites (no page changed; the HoldingsPanel fix changes only how it is exported).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 is unchanged (head 109670022 before and after); nothing in this unit sent to it. The testnet stack kept running (no heavy suite needed it stopped). .env is unchanged: CANARY_SIGNING_ENABLED was set only on the command line of the three canary commands.
Notes for the owner:
- Every canary transaction is on MonadVision; the links are in evidence/p2-ec/REAL_CHAIN.md and ADDRESSES.md (part 2 sections).
- The guardian and session key hold 0.00231 MON each; another sweep would cost more than it recovers.
Suggestions:
- PB-U1: watch the USDC/USD feed's age (or name it in the refusal), since a round about five minutes late stops deposits and trades (L-145).
- PB-U1: a second mainnet provider (still none), so the signer's failover has somewhere to go.
- A Monadscan key, if the beta wants Monadscan's verified badge as well as Sourcify's.
- Use `PendingAgentNotice`'s file the same way: it also uses `export function`, which the /design check only passes because the file has an export block for its other components.
Bugs: L-145 (a real-chain difference, no code change), L-146 (process).

## 2026-10-08, P3-U1 Goal form and translator (with Step 0)
Status: done (local fork). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: templates and the runner (P3-U3), data tools (P3-U2), the discovery loop (P3-U4), proposals (P3-U6) and the activation sweep (P3-U8).
Summary: Owners set their agent's goal on a structured Goal page, and a deterministic translator turns it into exact, validated configuration. Saving moves the agent from Not configured to Ready, bumps an offchain strategy epoch that never ends arming, and makes any trade proposed under the old goal refuse itself at submission. The agent reads its goal and every limit through `platform.get_goals_and_limits@1`, bound to its own token. Step 0 raised the USDC/USD staleness bound to 7,200 s, made stale-feed refusals name the feed and when it should update, and recorded mainnet's new fallback RPC provider.
- Step 0.1 (1b59c0c, D-317): USDC/USD may be 7,200 s old (two hourly heartbeats; L-145) in the deploy script, packages/policy and the regenerated parity fixture; MON/USD stays at 300 s. The value is an OracleAdapter constructor argument, so the custody set moved to salt version 2 (`DeployScope.salt(name, version)`, `saltFor` for env-free tests); AgentNFT stays at v1. By your choice the v2 set was deployed on the playtest fork beside v1 without a reset, the local address book names v2, and your wallet 0x683e...5f76 was put on v2's allowlist. Testnet and the canary are throwaway and keep 3,900 s.
- Step 0.2 (24f48b0, D-318): packages/domain's `PRICE_FEEDS` and `staleFeedMessage`: the web deposit pre-check, its `OracleUnavailable` revert text and chain tools' `ORACLE_STALE` blocker (proposals, submissions, why-not-traded) name the feed, the answer's age, the bound and when the next update was due.
- Step 0.3 (a73daf0, D-319): `pnpm rpc:probe`: MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY serves chain 143 (QuickNode), 42 ms median, 1,000-block logs, no refusals at 19 a second, no history (the fork's pin still comes from Alchemy; the upstream proxy retries its refusal on the primary). Only domains recorded.
- Schema and translator (d0c916e, D-320, A-55): the goal's fields in packages/domain, strict at every level; `translateGoal` in packages/policy gives `rebalance_bands@1` parameters inside the template's bounds, the target range narrowed by the owner's limits and the assets, effective limits (each stricter limit only at or inside its hard limit), a policy hash over canonical JSON (no integer-like keys) and the SOUL.md goal block, or every refused field with its reason.
- Storage (c6da42b): migration 0014, `platform.agent_goals` (history, one current, the saving owner's ownership epoch), `platform.agent_states`, `platform.agent_state_changes`, `strategy_epoch` on intents; `GoalStore.save` bumps the epoch in one locked transaction. A goal another owner saved does not apply (the agent reads Not configured).
- Strategy epoch (efb5d7b): proposals record it; the trade flow refuses a stale intent with `STRATEGY_EPOCH_STALE` before anything is checked or sent, and the arming stays open.
- API (8f59bc6): owner-only GET and PUT `/v1/agents/:id/goal` (fresh chain read, ownership epoch), public `POST /v1/goal/preview` (same translator, saves nothing), public goal summary (template and preset only), the goal state in the card's summary, PUT in CORS.
- Agent (1f697dc, 5eafcbe): `platform.get_goals_and_limits@1` takes no input and answers for the token's agent: goal, plan, hard, owner, live and effective limits, mode and the read's block; `research-deep` (Opus 5.5) in LiteLLM (served after a container restart); `renderAgentConfig` accepts the goal block for SOUL.md.
- UI (d490403, 09e6d0a, bb11b50): ChoiceGroup, LimitField, EffectiveLimits, CostPreview, GoalSummary and GoalSaveStatus on /design first; the Goal page at `/agents/:id/goal` with a plain explanation on every field, Light by default, a month's cost per intensity, the limits that apply as you edit, and a clear save result; links from the card, the portfolio and /configure; the state and goal on the card and the portfolio.
- Tests: translator 39 (every field, every limit at, inside and one step past its bound, hash stability, a mutation that loosened a bound failed 6), goal store 6 on Postgres (concurrent saves never share an epoch), trade flow 2 (stale refused, arming open, fresh sent), API 9 (owner-only, refusals, ownership epoch, preview, CORS), platform tool 3 and orchestrator reader 4 (identity by token, owner epoch, effective limits), components 8, form 6.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan on every commit; vitest 1,777 in 128 files; forge 276 with the fork suites skipped; `pnpm test:fork` 32 of 32 with the address book matching; `pnpm test:testnet-fork` 4 of 4; `pnpm test:canary-fork` 2 of 2; web e2e 212 passed, 2 desktop-only skips, re-baselined after looking at both widths and matched on a second run; console e2e 58 of 58 after re-baselining the address book. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-147 (two forge suites raced on one environment variable), L-148 (a silent typecheck failure behind `pnpm -s` in my gate), L-149 (the migration commit skipped packages/db's table check; c6da42b to bb11b50 fail that one test).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 was not stopped or reset; it gained the v2 custody deployment and the allowlist entry (head 109670022 to 109670024). Agent 1 and your v1 account and deposits are untouched at the v1 addresses, which the app no longer reads. The testnet stack was switched to the local one (`pnpm testnet:to-local`), and dev:all is running. The dev database has migration 0014. LiteLLM was restarted to load `research-deep`.
Notes for the owner:
- To try it: open http://localhost:3000/agents, press "Set goal" on agent 1, pick a preset and intensity, read the month's cost, tighten a limit and try one past the hard limit, save, and see Ready on the card and the portfolio.
