## 2026-10-09, F-U2 Contracts I: token registry, oracle v3, and generic venues (with Step 0)
Status: done. Everything in scope is built, tested and deployed on the local fork beside v1 and v2. One Step 0 item is still pending outside our code: the live token check, because the Anthropic account behind LiteLLM still answers "credit balance too low" (probed 2026-10-09; recorded, not waited on). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the custody core v3 (F-U3), Executor v3 (F-U4), the price attestor (F-U12), and any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: The fund agent now has its onchain foundation. A TokenRegistry holds a core lane (class F tokens, added only through the 9-day timelock) and a screened lane (class A tokens the screener adds at once, buyable only by personal accounts whose owners opted in). A ProtocolRegistryV3 pins every pool to its venue's factory or PoolManager by code hash. An OracleAdapterV3 prices each class F token from its own Chainlink feed with its own staleness bound. A RouteAdapter swaps through routes of up to 3 hops across Uniswap v3, PancakeSwap v3 and hookless Uniswap v4 pools, with no raw calldata and no approvals left behind. Offchain, agents can now look up any token and find its pools themselves; the registry is a shared cache and the screen a guardrail, never a whitelist.
- Step 0 (4904b81, 9d55c44, 6080b6f, 9d6168e): A-64 accepted. Q-68 resolved as D-359: bridged copies are refused by default, and pass only when their bridge confirms them (Wormhole's TokenBridge on Monad, isWrappedAsset and wrappedAsset for the origin) or the issuer's official list does (PancakeSwap's Monad list for CAKE), as a reviewed exception in `BRIDGED_EXCEPTIONS`. The timelock check now recognizes Aave Governance v3 (GHO's ProxyAdmin is owned by an Executor whose PayloadsController reports a 1-day delay), so GHO can pass; syrupUSDC, gMON and EURW stay refused. Discovery and the screen fork read through MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY first, the primary as fallback. Discovery reads each reviewed and class F token's own pools from GeckoTerminal (USDT0 now shows its pools). D-360: agents get `lookup_token` (by address, symbol or name) and `find_pools` (GeckoTerminal's pools for the token, confirmed onchain, plus each v3 factory and the common hookless v4 keys against USDC, WMON and MON); `screen_token` takes any address and looks it up first; what agents find enters the registry for everyone (`tokens.found_by`, migration 0021). A-65 records the prices (0.001 USDC upstream, free from a fresh registry entry, 10 per run). The console's Tokens page gained "Look up any token" for operators, and the page's sources wrap at phone width (0603738, L-173).
- Live token check: not run. The LiteLLM probe returned 400 with Anthropic's "credit balance too low". Pending until the account is topped up; then `pnpm test:orchestrator:live`.
- Feed spike (ab3cd68, `pnpm spike:feeds`, evidence/f-u2/feed-spike.json): every leg of the 19 class F feeds was read back through its round history. The bound is 300 seconds for MON/USD and heartbeat plus 300 seconds for every other leg (`feedMaxAgeSeconds`); all 19 pass. The tightest is shMON's exchange-rate leg: its longest gap was 86,467 seconds against a bound of 86,700.
- Contracts (9d2735f; chains/monad/src/fund and src/interfaces/IFund.sol): TokenRegistry: core seeds in the constructor, then ADD_CORE, RESTORE, SET_FEED, SET_CAP, PROMOTE, SET_SCREENER, SET_GUARDIAN and SET_VERIFIER through the RiskTimelock; the screener adds class A tokens and records screens at once (a screen lasts 6 hours onchain); sell-only, freeze and a lower cap are instant for the screener or guardian; restoring buyability always waits the timelock. `buyableFor(token, account)` reads the account's opt-in and refuses screened tokens to vaults. ProtocolRegistryV3: core pools by timelock, screened pools by the screener, each checked against its factory's getPool or its v4 PoolKey hash and pinned by code hash; pause and exit-only are instant. OracleAdapterV3: per-leg staleness, composites as rate times USD, a pool price cross-check of 200 basis points, and the `IAttestationVerifier` interface for F-U12's class A prices. Withdrawals never read it (F-U3 keeps them price-free). RouteAdapter: `swapRoute` takes pool IDs only, checks path continuity with no revisits, pays only the named recipient, refunds leftovers, checks nothing is left behind, gates v3 callbacks to the active pool and the v4 callback to its own unlock, and requires PoolManager to count exactly what a hop owes as paid (810af35, L-174). A-66 records the values.
- Tests: TokenRegistry 20, ProtocolRegistryV3 12, OracleAdapterV3 12, RouteAdapter 24 (every refusal, reentrancy through a hostile pool, fee-on-transfer, a fuzz test), invariants 2 (48 runs, 2,880 calls: routes pay only the recipient and the adapter keeps nothing) (02c77b0); on a fork of the pinned block 7 (69ae0cc): every core token priced by its own feed and bound, the traded pools within 2% of their feeds, a single hop on Uniswap v3, two 2-hop routes (both on PancakeSwap v3; Uniswap v3 then a native MON v4 pool), a 3-hop route across v4 and both v3 venues, and a sell-only token and a paused pool seen at once. The shared policy fixture covers feed legs, composites, lanes, statuses and routes, checked by vitest and by FundParity.t.sol (5) (ea18b6d). TypeScript: discovery, registry, screen, token tools and policy tests extended.
- Slither and gas (810af35, evidence/f-u2/SLITHER.md and GAS.md): 68 results on the new code, 1 fixed (the settle check), the rest justified one by one. swapRoute costs 97,850 gas at least, 502,080 median and 776,780 at most. Code sizes: TokenRegistry 19,517 bytes, ProtocolRegistryV3 19,718, OracleAdapterV3 10,235, RouteAdapter 12,727.
- Deploy (0402f49, b11a0e3): `pnpm deploy:fund` re-screens each core candidate through the orchestrator (a fresh screen if it has one, a new one if not), seeds only those that pass, with only the pools between them, and deploys by CREATE2 with `fund-<name>.v3` salts. On the playtest fork all seven passed (USDC, WMON, AUSD, WBTC, cbBTC, WETH, shMON) with 8 core pools: TokenRegistry 0x544E6dF6E70C1404A7e8a4827F8d9A58FD2D24f8, ProtocolRegistryV3 0x94F24a3dbbCdD64859466a461DA22444cbC7e606, OracleAdapterV3 0x697C4A9154AE5731A54C13FA36D2EFcD84205E65, RouteAdapter 0xA13d903f83Dd8685ce3a49a8C0C28ECc9b8b72C5, all in the address book (local verified; testnet, beta and canary undeployed). `pnpm test:fork` deploys the same set and its address book check matches. Agent 1 is untouched (owner 0x683eE842A16f85e69883F433745263BFe8D55f76).
- Owner's demo (0402f49): `pnpm fund:demo` starts a fork of its own on port 8585, deploys the set, lists the tokens and lanes, adds LV to the screened lane with its PancakeSwap pool, swaps 100 USDC to WMON on Uniswap v3 (2,899.66 WMON) and 100 USDC to WMON to cbBTC on PancakeSwap v3 (117,319 satoshi), shows the adapter keeps nothing, switches WBTC to sell-only at once and shows that restoring it is only a proposal (777,600 seconds to go), then stops its fork.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck and the secrets scan on every commit. vitest 2,066 passed in 154 files; it also reports the same unhandled rejection from scan.test.ts as before this unit (an MCP client posting after its lease ended). forge 353 passed, 2 skipped. `pnpm test:fork` 39 passed in 7 suites, address book matched. Console e2e 112 passed after re-baselining the Tokens pages (checked by eye at 1440 and 380) and adding the sideways-scroll check on every page. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked. Web e2e was not run (no web change).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-172 to L-175.
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 was never stopped or reset; it now also holds the v3 set. Migration 0021 is applied to the dev database. dev:all was stopped for the heavy suites and restarted with this unit's code.
Notes for the owner:
- Blocker, unchanged: top up the Anthropic account, then run `pnpm test:orchestrator:live` for the live token check.
- On 8545 most class F feeds read stale: the fork runs on wall-clock time and the local feed keeper refreshes only MON/USD and USDC/USD (D-237). The oracle refuses those tokens there, as it should; the fork tests and the demo run at fresh blocks.
- shMON's exchange-rate leg has 233 seconds of margin on its bound. If its updater slips, shMON goes unpriced; F-U3 should treat that as expected, not as an outage.
- The Wrapped SOL exception passes the look-alike check but still fails UPGRADEABLE (Wormhole's beacon has no timelock we recognize). Kept refused.
Suggestions:
- F-U3: read `buyableFor` and `sellable` in the custody core, and keep withdrawals off the oracle.
- F-U4: deploy the RouteAdapter bound to Executor v3 and register it through ADD_ADAPTER; the local one's executor is anvil account 0.
- F-U12: implement `IAttestationVerifier` and set it with SET_VERIFIER.
- A local feed keeper for every class F leg would let the playtest fork price all seven core tokens.
Bugs: L-172, L-173, L-174, L-175.
Commit: 4904b81, 9d55c44, 6080b6f, 9d6168e, 0603738, ab3cd68, 9d2735f, 02c77b0, 69ae0cc, ea18b6d, 810af35, 0402f49, b11a0e3, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-10, F-U3 Contracts II: custody core v3 (with Step 0)
Status: done. Everything in scope is built, tested and deployed on the local fork beside the v1, v2 and F-U2 sets, with agent 1 untouched. One Step 0 item stays pending outside our code: the live token check, because the Anthropic account behind LiteLLM still answers "credit balance too low" (probed 2026-10-10 02:20 UTC; recorded, not waited on). Out of scope as the unit prompt says: Executor v3 (F-U4), the StrategyVault (P7-U1), the price attestor (F-U12), UI, and any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: An owner's agent now has an account for a portfolio: up to 16 tokens the TokenRegistry knows, each valued by its own feed or attested price, with an onchain record of what was paid for each. The class A caps hold by that cost basis with no price at all. The owner opts in to screened tokens onchain, and out again at once. Every v2 guarantee holds over the whole list: the owner withdraws any held token with no price, no Executor and no platform; withdrawals cannot be paused; a token that refuses to move becomes a credit while the rest comes out; the sentinel only tightens; loosening waits the timelock in code. The breaker watches the value per unit of the whole portfolio, so deposits and withdrawals never trip it.
- Step 0 (ca75ddd, 98d99eb; A-67): the local feed refresher keeps every class F feed leg fresh on the playtest fork (20 proxies, MON/USD and USDC/USD first), skipping an address with no code so one missing feed never stops the rest; it still refuses anything but the local anvil fork. The feed bound rule is now MON/USD 300 s, every other leg its heartbeat plus 5 minutes (hourly) or 1 hour (daily); measured on mainnet (evidence/f-u3/feed-spike.json), every grace is at least ten times the worst lateness seen and all 19 class F tokens pass, the daily legs with over 3,500 s of margin where shMON had 233. The new bound changed the seeds, so the v3 set is deployed again beside F-U2's (TokenRegistry 0x171F36056B2449F0FBB9923676AffAb14A1cf55C, ProtocolRegistryV3 0x4eB383cA21819119fB9852a23F4f173208CAC098, OracleAdapterV3 0x82df37a295f0387e9d83788213CfC70E944EEaBD, RouteAdapter 0x509450abceD1123007F1DafCeFe4F38B9817dFb7) and the address book names the new set.
- Live token check: not run. The LiteLLM probe returned 400 with Anthropic's "credit balance too low". Pending until the account is topped up; then `pnpm test:orchestrator:live`.
- Contracts (ac933db; chains/monad/src/fund/CustodyCoreV3.sol, PersonalAccountV3.sol, AccountFactoryV3.sol, src/interfaces/ICustodyV3.sol; D-362, A-68): the held list is per account, USDC first, bounded at 16, a token joining on a deposit or a buy and leaving at zero balance. Deposits take USDC and buyable core-lane class F tokens, priced once through OracleAdapterV3 with the depeg guard kept. Trades need a fresh price on both sides (the feed, or the attestation the Executor passed for class A); other class A holdings count at their last attested price for 24 hours, then zero. The cost basis is the USDC paid, moved pro rata on sells, swaps and withdrawals; the 15% and 50% class A caps hold by it, class F keeps the 45% value cap, and the 12% trade cap and the 45% cap read the account with class A at the lower of basis and value. Withdrawals read no oracle and no registry, pay each token with 1,000,000 gas and copy one word of its answer, credit a token that fails (credits keyed by token and beneficiary, ready for the vault's redeemInKind), and are never stopped by a token whose balance read fails. The factory takes the registry as an immutable, the oracle and the Executor at deployment (none for now, D-361), keeps the beta allowlist with instant adds and timelocked caps, and has no buy list: the registry's lanes are it. Events cover deposits, withdrawals, credits, basis changes, held-list changes, attested prices, opt-in changes, mode changes, pokes, trips and every factory change.
- Tests (ac933db, a04462c): forge 143 in the custody v3 suites: creation and the factory; deposits (core class F only, the depeg guard, a fee-on-transfer token, a stale held feed failing closed while withdrawals pass, the 16-token bound); withdrawals of many tokens, one that reverts credited, hostile tokens (a reverting balance read, a gas burner, a return bomb); credits and claims; roles; every backstop at its threshold (12%, 45%, 1%); the class A caps at 15% and 50% exactly and one unit over, and with a wrong price high and low; the opt-in and instant opt-out; sell-only and frozen tokens; the breaker over many tokens with class A at zero after a day; invariants (48 runs, three tokens, the Executor unset: funds leave only through the owner, credits never exceed the balance, the held list consistent), fuzz (any calldata by anyone else moves nothing), reentrancy through a hooked USDC on every guarded entry point. The shared fixture (packages/policy/src/custody.ts, D-343) replays 40 sequences over eight tokens with every op and refusal on both sides (CustodyV3Parity.t.sol), and the model's constants are read from the contract. On a fork of the pinned block (CustodyV3Fork.t.sol): seven real core tokens, six bought through the real pools, deposited at their own feeds, withdrawn with the oracle and the registry replaced by reverting code, and Circle's real blacklist and pause turning USDC into a credit while the six others come out.
- Slither and gas (40e17f4; evidence/f-u3/SLITHER.md, GAS.md): 93 results on the new code, every one justified. A 16-token trade costs 2,127,872 gas, a deposit up to 1,909,124, withdrawAll up to 2,605,169 with 16 tokens; PersonalAccountV3 is 41,205 bytes of runtime (inside Monad's 128 KiB, like the Executor v2), deployed once per factory.
- Deploy (40e17f4): `pnpm deploy:custody-v3` deploys AccountFactoryV3 by CREATE2 (`alpha-agents.account-factory.v3`) bound to the v3 TokenRegistry and OracleAdapterV3, asserting the bindings and the initial state. On the playtest fork: AccountFactoryV3 0x8A7F8C0bc4C8f00Cbdab8EAAa130B8767058B9d5, PersonalAccountV3 implementation 0x84a1233476BC5F494Cf15BF4d9fc161EB1E278aD, both in the address book; `pnpm test:fork` deploys the same set on its test fork and the book check matches. Agent 1 is untouched (owner 0x683eE842A16f85e69883F433745263BFe8D55f76, epoch 0). How F-U4 binds Executor v3: it deploys this factory again with the Executor given, so a second factory appears beside this one (D-361).
- Owner's demo (40e17f4): `pnpm custody:v3:demo` on a fork of its own (port 8586): deploys the set, mints anvil account 6 an agent, opens its account, buys cbBTC and shMON through real pools, deposits USDC, WMON, cbBTC and shMON, shows each holding's cost basis and last price (70.58 USDC in all, 1.00 per unit), opts in to the screened lane and out again, and withdraws everything in one call.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan on every commit. forge 473 passed, 10 skipped. vitest 2,080 passed in 155 files (fork tests included); it also reports the same unhandled rejection from scan.test.ts as before this unit (an MCP client posting after its lease ended, 401). `pnpm test:fork` 44 passed in 8 suites, address book matched. Console e2e 112 passed after re-baselining the address book page at 1440 and 380 (pixel-identical above its entry count; the changes are the count, the two updated v3 notes and the two new rows, checked by eye and by a pixel comparison), then matched on a second run. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked. Web e2e was not run (no web change).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-176 (forge's `deal` cannot write a real proxy token's balance), L-177 (an exit's low-level calls must bound what they copy back, found by forge lint before any test), L-178 (slips caught by the unit's own checks, including a fixture struct name the full forge run caught).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 was never stopped or reset; its head moved from 109670024 to 109670027 by this unit's three deployments. It now also holds the F-U2 set at its old addresses (unused), the redeployed v3 set, AccountFactoryV3 and the PersonalAccountV3 implementation. dev:all was stopped for the heavy suites and restarted with this unit's code; under it the orchestrator now re-dates every class F feed on 8545 every minute, so the v3 oracle prices all seven core tokens there.
Notes for the owner:
- Blocker, unchanged: top up the Anthropic account, then run `pnpm test:orchestrator:live` for the live token check.
- The local v3 factory's allowlist holds anvil accounts 6 to 9 only; your wallet joins with an instant `addDepositor` from the admin when F-U11 brings the portfolio page to v3 (the v2 `pnpm custody:allow` targets the v2 factory).
- A deposit, trade or poke fails closed while any held class F feed is unusable; the owner's withdrawal never does. On 8545 every class F feed is now kept fresh by dev:all, so this only shows when dev:all is down.
- PersonalAccountV3 is 41 KB of runtime: fine on Monad and the fork, and the deploy script asserts it; it would not fit a 24 KB chain.
Suggestions:
- F-U4: deploy Executor v3, then `pnpm deploy:custody-v3` with `CUSTODY_V3_EXECUTOR` set and update the address book (D-361); the Executor passes `SwapParamsV3` with the attestation per class A side and `keccak256(route)` as `routeHash`; size its gas limit from GAS.md's maxima (a 16-token trade is 2.13M inside the account alone).
- P7-U1: the core's credits are keyed by beneficiary and `_credit`, `_settleWithdrawal`, `_free` and the held list are the pieces `redeemInKind` needs; `isVault()` and `screenedOptIn()` are the hooks the registry reads.
- F-U5: the chain tools' account reader should use `holdings()` (balance, free, basis, last price per token) and `capValues()` for the caps.
- A `custody:v3:allow` command (or an `--factory v3` flag on `custody:allow`) for the owner's wallet once the app targets v3.
Bugs: L-176, L-177, L-178.
Commit: ca75ddd, 98d99eb, ac933db, a04462c, 40e17f4, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-10, F-U4 Contracts III: Executor v3 and the policy mirror
Status: done on the test fork, blocked on the playtest fork. Everything in scope is built, tested on mocks and on Monad's real pools, mirrored in packages/policy, deployed deterministically on `pnpm test:fork`'s fork and recorded in the address book. The same deployment on the playtest fork (8545) did not run: that fork's anvil stopped answering at 05:15 UTC during this unit, after I pointed a forge fork test at it (L-181), and "never stop or reset the playtest fork" leaves its revival to the owner. Every address is the same on both forks by construction (D-363), so `pnpm deploy:executor-v3` lands the recorded set once the fork answers. Out of scope as the unit prompt says: the chain tools, the signer and the trade flow (F-U5), the runner (F-U6), the attestor (F-U12), vaults, UI, and any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: The fund agent's Executor now trades any pair of registered tokens through routes of up to three registered pools, checks every hard limit across the whole portfolio in one fixed order with a named reason for each refusal, prices each side by its own feed or by the attestation the intent carries (and refuses class A by name until the attestor exists), enforces the screened opt-in and the sell-only and frozen statuses, judges a fill by what actually arrived in the account, and keeps nothing. packages/policy answers the same offchain, held to the contract by 236 shared cases. The Executor's registry lists its adapter from construction, so no timelock stands between a deployment and the first trade.
- Contracts (3d2846d; chains/monad/src/fund/ExecutorV3.sol, src/interfaces/IExecutorV3.sol; D-363, A-70): `SwapIntentV3` (schema 2) carries the route and one attestation per side and no recipient, target, selector or value. The check order is the global pause, the intent's shape and policy hash, the route's length, both tokens' registry records (listed, not frozen; the token bought not sell-only and, in the screened lane, buyable for this account), the session and both epochs, the account's identity and the actionId, the deadline, the account's mode, the adapter, both sides' prices (a feed, or the attestation through the registry's verifier: `ATTESTOR_UNAVAILABLE`, `ATTESTATION_REQUIRED`, `ATTESTATION_INVALID`), every hop (usable, connected, no revisit, class F between the ends, the pool within 2% of the feed or of the attested price), the balance, the account's values (`ORACLE_STALE` when a held feed is unusable), an unpoked drawdown, the 10% size on the capped value, 20 trades and 100% turnover in the ring buffer, the floor of 0.5% (1% class A) under the implied output, then the caps projected at the prices: the lower of the policy's 40% and the registry's cap by value for class F, the class A caps by cost basis, and the USDC floor. Inside the account's `executeSwap` the Executor pulls exactly the input, hands it to the adapter, measures the account's balance change against the intent's minimum, and checks its own balances are unchanged; afterwards the value that arrived may be short of the value sent by at most the slippage plus the route's pool fees, and the caps and the floor hold on the real balances. Grants, epochs, the ring buffer, the timelocked loosening and the instant tightening are v2's. The Executor binds once to the factory deployed with it and to its pool registry.
- Reason codes (3d2846d; packages/domain/src/reasons.ts, services/chain-tools/src/logic.ts): nine new codes with their messages and the chain tools' guidance (`ROUTE_INVALID`, `NOT_OPTED_IN`, `TOKEN_SELL_ONLY`, `TOKEN_FROZEN`, `ATTESTATION_REQUIRED`, `ATTESTATION_INVALID`, `ATTESTOR_UNAVAILABLE`, `CLASS_A_POSITION_CAP`, `CLASS_A_TOTAL_CAP`); the domain test holds v2's `Reason` as the list's prefix and v3's `ReasonV3` as the whole list. `ExecutorPolicyV3` and `executorPolicyHashV3` compute the hash the contract computes.
- Policy mirror (3d2846d; packages/policy/src/executor-v3.ts, executor-v3-parity.ts, fixtures/executor-v3-parity.json): `executorV3.verdict` and `executorV3.blockers` take what the chain tools read (registry records, holdings with the prices the account's views use, each side's price or attestation state, each hop's fee and oracle verdict, the ring) and answer in the contract's order. The fixture builder drives the F-U3 custody model to build each case's account (deposits at a peak MON price, class A positions bought through the honest venue, a poke), then the market the trade meets; 76 hand-written cases and 160 random ones cover every market limit, every token rule, every attestation state, the class A caps at exactly their thresholds and one unit over, routes of every shape and trades that go through; `ExecutorV3Parity.t.sol` replays them in four slices (L-179).
- Tests (3d2846d): forge, six unit suites over FundBase's registries, oracle and pools priced on their feeds, the real RouteAdapter and a venue that misbehaves ten ways, both registered through the registry's timelock: every limit at and across its threshold; one, two and three hops through the mock pools; routes, venues and pool statuses; sell-only, frozen and screened tokens; class A refused until the attestor exists, then attestations none, expired, for another token and off the pool, and the position and total caps exactly at 15% and 50%; sessions and epochs; a venue reporting more than it delivers, paying less, nothing, elsewhere, the Executor or another token, refunding half, reentering, calling `onSwap`, withdrawing; a hostile screened token reentering from inside a trade; the invariant run (48 runs, 2,880 calls over four tokens with the owner's flows, mode changes, agent transfers and MON moves: the Executor and the adapter keep nothing, the account's balances are exactly its flows and measured trades, no limit broken, the window never past 20).
- Deployment (d7aedfb; chains/monad/script/ExecutorSetDeployer.sol, DeployExecutorV3.s.sol, BindExecutorV3.s.sol, scripts/lib/executor-v3.js; D-363): the adapter takes the registry's address and the registry pins the adapter's code, so one `ExecutorSetDeployer`, deployed by CREATE2 from every argument, deploys both from its nonces 1 and 2 inside its constructor; `pnpm deploy:executor-v3` deploys the Executor (CREATE2 `executor.v3`), that pair, an OracleAdapterV3 over the new registry, AccountFactoryV3 again with the Executor and the oracle given (D-361), and binds. On the test fork: Executor v3 0xe88959BAA4511E3a5Cf74E6b8777dd2491aA02c8, its RouteAdapter 0xb56Ba7888F4F8C6Fe6F2B6A7C5ddD4B7B17d7B3A, ProtocolRegistryV3 0xDd230457289fe2988F44E0c760DA80e00825d191 (eight core pools), OracleAdapterV3 0xA5eB497331f28728D4f3e97dC0aF9d99CB3469f0, AccountFactoryV3 0x8fd715548256E1b5919bc336Ac120337b84b9697, PersonalAccountV3 implementation 0x2D1E785ECF940adcd89790517F994bf01d114Cc5; the address book names them (and keeps the TokenRegistry and the demo adapter) and `pnpm test:fork`'s check matches. The fork test (test/fork/ExecutorV3Fork.t.sol) deploys in the same order and trades 20 USDC of value from the session key through one hop (PancakeSwap v3), two hops (PancakeSwap twice; the native MON v4 pool then PancakeSwap) and three hops (PancakeSwap twice then Uniswap v4), with funds only in the account; the two-hop buy of cbBTC is refused at the floor because both pools sit 0.2% and 0.27% over their feeds in the buying direction, while the sale fills.
- Gas and Slither (d7aedfb; evidence/f-u4/GAS.md, SLITHER.md; A-69): on the real pools a trade costs 2,128,234 (one hop, four tokens held) to 2,974,751 (three hops, six held); most of it is valuation of every held token's real feed, four times per trade, so the signer's rule is 1,000,000 plus 150,000 per hop plus 400,000 per held token (packages/domain `executorV3SwapGasLimit`). Executor v3 is 39,165 bytes of runtime on the fork. Slither: 311 results in all, 27 in the new code, every one justified.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan on every commit. forge 554 passed, 11 skipped (the fork suites, which ran under test:fork). vitest 2,099 passed in 157 files, 1 skipped, 0 failed (fork tests included); it also reports the same unhandled rejection from scan.test.ts as before this unit (an MCP client posting after its lease ended, 401), which makes its exit code 1 as it did in F-U3. `pnpm test:fork` 50 passed in 9 suites, address book matched. Console e2e 112 passed after re-baselining the address book page at 1440 and 380 (pixel-identical above its entry count; the changes are the count, the two updated v3 notes and the six new rows, checked by eye and by a pixel comparison), then 112 passed again on a second run against the new baselines. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked. Web e2e was not run (no web change).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-179 (one test over forge's 2^30 gas, read as ORACLE_STALE through a catch-all), L-180 (slips caught by the unit's own checks), L-181 (a forge fork test pointed at the playtest fork wedged it; not fixed, reported).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 is hung since 05:15 UTC: its anvil (pid 165477, started two days ago by `pnpm dev:up`) is alive and listening but answers no request; nothing was stopped or reset. The Executor v3 set is not on it yet. dev:all was stopped for the heavy suites; its restart with this unit's code failed because `pnpm dev:up` finds anvil DOWN ("no response"), so the dev stack is down until the fork answers, and `pnpm dev:all` starts it then. Postgres and redis are up.
Notes for the owner:
- Blocker: the playtest fork's anvil on 8545 is wedged (L-181 has the evidence). Its revival is your call: a restart loses its in-memory state (agent 1, every deployment) unless the runner's state dump restores it. Once a fork answers on 8545, run `pnpm deploy:fund` if it is fresh, then `pnpm deploy:executor-v3`; the addresses will be the recorded ones.
- Commit 3d2846d holds both the Executor and the policy mirror: the first slice's gate failed on a typecheck the new reason codes caused in the chain tools, and the second commit carried both once it was fixed. Its message describes the mirror; the Executor's description is in this entry.
- Blocker, unchanged from F-U3: the Anthropic account behind LiteLLM still answers "credit balance too low" (probed 2026-10-10 03:55 UTC), so the live token check stays pending.
- On real pools a multi-hop buy can be refused at the 0.5% floor while every pool is within 2% of its feed, because the deviations add up in one direction (evidence/f-u4/GAS.md); F-U5's `get_quote` should quote the route and the agent proposes where the quote meets the floor.
- An account that opts out of screened tokens cannot sell the ones it holds through their screened pools (the registry closes a screened pool to an opted-out account, F-U2); the owner withdraws them in kind. Recorded for the registry's owner to decide (F-U6 or F-U7).
Suggestions:
- A combined account view returning NAV, the capped value and the bases in one valuation would cut three of a trade's four valuations (about 300,000 gas per held token on real feeds); it changes the custody core's bytecode, so a later contracts unit.
- F-U5: intents are `SwapIntentV3` with schema 2 and `adapterId = keccak256("route-adapter")`; grants come from Executor v3's `registerSession` (the v2 Executor's grants do not carry over); the signer's gas limit is `executorV3SwapGasLimit(hops, heldTokensAfter)`; `executorV3.blockers` is `tradable_now`'s list; the account's `holdings()` and `capValues()` and the registry's records give the mirror its market.
- The F-U2 demo RouteAdapter still reads the F-U2 registry; `pnpm fund:demo` keeps working on its own fork, and a later unit can retire it.
Bugs: L-179, L-180, L-181.
Commit: 3d2846d, d7aedfb, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-10, F-U4 completion: the playtest fork restored, the sell rule in Executor v3, and one deployment
Status: done. F-U4 is complete on both forks: the playtest fork (8545) came back from its saved state, Executor v3 took the owner's sell rule (D-365) before its first deployment there, and one Executor v3 set is deployed and recorded, with every transaction sent also mined. The previous session (the F-U4 repair, 2026-10-10 06:30 to 14:22 UTC) rebuilt the fork and wrote the mirror side of the rule, then stalled on a background deploy that anvil never finished (L-182); its uncommitted work is now committed in order. Out of scope and untouched: F-U5, the chain tools, the signer, the runner, the attestor, vaults, any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: A `pnpm dev:up` after a WSL reboot loaded `.dev/anvil-state.json` and the fork answered head 109670009 with AgentNFT, agent 1 owned by the owner's wallet, the v1 set, the TokenRegistry and its history (D-364, first real restore). The Executor now lets a route use screened pools when the account opted in or when the token it sells is in the screened lane, so a held screened token always has its own pool as the way out while buying one still needs the opt-in; the policy mirror answers the same and the shared fixture holds 238 cases. The recorded fund set, then the Executor v3 set, were deployed in the foreground with the sent-against-mined check after each run; the set's addresses changed with the bytecode and the address book, the test fork and the console's address book page carry the new ones.
- Part A, the fork (f7da205; scripts/anvil-runner.js, scripts/dev.js, scripts/lib/paths.js, README; D-364): anvil runs with `--state .dev/anvil-state.json --state-interval 60 --preserve-historical-states`; a file anvil could not have written, or one behind the pinned block, is moved aside and the fork starts fresh. Restore verified by RPC: head `0x6896e79`, AgentNFT 34,459 bytes and the TokenRegistry 19,517 bytes of code, `ownerOf(1)` the owner's wallet, a balance at block 109670001 served, the deployer's nonce 646 with an empty pool (the three stranded transactions of L-182 gone), and the minute save writing again. What a restore does not bring back is the state at the pinned block itself (L-186).
- Part B, the sell rule (ee006cf, f907f34; chains/monad/src/fund/ExecutorV3.sol, packages/policy/src/executor-v3.ts, executor-v3-parity.ts, fixtures/executor-v3-parity.json, test/fund/ExecutorV3Tokens.t.sol; D-365): `_checkIntent` returns whether the token sold is screened and `_checkMarket` sets `allowScreened` from that or the opt-in; the token bought is still refused with `NOT_OPTED_IN` before the route. The mirror carries each hop's lane with the same rule; the fixture adds the exit into USDC, the exit on through a core pool and the refused swap into another screened token; the token suite sells a sell-only screened token in parts, before and after opting out (the inherited test sold the same five units twice and failed with `INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE`, fixed). The TokenRegistry and ProtocolRegistryV3 needed no change: `sellable` already covers SELL_ONLY. A doc comment added to RouteAdapter.sol moved the demo adapter's CREATE2 address and was taken out again (L-185).
- Part C, one deployment (1860bc0; packages/domain/src/address-book.ts, apps/console/e2e/__screenshots__/addresses-desktop.png, addresses-mobile.png): the recorded fund set with every candidate seeded, screens skipped (A-71): TokenRegistry 0x171F36056B2449F0FBB9923676AffAb14A1cf55C found, ProtocolRegistryV3 0x4eB383cA21819119fB9852a23F4f173208CAC098, OracleAdapterV3 0x82df37a295f0387e9d83788213CfC70E944EEaBD and the demo RouteAdapter 0x509450abceD1123007F1DafCeFe4F38B9817dFb7 deployed, 3 and then 1 sent and mined. `pnpm deploy:executor-v3`, 5 sent and mined: Executor v3 0x3443dbBd29E19CF17853732C260C6abDb6dC0658 (39,226 bytes), its RouteAdapter 0x9bA3aF3a1A591EF1D6d92FD5Ca26eCD0C6e76974 (12,727), ProtocolRegistryV3 0x16B3C81d48510a1b7e98D048C7904b56E387611c (19,718), OracleAdapterV3 0xeeB91f3371Db0d409bbE0Ffa9ea901E09A783a43 (10,235), AccountFactoryV3 0x5ebF58520f9bAa847959DcF69716030a1583BCDb (11,645) and the PersonalAccountV3 implementation 0x2Ec618e51FcD7562Fe1EBeF16536d48E60b5b994 (41,205); policy hash unchanged 0xb5a9fa71...3bb. `pnpm deploy:custody-v3` could not run before the Executor (it reads the Executor set's oracle from the address book) and, run after it, deployed an AccountFactoryV3 with the Executor unset at 0xf052fd76f450d4d804ab4182167dc329629cd882, unused (L-184). The fork mined blocks 109670010 to 109670015 for all of it.
- Unused contracts on the playtest fork, from the rebuild and this session (A-71): the fund set seeded from two tokens at 0x5040dd42c0a7a21d4dc6befbd7a72184cfaab20b, 0x57d6ecc088354098710daa62b4f7368116b59963, 0xbfcd6860a9ba47abf11cba358f297a5dfb1f18ab and 0x1e687f61c39e88bd8a98e795e63192fc0c778193; the demo RouteAdapter at 0x7c78a3C0899E723bC77DcB66978a94fBf69B456E; the factory at 0xf052fd76... above. Nothing references them.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan pass. forge 555 passed, 0 failed, 11 skipped (the fork suites) in 64 suites; the Executor v3 suites 82 tests in 7 suites. vitest 2,100 passed in 157 files, 0 failed; its exit code is 1 from the same unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts (an MCP client posting after its lease ended, 401) as in F-U3 and F-U4. `pnpm test:fork` 50 passed in 9 suites on a fresh fork, the Executor v3 set deployed there at the same addresses and the address book matching every entry. Console e2e 112 passed in update mode (only the two address book baselines changed, checked by eye at 1440 and 380: the six v3 rows carry the new addresses, 48 of 49 entries verified) and 112 passed again against the new baselines. On the playtest fork the address book matches at latest for every entry except the three Chainlink feeds, which show the LocalFeed's 688 bytes by design (D-237); the pinned-block reads the checker makes fail on a restored fork (L-186), so that check ran on `test:fork`'s fresh fork. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked; web e2e was not run (no web change).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-182 (the deploy stall), L-183 (the wrong chain ID constant in the readiness check), L-184 (the stray fund set and the factory), L-185 (a comment moved a CREATE2 address), L-186 (a restored fork's pinned block is empty). The inherited test slip is in Part B above.
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 is at head 109670015 with everything above; `.dev/anvil-state.json` saves every minute. `pnpm dev:all` is running (restarted after the heavy suites). Docker Desktop was not running after the WSL reboot and was started from WSL for `pnpm dev:up`; Postgres and Redis are up.
Notes for the owner:
- WBTC (0x0555E30da8f98308EdB960aa94C0Db47230d2B9c) has a fresh refused screen today (15:03 UTC) while the other six core candidates pass, so `pnpm deploy:fund` on its own would have seeded six tokens at addresses nothing records; the recorded set was deployed with the screens skipped, as `test:fork` deploys it (A-71). The screen's checks are on the console's Tokens page; the core lane itself only changes through the registry's timelock.
- The address book check on the playtest fork now has to be read at latest (L-186); `pnpm test:fork` keeps the full pinned-block check on a fresh fork.
- Step 3.2 of the prompt, `pnpm deploy:custody-v3` before the Executor, cannot run as scripted since F-U4 (it requires the Executor set's oracle); run after, it adds an unused factory. Left as found, recorded in L-184.
Suggestions:
- Retire the standalone `pnpm deploy:custody-v3` for the local fork or make it find the Executor-bound factory, so the prompt's order cannot leave a factory with the Executor unset.
- Let the deploy helpers apply the CLAUDE.md rule themselves: after a forge run, compare `eth_sendTransaction` against mined, drop and resend an unmined transaction after ten seconds, and fail loudly instead of waiting.
- Let `verify-addresses.js` read at latest on a restored fork, or let `dev:up` print L-186's caveat when it restores.
- Expose a screen's checks on the orchestrator's token record (or an endpoint), so a deploy can say why a candidate was left out.
- A fresh `pnpm deploy:fund` on the playtest fork is seeded from the screens of the moment; a `--recorded` flag would make the recorded set the explicit choice.
Bugs: L-182, L-183, L-184, L-185, L-186.
Commits: f7da205, ee006cf, f907f34, 1860bc0, plus the docs commit.

## 2026-10-10, F-U5 Chain tools, signer and trade flow for N tokens (with Step 0)
Status: done. Agents whose owner opened a PersonalAccountV3 trade any registered pair along the best route of up to three registered pools through the real signer and Executor v3, settle only after every token the route touched was reconciled, and are refused with the same why-not-traded record as before; agents with only a v2 account keep working unchanged; the dev console's Trades page drives both sets. The live E2B run is pending on the Anthropic account.
Summary: One custody path per agent (D-367): v3 once a PersonalAccountV3 exists, v2 while only a v2 account does, v3 for none. The tool server registers the v2 or the v3 chain tools per request by that path; intents, armings and snapshots carry it; a grant on one set never covers the other.
- Step 0 (6e08f78; scripts/deploy-custody-v3.js removed, package.json, README; D-366): the standalone `pnpm deploy:custody-v3` is retired, since a factory deployed without its Executor cannot trade; `deployCustodyV3Local` stays for the Executor deploy, `test:fork` and the demo. `pnpm test:orchestrator:live` was tried at the start (15:35 UTC) and once more at the end (16:54 UTC): everything through provisioning, credits, the sandbox and the gate passes, and the model call is refused by the provider with "Your credit balance is too low to access the Anthropic API" (HTTP 400 through LiteLLM); recorded as pending with the report in evidence/p1-u7/live-2026-10-10T16-54-17.400Z.json.
- Signer and domain (8d07515; packages/domain intents.ts, executor-policy.ts; packages/db migration 0022; packages/accounting journal.ts; packages/signer abi.ts, policy.ts, reconcile.ts, signer.ts, fake-chain.ts; packages/trading store.ts, json.ts): the signer signs Executor v3's `swap` as a second allowed entry point, requires schema 2 and a route of one to three pools, takes the gas limit the trade flow computed from F-U4's rule (A-69) and bounds it by the rule's maximum, keeps the chain pin, the outbox and "unknown is not failed", and reconciles a v3 swap by every token the route crossed: the token sold fell by exactly `amountIn`, the token bought rose by the event's `amountOut`, and no other route token moved; the ledger names a token without an asset ID by its lowercase address (A-72). Intents gain custody, both token addresses and the route; armings gain custody and the Executor; snapshots gain custody and per-token holdings.
- Chain tools (785084a; services/chain-tools reader-v3.ts, routes.ts, logic-v3.ts, schema-v3.ts, server-v3.ts, server.ts; packages/tool-server host.ts; orchestrator chain-store.ts, servers.ts, main.ts): `get_portfolio` reads every held token with its price, value, cost basis and share; `get_prices` every registered token and pool with the oracle's view; `get_quote` enumerates every registered route of up to three hops (exit-only pools only into USDC, screened pools only when opted in or selling a screened token, D-365), quotes each hop at its venue's quoter and picks the best by amount out; `get_limits` per-token caps and room; `tradable_now` every blocker, with the policy mirror naming the lane (L-187); `propose_swap` any registered pair with the route stored. A class A token is refused with the attestor named as unavailable until F-U12.

## 2026-10-10, P0-API: the Anthropic account, diagnosed
Status: done; the account still refuses. Every model call through LiteLLM has failed since 2026-10-10 03:55 UTC with Anthropic's "credit balance is too low". Checked without spending: a one-token request straight to api.anthropic.com with the key from .env fails the same way (`req_011CftxbcjkW2t2AtSuNHPmu`), as does the free token count; the model list answers 200, so the key authenticates; the key in the LiteLLM container is byte for byte the one in .env (compared by hash, no whitespace or carriage returns); the config's three model IDs all exist for this key; LiteLLM has no budget list, no global budget and no blocked key, and passes Anthropic's error through unchanged. Conclusion: the organization that owns the key has no usable credit as Anthropic sees it; nothing in the repository changes that. The owner's steps are in evidence/p0-api/DIAGNOSIS.md.
- Dry run (services/orchestrator/src/anthropic-dry-run.ts, `pnpm test:anthropic:dry-run`): builds the real request for every alias in infra/litellm/config.yaml and every model stage (Scan routine and activation, Dive, Challenge, Zoom out, plus a narrator entry): the cycle's SOUL.md as the system prompt, the orchestrator's stage prompt, and the 30 tools of the chain (v3), data and platform servers in Anthropic's format under Hermes' names, sent to the free `count_tokens` endpoint and recorded to evidence/p0-api/dry-run-<time>.json. All 16 refused with the same 400 today (`req_011Cfty3vPrAgpsUTPWBvrp5` to `req_011Cfty47WqsBx6CUjzr869B`); once the account works it records the token count per stage.
- The pending list (evidence/live-pending.md) and `pnpm test:live:pending` (scripts/live-pending.js): the dry run, then `pnpm test:orchestrator:live`, which holds every live check F-U1 to F-U5 were waiting on; later units append rows and steps.
- No fallback provider: .env holds no other model provider's key, and OpenRouter is allowed only for zero-retention routes (D-105, D-353), so none was configured. The units of this session build with recorded model responses in their tests.
- Checks: format, lint and typecheck pass on the new files; the secrets scan passes; nothing was deployed and no transaction was sent; the playtest fork is untouched.

## 2026-10-10, F-U6 Target portfolio, runner v2 and Test stage v2
Status: done. An agent's plan can be a target portfolio over any registered tokens (D-344): positions with a weight, a band, a thesis and an exit plan plus a cash target. The deterministic runner makes one capped leg a minute toward it, sells before buys, always against USDC, through the F-U5 trade flow and Executor v3; the deterministic Test stage v2 checks a draft against the registry, the screens, the aggressiveness envelope, the class A caps, depth at the slippage limit, turnover, the cooldown and the evals; `rebalance_bands@1` keeps its own path unchanged; no model sizes a trade anywhere. The dev console sets, checks and shows a target portfolio.
Summary: The rule lives in packages/policy as `target_portfolio@1` with 19 hand-computed evals; the runner reads a plan's template from the stored row and runs a portfolio plan on the fund agent's v3 set; the Test stage v2 is the plan's gate in the orchestrator and the Zoom out may now draft a target portfolio (F-U8 teaches it to); the aggressiveness envelope (A-60) is read from the saved goal's risk preset until F-U7 carries it (A-74). No live model check belongs to this unit: the runner and the Test are deterministic.
- The rule (d7146c3; packages/policy portfolio.ts, evals.ts, evals/target_portfolio@1.json; packages/domain runner.ts, goals.ts): sells first (the most overweight position, a trim level passed, a token moved to sell-only or dropped from the plan), then buys (the most underweight), each leg capped by the per-trade limit less a half-percent margin, the position's cap (the lowest of the envelope's, the registry's and the Executor's), the cash floor and the plan's minimum trade; holds name their reason: IN_BAND, BELOW_MIN_TRADE, SCREEN_STALE, ATTESTATION_UNAVAILABLE (class A until F-U12), CAP_REACHED, CASH_FLOOR, TOKEN_FROZEN, ORACLE_STALE, VOLATILITY_BRAKE (per token; WMON needs MON's reading), COST_HURDLE after the route's quote. Fingerprint pinned; the two-asset rule and its evals are untouched.
- The runner and the Test (04e839e; packages/trading plans.ts, decisions.ts; services/orchestrator runner.ts, cycle/test-stage.ts, cycle/research.ts, cycle/cycle.ts, plan-routes.ts, orchestrator.ts, narrator.ts; services/platform-tools briefs.ts): the plan store keeps either template by name with its own hash; the runner v2 builds the rule's state from the v3 reader (every registered token, the holding, status, cap, price), the platform's fresh screen per plan token (D-339) and the volatility reading, quotes the leg along the best registered route, runs the same v3 pre-check as `propose_swap`, prices gas by the route (A-69) and proposes a v3 intent with its route, so the trade flow sends it through Executor v3; the Test stage v2 (`checkPortfolioProposal`) and `portfolioEnvelope` for the Zoom out; the decision schema accepts a `target_portfolio@1` draft, checked by the Test v2 at `complete_stage`; `PUT /plan` takes either template, `POST /plan/check` returns the Test's findings for a draft, and the plan view carries the envelope and the registered tokens.

Baselines re-done at 1440 and 380 after a check by eye (the agents page gains the envelope line and the template switch; a new capture of the portfolio form).
- Tests (d7146c3, 04e839e, 79edb3c, 06814a3): the rule's evals and shape checks; the Test stage v2 with every code produced by a plan built to fail that rule; the runner on Postgres against a fake fund agent's set (a v3 intent with its route, LEG_PENDING, a sell-only exit over two pools, SCREEN_STALE, a paused Executor, a stale epoch); the plan routes for a portfolio; the console's form helpers and its composed source; the runner on a fork of its own (8559): from 90 USDC to WMON 20% and WBTC 20% through the trade flow, the real signer and Executor v3 one capped leg at a time (a two-hop route among them), in band, then WBTC sold first once the guardian moved it to sell-only.

## 2026-10-10, F-U8 Research v2: per-token Dives, position calls and playbooks 2.0
Status: done. Research is genuine fundamental analysis, organized the way the owner asked: a Scan over the registry, the recent pools, the market and the open positions that flags tokens, position reviews or market changes; a Dive per token that works the eight-item fundamentals checklist with the tools that reach each item, tags every claim by what its sources give and dates it, reads the safety screen, and ends in a thesis with its kill condition, horizon, confidence and fair weight or in "no thesis"; a Challenge that attacks the fundamentals and the fit with the account; and a Zoom out that calls ADD, HOLD, TRIM or EXIT per position, weighs the whole portfolio against the goal, says plainly when the evidence is weak, and drafts a target portfolio the Test v2 checks. The playbooks are 2.0.0, two skills are new built-in folders, and `pnpm skills:lock` records every version. No live model could run (the account still refuses); the cycle's stages are played by scripted briefs through the real tools, the validator and complete_stage, and the live checks are rows 4 and 5 of the pending list.
Summary: services/platform-tools holds the v2 brief shapes; the orchestrator's validator enforces the source-class rule, its engine carries each theme's token into the Dive's prompt and context, and complete_stage checks the Zoom out's position calls against its draft and the plan in force; packages/skills holds the content and the lock.
- Briefs v2 (f152fd9; services/platform-tools briefs.ts, schema.ts): a Scan theme has `scope` (TOKEN, POSITION or MARKET), `token` and `symbol`; the THEME brief is per token with `whatItIs`, `whyNow`, `fundamentals` (usage, feesRevenueVolume, tvl, holdersLiquidity, supplyEmissions, control, catalysts, relativeValue, each a claim or null), `risks`, `screen` and `fairWeightBps` beside the question, the evidence, the freshness, the thesis and the weakest link; every claim may carry `asOf`; the RATIONALE carries `portfolioView`, `evidenceStrength` and `positions` (one call per token); the schema refuses a fair weight without a thesis or a passing screen, a proposal on weak evidence, and a NO_CHANGE that moves a position; a candidate's asset is any symbol or MARKET.
- Engine (f152fd9; services/orchestrator cycle/validator.ts, rationale.ts, research.ts, cycle.ts, config.ts, stages.ts): the validator checks the new fields' numbers, URLs and skill runs, and refuses a claim whose class none of its sources can give (the P3-U7 tagging slips: an aggregator's TVL tagged onchain, a report tagged primary); `rationaleFindings` names every call that does not match the draft (an EXIT still in it, a HOLD whose weight moves, a TRIM that leaves, a position without a call, a held token leaving without an EXIT, calls on a two-asset proposal) and complete_stage refuses the proposal with them; the research context's `cycle.token` names a Dive's token from the Scan's theme; the stage prompts describe the v2 work and no longer assume a two-asset account.
- Content (2ed220e; packages/skills/builtin): playbook-scan, playbook-dive, playbook-challenge and playbook-zoom-out 2.0.0 (the Dive trimmed to the size limit three times); deep-dive-research 1.1.0 with references/fundamentals-checklist.md and a source-classes.md that maps each tool to its class; token-risk-screen 1.0.0 (each of the screen's eleven checks, what a failure means, what may be concluded); portfolio-construction 1.0.0 (weights from fair weights and depth, caps, cash, bands, exits, with data/params.json per level mirroring the envelope and the two-asset plan's brake and cost limit, and the target_portfolio@1 evals); usdc-wmon-band-rebalancer 1.1.0 with its data keyed by level; hermes/layers.ts names the twelve packages; skills-selection.ts gains two cases; the audit's S13 lets a placeholder address with 32 leading zeros pass (L-193).
- Tests (f152fd9, 2ed220e): the validator (the class rule, the per-token brief's refinements, the rationale's refinements), the pure position-call check, the stages' theme shape, the cycle on Postgres (the v2 briefs through the real gate, a proposal refused for its calls then accepted, the Dive's context token), the platform tools' schemas, the skills package (eight skills and four playbooks, every rule, the lock), the orchestrator's skills data against the policy and the domain, the built-in names and counts, the API's package list.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan pass. forge 555 passed, 0 failed, 11 skipped. vitest 2,195 passed in 167 files, 0 failed (node 1,426 in 115 files; forks 54 in 13 files, one file at a time, the keeper suite green; ui, console and web 715 in 39 files); the node project's exit code is 1 from the same unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts as before. `pnpm test:fork` 50 passed in 9 suites, the address book matching. Console e2e 114 passed with no baseline change. Web e2e not run (no web change). Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked. Nothing was sent to the playtest fork, whose head stayed at 109670015.

## 2026-10-10, P0-SPEND Model spending guardrails
Status: done. Real model spend is capped and visible: `pnpm spend:report` reads LiteLLM's own spend logs; every task runs under a 10 USD cap the gate enforces from LiteLLM's cost per call; the gateway carries a proxy-wide budget of 15 USD a day; scheduled research is off by default in local development; live checks run on the Low tier; CLAUDE.md carries the rule (D-369).
Summary: The report (evidence/p0-spend/REPORT.md) attributes 27.69 of the 28.93 USD spent since October 4 to the live checks' own keys, 20.20 USD of it on October 9 in eight live runs whose scheduled Scan and token check made 60 to 90 Haiku calls of about 30,000 prompt tokens each (1.3 to 2.9 USD per run); local development spent 0.17 USD, scripts 0.63, testnet agents 0.40.
- Spend report (14b642d, fe59ec0; scripts/spend-report.js, package.json): by day, by kind of caller, by model alias, by local task purpose, the costliest hours and calls, for any window (`--days=N`); dates as text; the root typecheck covers it.
- The task cap (3a73559; services/orchestrator gate.ts, cycle/cycle.ts, scan.ts, noop.ts, orchestrator.ts, gateway-admin.ts, live.ts, skills-selection.ts; scripts/live-pending.js): `leaseSpendUsd` sums LiteLLM's reported cost of a lease's model calls from the gate's own records and the gate answers a final 402 `task_cap` at 10 USD; a cycle halts with `TASK_CAP` keeping the stages before it, a Scan or no-op task ends with that reason; `globalSpend()` reads `/global/spend`; the live run stops between steps at the cap and reports its spend, its cycle runs on the Low tier; the selection check runs on `research-low` and sums the cost header; the pending-list runner reads the gateway's spend before and after each step and skips the rest at the cap. Tests: the gate's cap (two calls at 0.6 USD, the third refused, LiteLLM reached no more), a cycle stopped at the cap with the Scan standing and the Dive capped.
- The daily budget (3f76935; infra/litellm/config.yaml): `max_budget: 15`, `budget_duration: 1d`; the gateway recreated and `/global/spend` answering `max_budget 15`; a one-token call through it then cost 0.000015 USD, so the account accepts calls again and the budget leaves calls through.
- Scheduled research off (e6815a1; packages/config variables.ts, services/orchestrator main.ts, .env.example): `SCHEDULED_RESEARCH` (on or off, off locally); the orchestrator logs that scheduling is off and how to turn it on for a session.
- CLAUDE.md (2b25d22): the "Model spending" rule.
- The pending live list, first run after the account accepted calls (`pnpm test:live:pending`, 0.109 USD in total): step 1 the request shapes PASS (free); step 2 the live orchestrator run FAIL after 0.043 USD, at "the scheduler queues a Scan for each funded agent" because the live run's own orchestrator inherited the new default (L-195), and before that "at zero the next call is refused" failed because the drain call asked for `scan-cheap`, an alias the agent's key no longer serves since F-U7 (L-196); step 3 the skill selection PASS, 15 of 15 cases on every repeat on `research-low` for 0.065 USD, the two F-U8 skills chosen from the task alone (evidence/p3-u7/selection.json). Both live-run faults are fixed in F-U9's commits and the run is repeated there.

## 2026-10-10, F-U9 Portfolio proposals, the evaluator and the plan card
Status: done. The agent's research becomes a proposal the owner can see and approve. A Zoom out's `target_portfolio@1` draft (or a two-asset one) is stored as a proposal tied to the strategy epoch and the owner's epoch, with each position's weight, thesis, exit plan, sources by class and the skeptic's verdict; the evaluator runs it through the Test stage v2 and the goal's limits and estimates the cost to move before an owner sees it, and a rejected one is stored with every finding and never offered; the plan card on the web app shows it all with Approve and Decline; the goal's plan-change setting decides whether a proposal waits (ask first) or applies at once with the owner told, and the first plan after arming always waits; approval sets the plan the runner follows, re-checked first; "why the plan did not change" is stored and shown; the narrator writes entries for every event. The two follow-ups from F-U7 and F-U8 are done. Out of scope as the prompt says: the activation sweep (F-U10), the portfolio window (F-U11), the scheduler (P3-U10), any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: packages/trading holds the proposal store and the entry template; the orchestrator's proposal service builds and evaluates a proposal from the cycle's own records when the Zoom out ends; the control API serves the plan card and the owner's two actions, re-checking a portfolio through the orchestrator's Test v2 at approval time; the web app's portfolio page carries the card from the design system.
- Follow-ups (a65820a, 293e15e): the goal's brief names an excluded token by its symbol from the registry (the API passes symbols at save and preview; the e2e fake too); the console's skills fixture holds the twelve packages with the playbooks at 2.0.0.
- Storage and the evaluator (7932161; packages/db migrations/0024_plan_proposals.ts, schema.ts; packages/trading proposals.ts; services/orchestrator cycle/proposals.ts, cycle.ts, narrator.ts, orchestrator.ts, test-stage.ts, plan-routes.ts): `platform.plan_proposals` with the kind (proposal or no change), the epochs, the plan, the rationale, the positions, the evaluation, the status (pending_owner, applied, rejected, declined, expired, recorded), the decision and the plan it became; `ProposalService.fromZoomOut` reads the stage record's decision and the cycle's accepted briefs, builds each position (the RATIONALE's call, the Dive's thesis and confidence, its claims counted by class, the Challenge's verdict), evaluates (the template's shape, the Test v2, the goal's limits, `estimateMoveCost` with a `COST_HURDLE` finding above the plan's hurdle) and decides the status (ask first, the first plan after arming, or applied at once through the plan store as the agent); the narrator's `proposal` entries render from a template in packages/trading; the plan check route is open to the control API.
- The owner's routes (3837f7a; apps/control-api proposals.ts, app.ts, main.ts): `GET /v1/agents/:id/plan-card` (the plan in force with symbols, the proposal waiting, the recent decisions, the latest no change, the goal's setting), `POST .../proposals/:id/approve` (waiting, unexpired and from the current epochs; the evaluator again through `ORCHESTRATOR_URL`'s plan check for a portfolio or the goal's limits for the two-asset plan; a failing one stored as rejected with its findings and refused with `proposal_rejected`; a passing one set as the plan by the owner, bumping the strategy epoch) and `.../reject` (declined), owner only; each writes an activity entry; `not_configured` when no orchestrator is reachable.
- The plan card (5a8d92d, c821d24; packages/ui plan-card.tsx; apps/web agent/plan-card.ts, api/client.ts, agent/use-portfolio.ts, components/portfolio/portfolio-page.tsx, app/design/design-system.tsx; e2e fake-trading.ts, fake-api.ts, portfolio.spec.ts): `PlanCard` (the proposal against the current plan, each position's action, weights, thesis, exit plan, sources by class, confidence and verdict, the cash target both ways, the estimated cost, the platform's checks or findings, what would change it) and `NoChangeNotice`, on /design first; the portfolio page's "The agent's plan" section with Approve and Decline, the latest decided proposal when none waits, and the no-change notice; the e2e fake answers the card and the decisions.
- Tests (7932161, 3837f7a, 5a8d92d, b8076b5): the proposal store on Postgres (create, pending, decide once, expire, no change); the service's pure parts (sources by class, positions from the records, the cost estimate from cash, in band and leaving a token, the decision params); the cycle on Postgres (a NO_CHANGE recorded and narrated, a PROPOSE waiting under ask first, applied at once under apply and tell with the plan set by the agent); the control API (the card for the owner and 401/403 for others, approval re-checked and the plan set once, a failing re-check rejected with findings and `not_configured` without the orchestrator, the two-asset plan checked locally, declining, a stale proposal after a new goal); the card's component test; the web view helpers; the portfolio e2e at 1440 and 380 with an accessibility check (waiting with every field, approved, rejected with findings, declined, the no-change notice); the runner on a fork of its own: the plan arrives as a proposal approved by the owner with a real Test v2 re-check on the fork's routes, and the runner reaches the target through Executor v3.
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan pass. forge 555 passed, 0 failed, 11 skipped. vitest 2,216 passed in 171 files, 0 failed (node 1,440 in 117 files; forks 54 in 13 files, one file at a time; ui, console and web 722 in 41 files); the node project's exit code is 1 from the same unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts as before. `pnpm test:fork` 50 passed in 9 suites, the address book matching. Console e2e 114 passed in update mode and 114 again on the new baselines (the skills table with twelve packages on the agents page, the environment page with SCHEDULED_RESEARCH, checked by eye). Web e2e in update mode in two halves (104, then 110 and 32 after the decline test was split), then 104 and 114 on the new baselines (the two live specs skipped); four new captures (the plan card waiting and rejected at 1440 and 380) and four re-done (the design page with the plan card specimens, the saved goal naming WBTC), checked by eye. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked. Nothing was sent to the playtest fork, whose head stayed at 109670015.
- The live run (`pnpm test:orchestrator:live`, six attempts, the last with every fix): the sixth passed 52 checks and failed 2, for about 4.1 USD on the Low tier as the spend logs count it (the script's meter said 4.085 before the logs settled): mint, credits, the no-op task, the drain, the scheduler's Scans, the research check, discovery (27 tokens, 42 pools), the token check with a fresh screen, the chain check, the trade flow (the owner's first approval armed the agent and the swap settled through the v3 set), and a full research cycle whose Zoom out decided NO_CHANGE (NO_MATERIAL_CHANGE), stored for the plan card with its reason (the new F-U9 check passed). The two failures are the first Dive ending at its turn cap without complete_stage on the Low tier, so "each stage ended with complete_stage" and "every stage stayed within its caps" failed; the cycle went on and completed. A live proposal on the plan card is still to be seen: the Low tier's Zoom out chose no change on a fresh account, so the check moves to the pending list (row 6). Reports: evidence/p1-u7/live-2026-10-10T23-56-57.040Z.json and the five before it.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-194 (a piped secrets scan let a commit through; two false positives recorded by fingerprint), L-195 (the scheduler default broke the live check that proves it), L-196 (the drain call's old alias), L-197 (a discovery request held open past the fetch timeout), L-198 (the live fork lacked the v3 set), L-199 (the live run's 15-second Scan cadence kept running and the spend meters read the gateway before its logs settled).
- Model spend of this session, from `pnpm spend:report` (the figure of record): 16.76 USD, all on the live checks' own keys: 12.70 USD on October 10 UTC from 21:48 and 4.06 USD on October 11 UTC to 00:22, in six attempts of the live run (2.77, 2.94, 2.98, 3.85 and about 4.1 USD for attempts 2 to 6, the first under 0.1) plus 0.07 USD for the dry run and the selection check. The scripts' own meters had reported 6.13 USD because the gateway's spend logs lag the calls (L-199); most of the difference is the 7 to 11 scheduled Scans each attempt ran at the 15-second cadence the scheduler check sets, now stopped after the check. No single task exceeded the 10 USD cap (a Scan is 0.3 to 0.5 USD, the cycle 1.6), and the gateway's 15 USD day was not reached (12.70 on October 10 UTC).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 was never stopped or reset and is at head 109670015. dev:all was stopped for the heavy suites and restarted with this unit's code (migration 0024 runs at the services' start; scheduled research is off unless SCHEDULED_RESEARCH=on). Postgres, Redis and LiteLLM are up, LiteLLM recreated with the 15 USD daily budget.
Notes for the owner:
- The Low tier's Dives ran to their turn cap in the live cycle (the 2.0 checklist with 16 turns on Haiku); the Zoom out then chose no change on a fresh account. The Medium tier, or more Dive turns on the Low tier, is the first thing to try before Playtest T; this is F-T1 tuning (Q-60).
- The scheduled Scans outside a cycle also ended without complete_stage twice in the second run (16 model calls each on Haiku); with scheduled research off locally this spends nothing, and P3-U10 revises the scheduled Scan.
- A-77 records the values chosen where the plan was silent; say if any should change.
Suggestions:
- Make `POST /v1/tokens/discover` answer at once and let the console poll the run, as the live check now does.
- Show the proposal's expiry countdown and the declined and expired ones in a small history on the plan card.

## 2026-10-11, F-U10 Activation sweep with the step panel (with the F-U9 follow-ups)
Status: done. Turning on automatic trading runs one full research cycle before the agent trades anything, and the owner watches it activate step by step. The owner's wallet registers the trading permission, the orchestrator runs every check and names the one that fails, the ACTIVATION cycle of P3-U4 runs as the owner's task on the goal's tier within the ceiling shown before it starts and the 10 USD cap, the portfolio page follows it every two seconds through the step panel (each step's result and cost against its ceiling), the sweep's plan arrives on the F-U9 plan card and always waits for the owner, and approving it is what arms the agent (D-291). A sweep that fails, stops at a limit or runs out of credits says what happened and what the owner can do. On /configure the bee rests on its platform, takes off and hovers with its wing cue in the accent while the sweep runs and lands when it ends; a species shown in 2D pulses. The two F-U9 follow-ups are done. Out of scope as the prompt says: the portfolio window (F-U11), the scheduler and triggers (P3-U10), reusing a failed sweep's results under an hour old and stopping a sweep from the page (A-78), the compact panel on the agent's card.
Summary: packages/trading holds the activation record (migration 0025) and the pure rules; the orchestrator's activation service runs the checks, queues the cycle and records the sweep, with two hooks in the cycle engine; the control API's owner routes read and start the sweep through the orchestrator and its approval arms the agent; packages/ui holds the panel, the checks, the status tag and the 2D pulse; the web app's portfolio page and portal carry the flow; one live sweep on the Medium tier passed every check.
- Step 0 (b826abf, 937c52c): a test of its own for the rule that the first plan after an arming began always waits for the owner (ProposalService.approvalNeeded with fakes, every branch); the discovery route answers at once with the run it started (202, `runId`, `started`) and `GET /v1/tokens/discovery/:runId` serves the run, so no request is held open for a pass that pages GeckoTerminal at its rate limit (L-197); the console's button watches the run every three seconds until it ends, and the live run watches the run it was given by ID.
- The record and the rules (79d2f46; packages/db migrations/0025_activation.ts, schema.ts; packages/trading activation.ts): `platform.activations` keeps one row per attempt (the arming, the cycle and its task, the tier, the ceiling, the facts of the two deterministic steps) and the activity kind `activation`; `activationChecks` names every check with one line (the goal, provisioning, the account on the fund agent's set, its mode, A-53's 5 USDC trading minimum from the account's NAV, the credits against the ceiling, the grant as a step to come rather than a block, one sweep and one cycle at a time); `activationSteps` derives the eight steps from the cycle's stage runs, the activation's facts and the plan proposal, every result a fixed line over records; `activationStatus` and `activationOutcome` give the status (running, plan ready, active, off, declined, expired, no plan, failed, capped, out of credits) with a title, a message and the action (approve, try again, add credits).
- The orchestrator (b3b9ebe; services/orchestrator activation.ts, cycle/cycle.ts, narrator.ts, cycle/config.ts, orchestrator.ts, api.ts; packages/trading store.ts): `ActivationService.start` runs the checks, requires the grant on chain, records the sweep and queues the ACTIVATION cycle as the owner's task; the cycle engine gains `hooks.afterScan` (the deterministic pools-and-prices step: the registry's tokens, prices, pools and fresh screens through the Test v2's inputs; a failed read halts the sweep with `MARKET_UNAVAILABLE`) and `hooks.onEnd`; the narrator writes entries for the start, both deterministic steps and the result from `ActivationFacts` with a plain summary sentence, held to the number validator; the Zoom out's activation prompt says the first plan is expected unless the evidence is weak; `markArmedByPlan` arms an arming with no first intent; `GET` and `POST /v1/agents/:id/activation` on the internal API.
- The control API (fa57f70; apps/control-api activation.ts, app.ts, proposals.ts, main.ts): the owner's `GET /v1/agents/:id/activation` (the newest sweep with its steps, status and outcome, and the checks before the next; `available: false` where no orchestrator is reachable, so the page keeps the P2-U6 arming alone) and `POST` (turn automatic trading on, each refusal named by its check), both through `ORCHESTRATOR_URL` like the plan card's re-check; `approveProposal` arms the agent when the proposal is the activation sweep's own and answers `armed`.
- Design system (6765359; packages/ui activation.tsx, species-art.tsx, viewer-frame.tsx; apps/web design-system.tsx): `ActivationPanel` (the eight steps with live states, each step's result and cost against its ceiling, the running step's elapsed time, the sweep's cost against the ceiling, the outcome with the owner's actions), `ActivationChecks`, `ActivationStatusTag`, the `pulse` ring on `SpeciesArt`, and the viewer frame's top row that wraps on a narrow frame; every state on /design.
- The web app (32f3bb0; apps/web api/client.ts, agent/activation-flow.ts, use-portfolio.ts, use-activation.ts, components/portfolio/portfolio-page.tsx, portal/agent-portal.tsx, viewer/flight.ts, animated-model.tsx, agent-stage.tsx, agent-scene.tsx; e2e fake-trading.ts, fake-api.ts, portfolio.spec.ts, portal.spec.ts): the arming card shows the checks and the ceiling with "Turn on automatic trading", which registers the grant from the wallet (the P2-U6 flow, every state shown) and then asks the platform to start; the page re-reads every two seconds while the sweep runs; the panel's outcome carries "Read the plan", "Try again" (a whole new sweep) and "Add credits"; the portal reads the sweep's status with the owner session, the viewer wears it, the bee flies only while it runs (`wantsFlight`, `flightState`) and lands when it ends, a 2D species pulses, and reduced motion keeps everything grounded.
- Tests (every commit): trading (the checks, the steps in every state, the status and outcome, the store on Postgres); the orchestrator (the service on Postgres with every refusal, the start, both hooks and the entries; the scripted cycle with the hooks in order and a halted sweep; the API routes; the narrator's templates); the control API (owner-only routes, the proxy, the refusal, the approval that arms); the UI components; the web flow and the flight decision; the web e2e at 1440 and 380 with accessibility checks (the checks before the sweep, turning it on with the steps advancing live, the plan's approval arming the agent, a sweep out of credits with its retry, a failing check named with nothing sent, the viewer during a sweep with the tag and the pulse and no overlap of the frame's tags); the live sweep.
- The live sweep (1eb9a7d; `pnpm test:activation:live`, evidence/f-u10): on its own stack (a fork on 8551 with AgentNFT, the v2 factory, the fund set and Executor v3; a throwaway database; the indexer; the orchestrator as a child on 4251 with scheduled research and token loops off) the owner's mint, 8 USDC of credits, a Balanced goal on the Medium tier, a PersonalAccountV3 with 90 USDC and the grant. First attempt (live-2026-10-11T02-43-28): the sweep ran Scan, pools and prices, two Dives, Challenge, Test and Zoom out in 286 s for 0.81 USDC of the 3.80 ceiling and ended with a plan waiting for the owner, whose approval armed the agent and set it RUNNING; 9 of 11 checks passed, the two failures being the script's own (an order check tripped by the Dives step running once per theme, and a leg expected of a cash-only plan) in a stack with no safety screens, so the skeptic rejected both theses. Second attempt (live-2026-10-11T02-59-56), with the six core tokens screened first (five passed, WBTC refused): the sweep ran in order with never two steps at once in 273 s for 0.77 USDC (Scan 0.15 of 0.50, Dives 0.42 of 2.40, Challenge 0.07 of 0.30, Zoom out 0.13 of 0.60), the skeptic again rejected both theses (AUSD yield, MON's volatility regime) and the Zoom out proposed a plan of cash only, which waited for the owner; approving it passed the Test v2 re-check, armed the agent and set it RUNNING, the runner held with BELOW_MIN_TRADE and sent nothing; 13 of 13 checks passed, every activity entry passing the number validator. A live plan with positions is still to be seen (evidence/live-pending.md row 6).
- Checks on the final tree: format, lint, typecheck, forge fmt and the secrets scan pass. forge 555 passed, 0 failed, 11 skipped. vitest: node 1,466 passed in 119 files (the exit code 1 is the known unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts); forks 54 passed in 13 files, one file at a time; ui, console and web 671 passed in 36 files. `pnpm test:fork` 50 passed in 9 suites, the address book matching. Console e2e 114 passed with no baseline change. Web e2e: the three touched specs 80 passed in update mode and 80 again on the new baselines; the full suite 226 passed with the 2 live specs skipped and no baseline change. Twelve new captures (the checks before the sweep, the sweep running, the plan ready, a sweep out of credits, the viewer during a sweep, at 1440 and 380) and three re-done (the design page at both widths, the no-WebGL portal at 380, where the frame's tools now wrap under the badge) checked by eye. Heavy suites ran one at a time with dev:all stopped and memory checked. Nothing was sent to the playtest fork, whose head stayed at 109670015.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-201 (the activation tag and the viewer's tools overlapped at 380 pixels), L-202 (slips caught by the unit's own checks, including a `git checkout --` on two regenerated baselines against the git safety rule).
- Model spend of this session (from `pnpm spend:report`, the figure of record): 1.25 USD, the two live sweeps (0.63 and 0.62 USD as the scripts measured them), all on the live checks' own keys; no task came near the 10 USD cap and the day stayed inside the gateway's 15 USD budget (5.31 USD on 2026-10-11 UTC in all).
- Dev state: the playtest fork on 8545 was never stopped or reset. dev:all was stopped for the heavy suites and restarted with this unit's code (migration 0025 runs at the services' start). Postgres, Redis and LiteLLM are up.
Notes for the owner:
- On the playtest fork, agent 1's portfolio page now offers "Turn on automatic trading" once a goal is saved and the account holds at least 5 USDC: the checks are listed first with the sweep's ceiling (3.80 USDC on the Medium tier). The sweep costs real model credits; the two live sweeps cost about 0.62 USD each.
- Both live sweeps ended with a cash-only plan: on a fresh account the Medium tier's skeptic rejected every thesis the Dives raised. The plan is honest, and the runner holds on it, but the demo wants a plan with positions; the Dive and Challenge prompts are F-T1's tuning (Q-60).
- A-78 records the values chosen where the plan was silent (the ceiling as the engine enforces it, the grant before the start, a retry as a whole new sweep, no stop from the page, the bee grounded at rest); say if any should change.
- The gateway's `/global/spend` reads the all-time total (45.69 USD), not the day's window; `pnpm spend:report --days=1` is the day's figure.
Suggestions:

- The compact panel on the agent's card on My Agents (P3-U8's form).
