## 2026-10-11, F-U11 Portfolio window
Status: done. The owner opens a window on what the agent holds and how it is performing, in numbers they can trust. Every held token is listed with its amount, value where priced, share of the priced total, cost basis and gain or loss, with the allocation across every position as a donut; performance over time is read on the account's own value per unit, which every value snapshot now records, so a deposit or a withdrawal never counts as a gain or a loss, and the chart draws what $100 put in at the start would be worth with every trade marked on it; the three scores (the return since the start, the largest fall from a peak, the share of closed positions that made money) each say "not enough data yet" and why while the history is too short; "$100 at the start would be worth $X now" stands beside holding USDC; the current plan, the recent trades and "why the agent did not trade" are a link away on the portfolio page. Out of scope as the prompt says: live checks (none were needed; no model was called), the scheduler (P3-U10), vaults (P7), any testnet or mainnet transaction.
Summary: packages/trading holds the measure (migration 0026 adds the value per unit to every snapshot) and the pure window rules; the orchestrator reads an account live for the window; the control API's owner-only window route builds it from the snapshots, the intents and the live reading; packages/ui holds the table, the donut, the chart and the tiles on /design first; the web app's window page carries them, linked from the portfolio page and the My Agents card.
- The measure and the math (25ee348; packages/db migrations/0026_snapshot_per_unit.ts, schema.ts, index.ts; packages/trading snapshots.ts, window.ts; services/orchestrator snapshots.ts): `platform.account_snapshots.per_unit_e18` carries the breaker's value per unit with every reading the recorder makes (v2 and v3 alike; null while the breaker's price is unusable), and `SnapshotStore.since` reads a window oldest first; `perUnitSeries` (the readings with a value per unit, thinned to 300 points keeping the first, the last and every trade's reading), `returnSinceStart`, `maxDrawdown`, `hundredDollars` (two decimals truncated, L-94), each "not enough data yet" under two readings or under an hour of history; `tradeMarks` from the settled intents (a buy when USDC was sold, with the USDC each way), `closedPositions` and `winShare`; `holdingLines` (value, share of the priced total, cost basis, gain or loss, cash never a gain, an unpriced token kept with its share unknown).
- The reads (3e44943, d6f9c4c; packages/trading window.ts; services/orchestrator snapshots.ts, api.ts; apps/control-api window.ts, app.ts, main.ts): `SnapshotRecorder.read` reads an account without recording it (a v3 account with every held token as the account values it, a v2 account with its two balances) and `observe` records that reading; `GET /v1/agents/:id/account` on the orchestrator serves it (503 without chain tools, 404 without an account); `buildWindow` assembles the window from the snapshots, the intents and the live reading, which joins the chart as its last point, with the latest snapshot standing in, dated as such, when the orchestrator does not answer (a v2 snapshot as its two balances); the control API's `GET /v1/agents/:id/window`, owner only, reads at most 4,000 snapshots and 500 intents and asks the orchestrator through `ORCHESTRATOR_URL` like the sweep.
- Design system (d1c56b3; packages/ui styles.css, tokens.ts, components/agent/window.tsx; apps/web design-system.tsx): six chart series tokens (`chart-1` to `chart-6`) whose hexes passed the dataviz skill's validator on the dark surface (lightness, chroma, adjacent-pair colour-blind separation, the normal-vision floor, contrast); `AssetMakeupTable` (stacked on a phone, the total and when the holdings were read in its caption), `AllocationDonut` (USDC steel, up to five tokens with a colour that follows the token, the rest as Other, each named with its share, a 2px gap between slices, the unpriced named under it), `PerformanceChart` (one 2px line with a 10% wash, hairline gridlines, a marker per trade ringed in the surface, a buy pointing up and a sale down, a crosshair and tooltip on hover, the readings as a table on request, "not enough data yet" under two readings), `ScoreTiles` and `HundredDollarTile` with their waiting states; every state on /design.
- The web app (4f34be3; apps/web api/client.ts, agent/use-window.ts, components/window/window-page.tsx, app/agents/[id]/window/page.tsx, components/portfolio/portfolio-page.tsx, components/my-agents/my-agents-page.tsx): `/agents/[id]/window` for the owner (the wallet gates as on the portfolio page, loading, an error with Try again, "no trading account yet" pointing to the portfolio, then the $100 tile, the scores, the chart, the makeup with the donut, when and how the holdings were read with Refresh, and the three links to the portfolio page's plan, trades and why sections, which gain their anchors); the portfolio page's header and the My Agents card link to it; the hook re-reads every minute.

## 2026-10-11, F-U11 (fix after playtest), The portfolio page on the fund agent's set
Status: done. Found in the playtest: agent 1's portfolio page opened a v2 PersonalAccount and took 100 USDC into it, while the automatic trading card checked the v3 account and asked the owner to open the trading account and deposit first. Now, wherever the fund agent's set is deployed, every section of the portfolio page uses the PersonalAccountV3: opening the account on AccountFactoryV3, deposits and withdrawals of any registered token, the positions and the allocation across every held token, and the caps from the v3 factory; the owner's v2 account shows as an older account with one "Withdraw all to your wallet"; the owner's wallet is on the v3 factory's allowlist on the local fork; and no script or test can send as a real wallet on the playtest fork. Nothing else changed: the sweep, the plan card and the window stay as F-U10 and F-U11 built them.
Diagnosis (which account each part read or wrote, for an agent on the v3 path, before the fix): the portfolio read (`GET /v1/agents/:id/portfolio`), the open-account card, the deposit and withdraw forms, the positions panel, the allocation chart and bar, the caps panel, the claimables and the My Agents card's trading summary all read the v2 account through the control API's v2 reader and the v2 factory (USDC and WMON only, the v2 caps); the D-315 holdings list read the v2 account as "Trading account"; the activation card, the plan card and the window read the v3 set through the orchestrator (the activation checks' account and trading balance, the Test v2 inputs, the window's live reading). The custody path readers (control API and chain tools) put an agent with only a v2 account on v2 (D-367), so once the page had opened a v2 account the platform's two halves could never agree: the orchestrator's activation facts read the v3 reader regardless and found no account.
Summary: packages/trading holds the v3 portfolio reading; the control API serves it wherever the set is deployed; packages/ui gained the fund overview and the older account card; the web app's portfolio page branches on the reading's custody; the dev tooling guards the playtest fork and allowlists both factories.

- The control API (4c8ebec, 3124cdd; apps/control-api app.ts, main.ts): the portfolio route serves the v3 reading where the set is deployed (the depositable tokens from `platform.tokens`), the v2 reading named `custody: "v2"` elsewhere; the holdings read takes the v3 factory.

## 2026-10-11, F-T1 Step 0 of the P3-U10 and P4-U2 session: research calibration, the commit hook, the live portfolio spec
Status: done. (1) Research calibration (A-81): the Challenge's verdicts scale with the evidence (REJECTED only for a broken thesis, WEAKENED for moderate evidence, which still earns a smaller position); each aggressiveness level carries an expected invested share (`envelope.investedRangeBps`: Conservative 30% to 70%, Balanced 50% to 85%, Aggressive 65% to 90%) into the goal block and the briefs; a Zoom out that proposes outside the range, or all cash, must say why in the RATIONALE's `allocationNote`, refused at `complete_stage` until it does; the playbooks are 2.1.0 and locked; the plan card shows the note. Proven with recorded model responses (the scripted cycle tests refuse a cash-only plan without the note and a plan outside the range without it) and with one live Medium-tier Balanced sweep. (2) The commit gate is a versioned pre-commit hook (`.githooks/pre-commit`, installed by `pnpm prepare`) that runs the secrets scan and refuses the commit on any failure (L-203, L-205), proven by a refused probe after a first probe that used gitleaks' own example key and went through (L-207). (3) The live portfolio spec moved to the v3 page and its test stack deploys the fund set.
The calibrated live sweep (0.89 USDC of a 3.80 USDC ceiling, 281 seconds): the Scan found two themes (MON_ECOSYSTEM_DIVERGENCE, WMON_CORE_ALLOCATION), both Dives ran, the Challenge returned WEAKENED verdicts rather than REJECTED, and the Zoom out proposed a one-position plan in WMON with the rest in USDC, its allocation note saying why the account stays under the Balanced range on a first plan with mixed evidence; the plan went to the owner, the approval re-check passed and armed the agent; the runner's first leg (USDC to WMON) then reverted on chain while the replay passed, since the fork's feeds had gone stale over the sweep's minutes (L-206); the live script now re-dates them before the leg. The sweep's costs: Scan 0.19 of 0.50, Dives 0.49 of 2.40, Challenge 0.08 of 0.30, plan 0.14 of 0.60 USDC. Reports: `evidence/f-u10/live-2026-10-11T06-30-23.170Z.json` (the first attempt, stopped by the wallet guard before the fix that keys it on the playtest fork's port alone) and `evidence/f-u10/live-2026-10-11T06-35-25.632Z.json`.
- Commits: 00092fc (calibration), 90925a2 and e6019d8 (the probe and its removal), f5fe6f9 (the hook, the live spec, the guard), dc7c4fe (the live script's feed refresh).
- Model spend of Step 0: about 0.70 USD for the calibrated sweep (the day's total at its end 6.674 USD).

## 2026-10-11, P3-U10 Autonomous research loop, the Research page and the agent terminal
Status: done. After activation an agent researches on its own: the scheduler runs cycles at the goal's intensity (Scans every 12, 6 or 3 hours, the daily review, the weekly overview at Standard and Deep) under the day's budget and says why it waits; the watcher's free triggers (MON's move by level, volatility across the brake, the mode, a flow of a fifth, the goal, a trade blocked three times, a thesis at its recheck, a held token's move, liquidity halved, a failed re-screen) start a review or a Scan, each once and two hours apart (A-82). The loop runs for RUNNING agents only and replaces D-216's scheduled Scans; it stays off locally unless SCHEDULED_RESEARCH=on (D-369). The owner's Research page shows the agent terminal (a live, monospace feed of one plain line per event, with history after the cycle), the schedule with pause and resume, the budget and the triggers, the cycles with each stage's cost against its ceiling, the market overview, the last Scan's themes, the watcher's snapshot, why the plan did not change and why the agent did not trade; the terminal also runs inside the activation step panel while a sweep runs. The console's cycles page gained the playtest tools: pause and resume, the fork's fast cadence (minutes), a trigger fired by hand, one pass of the loop now, and the market mover (MON/USD on the LocalFeed by a share, the local fork only).
Summary: the orchestrator holds the loop and the terminal; the control API serves them to the owner alone; packages/ui holds the components; the web app the page; the console the tools.
- The review cycle and the tables (09e5bd9; packages/db migration 0027; services/orchestrator cycle/{cycle,config,store,research,stages}.ts): the REVIEW kind (the Test and the Zoom out alone), a cycle's wish for a fresh overview carried to the prompt and required at the stage's end, `platform.research_schedules` (one row per agent) and `platform.research_triggers`.
- The loop (fcab0b3; research-loop.ts, research-wiring.ts, research-routes.ts, orchestrator.ts, cycle/store.ts `lastStages`, main.ts): `decideWork` and `nextDue` (pure, over the cycle records and a clock), `detectTriggers` and `baselineFrom` (pure, each condition once against the baseline), `ResearchStore`, `ResearchScheduler` and `Watcher` with injectable readers; the routes `GET /v1/agents/:id/research`, `POST .../research/pause`, and for the dev console `POST .../research/cadence`, `POST .../research/triggers/:code`, `POST /v1/research/tick`.
- The terminal (043c4af; terminal.ts): `terminalEvents` turns a cycle's stage runs, tool calls, briefs, records and proposals into events in time order with stable sequence numbers; `guard` withholds a line that carries playbook text (eight-word runs of the mounted skills), the canary, a link, or a number not in its record; sources show as domains and tool names; `GET /v1/agents/:id/terminal?cycleId=&after=` serves from a cursor, the open cycle by default.
- The Research read and the mover (dccfc37; research-routes.ts, local-feeds.ts, packages/devenv local-feeds.ts `moveLocalFeed`): the latest overview, the last Scan's themes, the last review that kept the plan, the recent cycles with their stages' costs and the watcher's snapshot, every brief text through the terminal's guard; `POST /v1/dev/market/move` on the local fork only.
- The control API (d36e4a9; research.ts, app.ts, main.ts): the three owner-only routes through the orchestrator, 503 without it.
- Design system (e98022d; packages/ui research.tsx, activation.tsx; apps/web design-system.tsx): `AgentTerminal` (connecting, live, ended, reconnecting from its last line, nothing yet; withheld lines marked), `ResearchScheduleCard`, `BudgetMeter`, `TriggerList`, `StageTimeline`, `ResearchOverview`, `ThemesPanel`, `MarketSnapshotPanel`, `PlanUnchangedCard`; the activation panel's `terminal` slot; every state on /design.
- The web app (633e73b, ac950c9; api/client.ts, agent/use-research.ts, components/research/research-page.tsx, app/agents/[id]/research, components/portfolio/{activation-terminal,portfolio-page}.tsx): `useTerminal` reads from the last sequence it has (a dropped read resumes there; a stack without the orchestrator is "nothing yet", not a dropped connection); the page; the terminal in the activation panel once a cycle is open; the links from the portfolio.
- The console (aab6489; cycles/research-{actions,controls}.ts(x), page.tsx, e2e fixture): the playtest tools on the cycles page.
- Tests: the loop's decisions and triggers with fast clocks and over Postgres (25); the terminal's events, ordering, the reconnect prefix, the web-content rule, the deliberate leak attempts (playbook text in a query, a brief and a refused brief's reason; the canary; a link; an invented number) (6); the control API's owner-only and pass-through (2); the components (8); the web e2e at 1440 and 380 with accessibility checks (live following from the cursor, a dropped read resumed without a repeated line, history of a finished cycle, pause and resume, the empty page, another wallet, no orchestrator, the terminal inside the activation panel); the market mover and the price trigger on a fork of their own (1).
- Checks on the final tree: typecheck, lint (eslint) and format (prettier) clean; forge fmt clean; vitest node 1527 passed in 124 files (one pre-existing unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts's fake sandbox, present in the F-U11 fix's check log before this session, makes vitest exit 1 while every test passes); vitest ui, console and web 789 passed in 48 files; Playwright web 262 passed (6.2m) at 1440 and 380 with the design, portfolio, portfolio-v3, goal and research captures refreshed (the design page's new section, the portfolio header's Research link, A-81's invested-share sentence in the goal briefs, the research page on a fixed clock); Playwright console 114 passed (1.7m) with the cycles and agents captures refreshed (the Research loop card; the playbooks at 2.1.0); vitest forks 55 passed in 14 files, every transaction mined; each heavy suite run alone with the dev stack stopped.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-208 (the re-screen trigger), L-209 (the review before any Scan, the withheld check line, the test database's name).

Notes for the owner:
- The loop runs only with SCHEDULED_RESEARCH=on and for RUNNING agents; on the playtest stack turn it on for a session to watch it, or drive it from the console's cycles page (a pass now, the fast cadence, a trigger by hand, the market mover).
- D-216's scheduled Scans no longer run: a provisioned agent without an approved plan researches only when its owner starts a cycle.

- Close the scan test's fake sandbox before its lease ends, so vitest's node run exits 0 again (the unhandled rejection predates this session).

## 2026-10-11, P4-U2 Risk Sentinel service
Status: done. A deterministic service in the orchestrator, no model, on its own sentinel key (D-138): every pass it pokes each agent's account (so the breaker's 10% and 20% trips happen), reads every held token's feed, screen and liquidity, the account's fall from its 7-day peak, the agent's credits, gas, signer, chain, Executor, permission and owner, and tightens once per condition: reduce-only, pause new risk or close deposits through the key, and the agent state RESTRICTED (no credits) or INCIDENT (an operational fault that needs a human) with the breach's reason (A-83). It never unpauses, loosens, moves funds or touches a withdrawal: its wallet knows four functions, and the core refuses it the rest. Every action is recorded with its reason and transaction, shown to the owner on the Research page's sentinel card, and appears in the agent terminal as its own lines. The console's cycles page gained a pass now, the feed drill (MON/USD stalled or restored) and an operator's clearing after review; the market mover from P3-U10 drives the drawdown drills.
Summary: services/orchestrator holds the service (sentinel.ts: the rules as pure functions, the store, the service; sentinel-chain.ts: the key's wallet; sentinel-wiring.ts: the readers and the state setter; sentinel-routes.ts); packages/db migration 0028; the control API serves the view to the owner; packages/ui holds the card; the web page shows it; the console drives it.
- The service (sentinel.ts, sentinel-wiring.ts, sentinel-chain.ts, orchestrator.ts, main.ts): `assess` over an observation, `stateFor`, `tightens`; `Sentinel.tick` over every agent with a goal; the key from anvil's mnemonic (index 5) locally and `TESTNET_SENTINEL_PRIVATE_KEY` on testnet; the signer worker's heartbeat (`alive`) for the signer check; `platform.sentinel_actions` and `platform.sentinel_watch`.
- The terminal (terminal.ts, research-routes.ts): the sentinel's actions in a cycle's window as "Sentinel set the account to reduce-only (sales only): 12.5% below the 7-day peak." lines, their numbers from the record.
- The routes (sentinel-routes.ts, api.ts; apps/control-api research.ts, app.ts): `GET /v1/agents/:id/sentinel` (the orchestrator and, owner-only, the control API), `POST .../sentinel/clear` (operator), `POST /v1/sentinel/tick` and `POST /v1/dev/market/feed` (dev console); the feed drill through the local feed refresher (`stallMon`).
- Design system and web (packages/ui sentinel.tsx; apps/web design-system.tsx, api/client.ts, research-page.tsx): `SentinelCard` (calm, tightened with the conditions in force and the actions, not configured), on /design and the Research page with a link to the portfolio.
- The console (cycles/sentinel-{actions,controls}.ts(x), page.tsx, e2e fixture): the tools above.
- Tests: the rules (6, every breach and the state and tightening rules), the service over Postgres with a fake key (5: once per condition and in order, cleared noted and never loosened, a breach the breaker already answered, the states with the resume on credits, no key), the terminal's sentinel lines (1), the control API's route (in the research tests), the card (3), the drills on a fork of their own (4: the key is the factory's sentinel; 12% sets reduce-only with the owner's withdrawal still working; 22% pauses, withdrawals work and the key cannot unpause; a stalled feed is an incident and clears without loosening).
- Checks on the final tree: typecheck, lint (eslint), format (prettier) and forge fmt clean; vitest node 1539 passed in 125 files (the pre-existing unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts's fake sandbox still makes vitest exit 1 while every test passes); vitest ui, console and web 796 passed in 49 files; Playwright web 262 passed of 264 at 1440 and 380 with the research and design captures refreshed (the sentinel card); Playwright console 114 passed with the cycles captures refreshed (the sentinel card); vitest forks 59 passed in 15 files including the sentinel's four drills, every transaction mined; each heavy suite run alone in the foreground with the dev stack stopped.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-210 (the drill's stimulus on the threshold), L-211 (a stalled feed hides the drawdown condition).
- Model spend of this unit: none; no model was called.
- Dev state: pnpm dev:all stopped for the heavy suites and restarted at the session's end with the sentinel on (anvil account 5 as its key); the playtest fork on 8545 untouched throughout; the drills ran on a fork of their own (port 8563).
Notes for the owner:
- The sentinel runs on the dev stack from the next `pnpm dev:all` with anvil account 5 as its key; on the playtest fork a condition it sees will tighten agent 1's account and the state shows on the Research page. The owner lifts a mode from their wallet (`unpause`); the web app has no button for it yet (a suggestion below).
- The plan's drills "kill the bot runner" and "expire the key" are covered by the signer-down and mandate-expired rules; "remove credits" by the RESTRICTED rule; "delay the feed" by the stalled-feed drill; "transfer the agent" by the owner-changed rule; "disable an adapter" by the Executor and adapter rule. Each is a unit test over an observation; the feed and drawdown drills also run on a fork.
Suggestions:
- An "Unpause after review" action on the portfolio page (the owner's own transaction), with the sentinel's reasons beside it.
- Notifications (P4-U7) for each sentinel action.
- The sentinel's gas check uses the signer's swap cost; a per-chain floor would do for agents without a signer.

## 2026-10-11, F-U12 Price attestor, class A live and the open universe
Status: done. The owner's decision recorded (D-370): F-U12 is required, so the agent can trade any token that passes the safety screen, feed or not. The price attestor signs a class A token's price (the median of the chain's own pool quoted both ways and GeckoTerminal's price, at least two fresh sources within 5%, valid 60 seconds) with its own key, which moves no funds; the AttestationVerifier checks the EIP-712 signature on chain and the TokenRegistry takes it through its own 9-day timelock (SET_VERIFIER), which `pnpm deploy:attestor` drives on the local fork's clock; Executor v3 trades class A with the attestation at 1% slippage against it, the 15% and 50% cost-basis caps holding with a wrong price; a passing screen registers a token in the screened lane at once by the platform's registrar on the screener key, with its routable pools, and a refused re-screen moves it to sell-only; the owner opts in on chain from the Goal page with their own wallet, and the brief and the goals tool describe the lane as the chain records it; every token list, lookup, screen result and price list tells the agent the lane and whether this account may buy it and why not; every cycle records the universe it considered (each token's buyability and reason, why each theme was or was not dived, why each token seen was not proposed), shown on the Research page's "Universe considered" panel and in the agent terminal; the Research page's sentinel card has the owner's "Unpause after review"; the goal envelope and the Scan playbook's limits match what the Executor and the task caps allow; vaults stay class F only (D-355, the forge test).
Summary: chains/monad (AttestationVerifier.sol, its test, DeployAttestor.s.sol); scripts (lib/attestor.js, deploy-attestor.js); packages/config (the testnet attestor and screener keys), market (tokenPriceUsd), domain (laneSentence, the 40% envelope), policy (briefOf), trading (briefOnChain, screenedOptIn in LiveGoalLimits and the portfolio reading), db (migration 0029 research_universe), ui (UniversePanel), skills (playbook-scan 2.1.0, playbook-zoom-out 2.3.0, portfolio-construction 1.0.1); services/chain-tools (whyNotBuyableV3, the attested side and hop in the Executor mirror, the pool price and deviation limit in the market read, the class A floor), data-tools and platform-tools (buyability in every token tool, notProposed, screenedOptInOnChain), orchestrator (attestor.ts, attestor-sources.ts, attested-reader.ts with the ChainClock, tokens/registrar.ts and buyability.ts, cycle/universe.ts, the terminal's universe lines, the research routes, the goals tool, the sentinel read, live-activation.ts --open-universe, class-a.fork.test.ts); apps/control-api (the goal's chain state); apps/web (use-account-tx.ts, the Goal page's onchain step, the Research page's panel and unpause, /design); the console's skills fixture.
- The attestor (attestor.ts, attestor-sources.ts, main.ts): `Attestor.attest` signs the EIP-712 attestation over `(chainId, token, priceE18, observedAt, validUntil)` for the registry's verifier, refuses with TOO_FEW_SOURCES, SOURCES_DISAGREE, NO_VERIFIER or NOT_CLASS_A, reuses one for 20 seconds; its clock is the chain's (ChainClock, L-213); anvil account 2 locally, TESTNET_ATTESTOR_PRIVATE_KEY on testnet.
- The verifier (AttestationVerifier.sol): immutable attestor, MAX_LIFE 60 seconds, `verify(bytes)` recovers the signer; OracleAdapterV3.attestedPriceE18 refuses an expired or foreign attestation; the forge tests set it through the timelock and prove a vault never buys a screened class A token.
- The registrar (tokens/registrar.ts, registry.ts, store.ts): after a screen, a passing unregistered token joins the screened lane with the 15% cap and its routable pools against listed tokens (v4 hookless pools included), a registered one has its screen recorded, a refused screened token goes sell-only, a core token is left to the guardian; the token table mirrors what was written and the chain's lanes (L-216); the screen is stamped by the chain's clock (L-213).
- Buyability (chain-tools logic-v3.ts, orchestrator tokens/buyability.ts, tool-source.ts, data-tools token-tools.ts): `lane`, `status`, `buyable`, `whyNot` on every token item, lookup match, screen result and price entry, the reason naming the lane, the status, the owner's onchain opt-in, the screen or the price.
- Class A trades (trade-flow.ts, chain-tools reader-v3.ts and logic-v3.ts, signer): each class A side carries a fresh attestation at submission, else ATTESTOR_UNAVAILABLE; the Executor mirror attests the side from the market's attested price and judges the hop by the pool's own price within the oracle's deviation limit (L-214); the floor prices a class A side by the attestation (L-215). The attested reader lays the attestor's prices over the market for every reader.
- The universe (cycle/universe.ts, cycle.ts, research.ts, briefs.ts, config.ts, terminal.ts, research-routes.ts): recorded before a cycle's first stage from the market and this account's buyability; the themes' decisions after the Scan by the Dive rules; the Zoom out's RATIONALE carries `notProposed` for every buyable, dived or held token the plan leaves out, refused until it does, and never proposes a token the account cannot buy; `GET /research` carries the newest cycle's universe and the terminal its lines.
- The Goal page (goal-page.tsx, use-account-tx.ts, custody.ts, control-api app.ts, client.ts): the onchain step shows the chain's opt-in and sends `setScreenedOptIn` from the owner's wallet through its own provider behind the network guard; the goal brief and get_goals_and_limits swap the lane sentence to the chain's (`screenedOptInOnChain`).
- The Research page (research-page.tsx, universe.tsx, sentinel read): the "Universe considered" panel; "Unpause after review" sends `unpause()` from the owner's wallet when the account's mode is not normal.
- Tests: the attestor (5), the verifier (6 forge), the registrar (4), buyability (3), the mirror's attested side (3), the universe store and the themes' decisions (3), the terminal's universe lines (1), the panel (2), the goals tool's chain lane (1), the brief swap (1), the portfolio reading's opt-in (2), the token tools (9), the Goal page's opt-in and the Research page's unpause e2e (web), the class A fork test (4: the verifier through the timelock, GHO registered with its v4 pool and the opt-in; a buy at the attested price within 1% and the 15% cap on basis; the caps with a wrong price, slippage, deviation and disagreement refusals; sell-only).
- The live sweep (`pnpm test:activation:live --open-universe`, evidence/f-u12/live-2026-10-11T20-09-34.502Z.json): every check passed: the verifier set through the timelock, the opt-in recorded, GHO screened and listed in the screened lane at once, the sweep in order within its ceilings (0.89 of 3.80 USDC), the universe recorded with 8 tokens, 7 buyable, GHO buyable for the opted-in owner and "not proposed: not dived; stablecoin without a return thesis", the plan (cash only, every token's reason given) approved, the agent armed and RUNNING. The first run (17:50) found L-216 and its plan was rejected by the proposal evaluator; the second (18:09) was lost to a host suspension of 1.6 hours (the Scan capped at its deadline on resume); the third (19:56) passed every check but the plan's approval, the evaluator rejecting a three-position plan the Zoom out's own Test had accepted; the fourth passed in full. The evaluator's findings are now in the report (`proposalChecks`).
- Checks on the final tree: typecheck, lint, format and forge fmt clean; forge 561 passed, 11 skipped; vitest node 1561 passed in 130 files (the pre-existing unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts still makes vitest exit 1 while every test passes); vitest ui, console and web 801 passed in 50 files; vitest forks 63 passed in 16 files, every transaction mined; Playwright web 263 passed, 2 skipped (the goal, research and design captures refreshed; a one-pixel flake on the new mobile capture fixed by capturing before the send, the goal spec then passing twice); Playwright console 114 passed (the pages spec's captures refreshed for the playbook versions).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-212 (two ProtocolRegistryV3 deployments on the local fork), L-213 (clocks on a warped fork), L-214 (the mirror never attested a class A side), L-215 (the flow's minimum output under the Executor's floor), L-216 (the token table keyed by mainnet's chain id).
- Model spend of this unit: 2.29 USD by `pnpm spend:report --days=1` (6.674 to 8.966 USD on 2026-10-11), all in the four live sweeps (0.71, 0.13, 0.79 and 0.69 USD by the gateway's own figures); no other model call.
- Dev state: pnpm dev:all's services stopped for the Playwright suites and restarted at the end; the playtest fork on 8545 untouched throughout (no verifier set there: `pnpm deploy:attestor` sets it, warping the fork's clock by nine days); the fork tests and the live sweeps ran on forks of their own (8565, 8551, 8582).
Notes for the owner:
- On the playtest fork, class A stays off until `pnpm deploy:attestor` sets the verifier (it warps the fork's clock nine days; feeds then need `refreshLocalFeeds`, which the dev stack's keeper does). The attestor and the registrar run from the next `pnpm dev:all` on anvil accounts 2 and 3.
- The owner tests it as the prompt says: an Aggressive goal with the opt-in on, "Record the opt-in on chain" on the Goal page, automatic trading on, then the "Universe considered" panel and the terminal.
Open issues and suggestions:
- The proposal evaluator rejected two of the live sweeps' plans that the Zoom out's own Test had accepted (runs 1 and 3); the evaluator's findings were not captured then and are now reported from the terminal's check lines. The likely rule is the cost hurdle, which only the evaluator applies; the Test stage could apply it too, or the Zoom out could be told the route costs. Research tuning is out of scope here.
- The mirror's refusal of a sell-only token lists NOT_OPTED_IN beside TOKEN_SELL_ONLY for an opted-in account; the second code is spurious.
- The 50% total class A cap is proven in forge; the fork test proves the 15% position cap live (one class A token on the fork).
- The Scan's "Universe:" terminal line could name the not-buyable count on the Research page's summary tiles too.

## 2026-10-11, F-T2 Investing plumbing
Status: done. Step 0 recorded the owner's schedule (D-371; BUILD_PLAN 4.0.1 revision 5): F-T2, the vaults, SK-U1 and BK-U1 while the owner tunes research, the replay tool if time allows, then F-U13, W-1, Playtest P, PB-U1 and PB-U2; the profile page, the Gallery and the community feed after the beta; the beta on Monday, October 12, the submission on Tuesday, October 13. The unit: one rule set for plans shared by the Test stage, the proposal evaluator and the console's draft check, with a fingerprint that names what changed when the evaluator refuses a plan the Test accepted, and a shared fixture checked on every side; a background registrar that registers every token with a fresh passing screen in the screened lane without waiting for a sweep and moves a refused listed token to sell-only, its gas and rate bounded and its gas measured; the Executor mirror's sell-only refusal naming one reason; the signer freeing the nonces a local fork reset took back, so a restart never collides on the outbox's unique nonce; the 50% total class A cap proven on a fork with four class A tokens; and one live Aggressive sweep with the opt-in on, which the evaluator accepted and whose plan traded.
Summary: services/orchestrator (cycle/plan-rules.ts with its fixture and tests, cycle/proposals.ts, cycle/research.ts, cycle/test-stage.ts, tokens/registrar.ts and store.ts, token-routes.ts, main.ts, orchestrator.ts, class-a.fork.test.ts, live-activation.ts); packages/policy (executor-v3.ts); packages/signer (signer.ts); Planv2 (D-371, A-85); evidence/f-t2.
- One rule set (plan-rules.ts): `checkTargetPortfolio` is every Test v2 rule plus the cost to move against the plan's own hurdle; `targetPortfolioFromDecision` is the one conversion of a Zoom out's decision (the minimum trade from USDC text, tokens lowercased); `inputsFingerprint` and `whatChanged` name each difference between two reads; the Test records the fingerprint with its verdict and the evaluator adds STATE_CHANGED when it refuses a plan the Test accepted. The fixture (plan-rules.fixture.json) runs on the rule set, on `CycleResearch.targetPortfolioVerdict` and on `ProposalService.evaluate` through `portfolioParamsFromDecision`, with a case whose tokens are checksummed as the model writes them.
- The real cause of F-U12's rejected plans (L-220): the proposal path kept the model's checksummed addresses where the Test lowercased them, so the template's bounds refused every position; found by the first F-T2 live sweep's captured findings ("A position's token is its lowercase address", three times, beside STATE_CHANGED "nothing changed"), not by the cost-hurdle guess.
- The background registrar (registrar.ts `sweep`, store.ts `passingUnregistered` and `refusedListed`, main.ts): a pass 30 seconds after the start and every 5 minutes with the token loops, at most 3 registrations a pass (deepest first, at least 50,000 USD of liquidity), each transaction under 1,000,000 gas and 100 gwei, a failure on one token a note; `POST /v1/tokens/registrar/sweep` runs a pass now and reports the gas per kind. Measured on the fork (evidence/f-t2/registrar-gas.json): addScreened 145,939 gas; addScreenedPool 202,968 gas for a hookless v4 pool (A-85).
- The mirror (policy executor-v3.ts): NOT_OPTED_IN only for a buyable screened token the account has not opted into; a sell-only token refuses a buy with TOKEN_SELL_ONLY alone, opted in or not (test on the parity fixture's case).
- The signer (signer.ts): when the local realign moves the sequence back (a fork reset below the recorded nonces), the key's rows at or above the chain's pending nonce keep their history and hashes and free their nonce column; a test restores the fake chain below two reconciled transactions and restarts the signer with no conflict and no gap.
- The fork test (class-a.fork.test.ts): a fifth case on a second owner's 100 USDC account registers GHO, CHAOS, SAKURA and LV through the registrar (their v3 and PancakeSwap pools read from the fork), attests each at its pool's own spot, buys each to a 12.5 USDC basis (eight legs), reads the account's classABasis at exactly 50.0 and totalBasis 100.0, and has one more USDC of GHO refused with CLASS_A_TOTAL_CAP alone while a sale of CHAOS still goes through.
- The live sweep (`pnpm test:activation:live --open-universe --unit=F-T2`, evidence/f-t2/live-2026-10-11T22-01-45.483Z.json): every check passed. Four class A tokens screened and registered at once (GHO, CHAOS, SAKURA, LV all passed this time); 11 tokens in the universe, 8 buyable (AUSD, CHAOS, GHO, WBTC, WETH, WMON, cbBTC, shMON), SAKURA and LV not buyable with "no attested price right now: the attestor's sources do not agree or are not fresh", USDC the cash; the agent dived GHO (SCREENED_ALT_LIQUIDITY) and WMON (MON_INTRADAY_STRENGTH), proposed WMON at 30% with 70% cash, every other token with its reason ("Pegged stablecoin with no return; one pool, concentrated holders" for GHO; "Not dived; no thesis this cycle" for the core tokens; "overlaps MON exposure" for shMON); the evaluator accepted ("the plan passed every limit"), the owner's approval passed the re-check, the agent armed and RUNNING, and the runner's first leg (8.955 USDC to WMON) settled on the fork's real pools. 0.88 of 3.80 USDC charged; 0.70 USD of model spend. The first F-T2 sweep (evidence/f-u12/live-2026-10-11T21-49-33.082Z.json, before the conversion fix) ran the same way up to the evaluator, which refused the plan with the three "lowercase address" findings that named the bug.
- Checks on the final tree: typecheck, lint, format and forge fmt clean; forge 561 passed, 11 skipped; vitest node 1580 passed in 131 files; vitest ui, console and web 801 passed in 50 files; vitest forks 72 passed in 16 files, one at a time, every transaction mined (the devenv guard "never touched the playtest fork" trips while the dev stack's sentinel mines blocks on 8545 and passes with the dev services stopped); Playwright console 114 passed; Playwright web 263 passed, 2 skipped, 1 failed on an unchanged capture (portal `configure-activating.png`, 14 pixels in the Activating badge's pulsing dot, twice; the web app is untouched by this unit).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-217 (the signer after a fork reset), L-218 (the mirror's second reason), L-220 (the decision's token case between the Test and the evaluator). Noted, not fixed: L-219 (the attestor's two-way pool quote on a one-sided pool).
- Model spend of this unit: 1.40 USD by the two live sweeps' own figures (0.70 and 0.70); `pnpm spend:report --days=1` reads 10.37 USD for 2026-10-11 after them (8.97 before).
- Dev state: the dev stack's services stopped for the Playwright suites and restarted at the end; the playtest fork on 8545 untouched by this unit (the devenv guard passed with the services stopped); from this restart the registrar's background pass runs on the dev stack and registers the passing tokens with at least 50,000 USD of liquidity on the playtest fork, three a pass.
Notes for the owner:
- On the dev stack the registrar now lists every passing token in the screened lane on its own; the console's `POST /v1/tokens/registrar/sweep` runs a pass now. Class A trades there still wait for `pnpm deploy:attestor`.
- A plan the research Test accepts now passes the evaluator; when it does not, the plan card's findings say what changed since.
Open issues and suggestions:
- The attestor's pool source misprices a one-sided pool (L-219); SAKURA and LV were unbuyable in the live sweep for that reason. Take the pool's spot as the source and keep the round trip as a check, or let GeckoTerminal decide.
- The devenv guard "never touched the playtest fork" cannot distinguish the playtest stack's own blocks (the sentinel's pokes) from a test touching the fork; run it with the dev services stopped, or compare only transactions from test accounts.
- The portal's Activating capture pulses; the capture style sheet could freeze the dot.
- The Zoom out still ends mostly in cash on an Aggressive goal (one position at 30%); research tuning was out of scope.

## 2026-10-11, F-T2 Step 0 of the vault session (P7-U1 to P7-U4): the attestor's pool source, the capture style sheet
Status: done, in two commits before the vault units. (1) L-219: the attestor's pool source is the pool's own spot price as the oracle reads it (`poolSpotSource`, the median over the token's usable registered pools), the price the Executor checks an attestation against; GeckoTerminal stays the independent source, and the agreement rule refuses a token whose spot and aggregator price are more than 5% apart for that stated reason, so a one-sided pool such as SAKURA's or LV's is priced at its spot or refused with the reason named; the class A fork test attests through the same source (2d5ca15). (2) The screenshot capture style sheet (apps/web/e2e/portal-capture.css) holds every animation and transition still, so the Activating badge's pulsing dot never decides a pixel; the portal captures were refreshed and verified twice (5ef75a6).

## 2026-10-11, P7-U1 StrategyVault core
Status: done. StrategyVaultV3 is the custody core v3 in public-vault mode (FINAL_PLAN 4.7, D-338, D-355): one agent's public fund holding USDC and core-lane class F tokens only, with shares nobody can transfer at launch. Deposits are USDC, priced at the vault's value plus a 0.5% spread against a 1e12 virtual offset (A-06), under the factory's allowlist and caps; every deposit sits in a lock bucket (one day by default, seven at most) that only the depositor's own top-up extends, and a lockup increase by the manager waits the new duration plus two days' notice and applies to later deposits only (D-072, D-076). The manager is the agent's current owner, resolved live, only while the ownership epoch is the accepted one; the manager's deposits are the leader stake, at least 5% of the supply after every other deposit and every leader exit, in a balance of its own. Exits never depend on the Executor, the platform, a role or a pause: the USDC path pays from USDC above the 10% floor at the value less the spread and names the in-kind path when USDC does not cover it; `redeemInKind` pays each depositor's floor share of every held token with no oracle at all, and a token whose transfer reverts is skipped and kept as the exiter's claimable credit (D-071). A sale of the agent moves the epoch and the vault is in handover: deposits close, every lock is waived, the old manager can only exit, the incoming owner stakes and accepts after three days with 5% or more. `closeVault` winds the vault down (no deposits, reduce-only trading, locks waived, the leader last after the longest lockup) and emits the event the platform reads to start the agent's unstake cooldown (D-242). The breaker watches the share price; a pause lifts only after one lockup, by the manager alone (D-080); the reference band refuses deposits and USDC exits more than 10% from a reference that moves toward the live price by at most 10% a day, and resumes by itself. The dormant fee storage is there (each holder's weighted entry price, a rate capped at 20% behind the 9-day timelock, charged in shares to the leader on gains only, never the leader's). `isVault()` marks the account for the Executor's grant without a fixed expiry (D-241, P7-U2).
Summary: chains/monad/src/fund/StrategyVaultV3.sol (new, 61,778 bytes of runtime); CustodyCoreV3.sol gains the vault's hooks (`_mayWithdraw`, `_checkUnpause`, a virtual `_setMode`, and `_tryTransfer`, `_reduceBasis`, `_removeHeld`, `_checkRecipient`, `_heldList`, `_isHeld` made internal for the vault's exit); test/mocks/VaultMocks.sol (a harness in the factory's place until P7-U4) and CustodyMocks.sol (the mock AgentNFT's `epochStartedAt` and a broken switch); test/fund/StrategyVaultV3Base.sol and five suites; evidence/p7-u1.
- The contract (05a5082): the session's uncommitted draft from the F-T2 session, reviewed line by line against FINAL_PLAN 4.7 and finished: the reserved word `reference` (solc 0.8.37) renamed; the breaker observed on every deposit and USDC exit, with a deposit refused when its own value would pause the vault; a wound-down vault never returning to NORMAL through an unpause; the in-kind exit freeing a token's slot by its balance after the transfer, not before; a guardian's cancel of a pending lockup or fee change; `effectiveMode()` (HANDOVER while the agent has changed hands); `inKindAmounts()` for the UI; `maxDeposit` never reverting even with the agent unreadable; the incoming owner's stake exempt from a pause's deposit block as well as from closed deposits, so a handover can always end (A-86).
- Tests (05a5082; 75 in all, every one on mocks): StrategyVaultV3.t.sol (40: creation, the seed and `depositsOpen` (M-03), shares at the value plus the spread matching the preview (M-01), no profitable round trip on either path (M-02, fuzzed), the inflation attack after a tiny seed and a large donation (M-03, fuzzed: the victim loses at most the spread, the attacker gains at most it), the leader stake on deposits and exits with others never blocked (M-14 to M-17), lock buckets, top-ups, expiry, bounds, the timelocked increase applying to later deposits only, lapse and staleness after a sale, the cancel, and the waivers in a pause, wind-down and handover (M-18 to M-23, MV-S4), the USDC path's value, floor, `UseInKind`, `minAssetsOut` and deadline (M-07 to M-11 as built, MV-S12), only the share owner burning their shares to any receiver (MV-S14), in-kind floors with others' claims never falling (M-12, fuzzed), a reverting token as a credit claimed later to any address (MV-S1), the exit with the oracle, the registry, the factory, AgentNFT and the Executor all reverting and the vault paused (M-25, M-26), no feed needed, and gas-burning, return-bomb and balance-reverting held tokens); StrategyVaultV3Modes.t.sol (28: handover, the incoming stake and acceptance, a second sale releasing the first stake, the stake exempt from closed deposits and a pause and locked like any other, an agent bought back still in handover, wind-down and its survival of a pause, the pause's wait and the manager alone unpausing, the breaker on the share price with flows ignored, the band's refusal and self-recovery, its symmetry, the reference's 10% a day, lag arbitrage bounded by the spread (M-28), the fee's bounds, timelock, gains-only charge in shares diluting nobody and the leader's exemption (MV-V19), class A and screened tokens refused at every trade, stale epochs, the Executor alone trading, the tighteners never loosening); StrategyVaultV3Invariant.t.sol (a handler with the leader, three depositors, the Executor trading two tokens, the tighteners, a buyer and the world; invariants: the supply is the sum of shares (MV-S3), funds leave only through exits, claims and trades, nobody else moves funds or loosens anything, the leader stake holds whenever the rule applies, every holder with free shares can leave in kind now for the floor of each slice or a credit and a locked holder unlocks within seven days (M-13 in its P7-U1 form), credits and the held list consistent, the platform total the principal, a wind-down never NORMAL again; a scripted probe (L-40) reaching deposits, both exits, credits, trades, a sale and an acceptance; a fuzz that any call by anyone without shares moves nothing); StrategyVaultV3Reentrancy.t.sol (4: the leader as a contract reentering from a hooked USDC during a deposit, a USDC exit, an in-kind exit and a claim; every guarded entry point refused, every amount exact).
- Slither and gas (0963fea; evidence/p7-u1/SLITHER.md, GAS.md): 369 results in all, 39 in the vault, every one justified, none changing code; deposit up to 552,897 gas with three tokens held, the USDC exit 478,409, the in-kind exit 183,407 median and 1,518,685 with a hostile token burning its 1,000,000-gas allowance, a trade 943,812; StrategyVaultV3 is 61,778 bytes of runtime, inside Monad's 128 KiB limit.
- Checks on the final tree: forge build and `forge fmt --check` clean; forge 636 passed, 11 skipped (the fork and testnet tests, as before); typecheck, lint and format clean; vitest node 1582 passed in 132 files (the pre-existing unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts's fake sandbox still makes vitest exit 1 while every test passes). The web, console, fork and Playwright suites were not run: this unit changes no TypeScript and deploys nothing; no TypeScript reads the vault yet.
- Bugs found and fixed: L-221 (a prank spent on a view call inside an assertion's arguments), L-222 (the draft's in-kind exit judged a token's slot before the transfer). Not a bug, found by the invariant: a token donated to the vault but never held is not part of its value or its in-kind payout until a deposit or a trade lists it (D-056); the check counts held tokens only.
- Model spend of this unit: none; no model was called.
- Dev state: the dev services were already stopped (only Docker's Postgres, Redis and LiteLLM, and the playtest fork on 8545, were running); nothing was started; the playtest fork untouched. The F-T2 registrar gas file's regenerated timestamp from the earlier fork test run was edited back to its committed value.
Notes for the owner:
- The vault has no factory path yet: P7-U4 adds `createVault` to AccountFactoryV3 with the per-vault cap, the allowlist and `depositsEnabled`; until then the tests deploy it through a harness factory (test/mocks/VaultMocks.sol). P7-U2 needs the Executor to know a vault's address for its grant and may add the minimal registration early; the handoff says so.
- The USDC path never sells anything: when USDC above the floor does not cover an exit, the call reverts with `UseInKind(owed, usdcAboveTheFloor)` and the depositor redeems in kind (A-86). The proportional close over generic routes of FINAL_PLAN 4.7.5 is not built; it is listed below as a suggestion.
Open issues and suggestions:
- The proportional close (selling the exiter's slice through the registered pools with an oracle floor) would let a depositor leave in USDC from a vault that holds little USDC; it needs the vault to swap outside the Executor, and the plan's thin-pool guard; a periphery `ExitZap` that calls `redeemInKind` and swaps in the user's context is the lighter alternative.
- The fee rate applies at exit time to every depositor once the 9-day timelock passes; the plan's "apply only to later deposits" would need a rate per depositor at entry. Fees are zero at launch.
- `IAgentNFTEpochs` is a local interface in the vault; folding `epochStartedAt` into `IAgentNFTView` would touch every v2 mock and test.

## 2026-10-12, P7-U2 Vault trading through Executor v3, with backstops
Status: done. An agent's StrategyVaultV3 trades through Executor v3 under every hard limit and the vault's own backstops, exactly as a personal account does, and nowhere else. AccountFactoryV3 deploys one vault per agent for its owner (`createVault`, `vaultOf`, `predictVault`; the StrategyVaultV3 implementation beside the personal one, both asserted by the deploy script), so the Executor can resolve an intent's account as the agent's vault or its owner's personal account (A-87). Executor v3 takes a vault grant without a fixed expiry (`registerVaultSession`, `validUntil` NO_EXPIRY, D-241) that serves the vault alone and ends only on a sale, a configuration change or the owner's revocation; a timed grant serves either account; one grant per agent. For a vault the Executor refuses any class A or screened token by name (ASSET_NOT_ALLOWED, D-338) before any price is read, and refuses every trade while the vault is in handover (VAULT_IN_HANDOVER, from the vault's epochs) until the new owner accepts; the counters are per account, so the vault's 20-trade window and turnover are its own. The vault's backstops (12%, 45%, 1%, the post-trade checks) stand behind the Executor and catch every misbehaving Executor the mock can be. On the platform side the vault is the agent's trading account once it exists: the chain reader resolves the account to the vault (its canonical mode read through `effectiveMode`, HANDOVER included; the personal account stays readable as an older one), the research universe and the token tools say why a vault cannot buy a class A or screened token, the Executor mirror repeats the vault rule in the contract's order and treats a closing vault as reduce-only, the control API hands the owner's wallet `registerVaultSession` for an agent with a vault, and the arming record for a vault has no expiry date and no renewal. The agent's target portfolio and runner need no change: they read the account the reader resolves, and the fork test drives the trade flow for the vault through the real signer and Executor v3 in one hop and two.
Summary: chains/monad (AccountFactoryV3.sol, ExecutorV3.sol, interfaces/IExecutorV3.sol, script/DeployCustodyV3.s.sol; test/fund/ExecutorV3Vault.t.sol, StrategyVaultV3Backstops.t.sol); packages/trading (arming.ts, actions.ts, chain.ts, json.ts), packages/policy (executor-v3.ts), packages/domain (address-book.ts), packages/devenv (trading.ts, trading-v3.ts); services/chain-tools (reader-v3.ts, logic-v3.ts, server-v3.ts), services/orchestrator (cycle/universe.ts, vault-trade.fork.test.ts); apps/control-api (app.ts, main.ts); apps/web (agent/custody.ts); evidence/p7-u2.
- Contracts (09cc31f, then the NO_EXPIRY change in 46fe562): the factory's vault creation; the Executor's vault grant, account resolution, vault-only refusals and handover read; `IExecutorFactoryV3.vaultOf` and `IVaultAccountV3.inHandover`. NO_EXPIRY is 2^53 - 1 (L-223).
- Tests on mocks (09cc31f): ExecutorV3Vault.t.sol (16: the factory's one vault per agent; the vault grant's life, no expiry, the owner alone, no vault no grant, never the personal account; a timed grant on the vault and its expiry; an allowed swap within every limit in one hop and two with the shares untouched (M-04); class A and screened tokens refused for the vault while the personal account still buys them; the hard limits (M-05); the counters per account (MV-S7); reduce-only, pause and wind-down; handover until acceptance under the new owner's grant (MV-S13, M-24); the guardian's pauseAll and clearExecutor; the Executor moving nothing outside a swap (M-06)); StrategyVaultV3Backstops.t.sol (3: every misbehaving Executor caught with no share touched (MV-S5), the 12%, 45% and 1% thresholds, a fair trade moving no value between depositors).
- The platform (46fe562): the reader's account resolution and `isVault`, `vault`, `personalAccount` on the agent state; `whyNotBuyableV3` with the vault's reason; the mirror's `isVault` and WIND_DOWN; `VAULT_GRANT_NO_EXPIRY`, `isNoExpiry`, `registerVaultCall`, `grantProblem` accepting the marker for a vault alone, `grantDate` and `armingJson` without a date for it, `renewalDue` never; the control API's `vault` flag and grant call; the web's grant ABI; devenv's `createTestVault`, `depositTestVault`, `setTestVaultDepositsOpen`, `registerTestVaultGrant`. Unit tests: the grant (3), the mirror's vault rule on the fixture's class A, screened and WMON cases (1), the reader's market and the buyability (2); every fake agent state carries the new fields.
- The fork test (46fe562; services/orchestrator/src/vault-trade.fork.test.ts, port 8566): the vault created through the factory, seeded with 20 USDC, opened, 30 USDC from each of two depositors; the reader shows the vault as the account with the leader stake and the locks; the vault grant armed with no expiry and the first trade approved; a one-hop and a two-hop swap through the real signer and Executor v3 with the supply untouched; after the lockup one depositor leaves in USDC (29.x of 30), the other in kind across USDC, WMON and WBTC for exactly the previewed floors, and the leader leaves in kind with the oracle, the registry, the factory, the Executor and AgentNFT replaced by reverting code (the mid-phase playtest of BUILD_PLAN 4.0.1).
- The address book (46fe562): the Executor's code changed, so every deterministic address of the v3 set moved with it (L-185): Executor v3 0x9C96ca817322AeE0F1a1F776149c8406F2f0a264 (41,210 bytes), its RouteAdapter 0x45ecC9E1bbCFF5908e9D94701F826Ee05E1910B1, ProtocolRegistryV3 0x1cFF11d43827f5c3605E563435f23b70959E0768, OracleAdapterV3 0x9CC7AA6A7A26ed178Be72506443E828908747406, AccountFactoryV3 0x6c836E53250cd120d25182313F1cDF8Ae6b4f655 (13,026), the PersonalAccountV3 implementation 0x0457Bff5B22aF3389770A508a06Ea092346751Fa and the StrategyVaultV3 implementation 0xB9131B9b52E58D822487eDF95f397FEBA1C1cfD7 (61,778), all recorded with their sizes; the F-U4 set stays on the playtest fork unused until `pnpm deploy:executor-v3` runs there.
- Slither and gas (evidence/p7-u2/SLITHER.md, GAS.md): 371 results in all, two new (the vault's missing interface inheritance and an immutable's name), each justified, none changing code; a vault swap 1,423,634 gas median and 2,070,084 at most through the Executor, `registerVaultSession` 110,502, `createVault` 374,448; ExecutorV3 41,210 bytes, AccountFactoryV3 13,026.
- Checks on the final tree: forge build and `forge fmt --check` clean; forge 655 passed, 11 skipped; typecheck, lint and format clean; vitest node 1587 passed and 1 skipped in 132 files (the pre-existing unhandled rejection in scan.test.ts's fake sandbox still makes vitest exit 1 while every test passes); vitest forks 67 passed in 17 files, one file at a time with the dev services stopped, every transaction mined (the vault fork test among them). The web, console and Playwright suites were not run: this unit changes no page (the web's grant ABI gained one function the API now sends).
- Bugs found and fixed: L-223 (the no-expiry marker against the stores that carry it). The fork test's first run found the address book stale for the new bytecode, as L-185 says: the recorded set is the one `pnpm test:fork` deploys.
- Model spend of this unit: none; no model was called.
- Dev state: the dev services stayed stopped; the playtest fork on 8545 untouched; every fork test on a fork of its own (8566 for the vault test, 8567 for the address readings).
Notes for the owner:
- The playtest fork still holds the F-U4 Executor set and factory, which the address book no longer names. On the next `pnpm dev:all`, run `pnpm deploy:executor-v3` first: it lands the P7-U2 set beside the old one at the recorded addresses. Agent 1's v3 account then belongs to the old factory: its holdings are withdrawable by your wallet from the account's own address (the console's Trades page shows the old snapshot until then), and a fresh PersonalAccountV3 or a vault is opened through the new factory.
- An agent with a vault trades the vault: the Goal page's arming now sends the grant without expiry, and the portfolio page's "Permission until" shows no date for it.
Open issues and suggestions:
- The parity fixture has no vault case; the mirror's vault rule is covered by a unit test on the fixture's cases and the contract's by forge (A-87).
- FINAL_PLAN 4.7.7 lets the Executor make reduce-only swaps for a vault in handover when the guardian or the sentinel requires them; the Executor refuses every trade in handover for now.
- The console's Trades page prints a vault grant's `validUntil` as a date; P7-U4's UI should say "no expiry" there too.
- The portfolio and holdings readers of packages/trading still read `personalAccountOf`; P7-U4 reads the vault's holdings and snapshots for the UI.

## 2026-10-12, P7-U3 Vault invariant and spike suite over many tokens
Status: done. Three suites prove the vault's promises under random sequences by every role and on Monad's real tokens. (1) StrategyVaultV3ExecutorInvariant.t.sol: the vault through the real Executor v3 and RouteAdapter over six class F tokens (the base's four and two more, each added through the token registry's and the pool registry's 9-day timelocks with a mock pool on its feed), driven by a session key trading every pair through the real pools or a venue that misbehaves one call in eight, three depositors and the leader, the sentinel and the guardian, the manager, a buyer, an oracle mover, a pool mover, time and strangers; every trade is checked as it happens against the Executor's limits (size, floor, caps, floor, the 20-trade window, turnover), the vault's own backstops (45%, the shares untouched) and the per-trade loss bound (the slippage limit plus the route's fees). (2) StrategyVaultV3ManyTokensInvariant.t.sol: fifteen core-lane class F tokens traded by the mock Executor, with gifts, blacklists, feed moves, waits, a sale and an acceptance. (3) StrategyVaultV3Fork.t.sol, in `pnpm test:fork`: the whole v3 set deployed as on chain, the agent's vault created through the factory, seeded by its leader, funded by two depositors and traded by the session key through the real Executor and pools into the real WMON, WBTC, cbBTC, WETH, AUSD and shMON. The invariants, over both handler runs: every holder leaves in kind after every call with the vault paused and the oracle, the registries, the factory, the Executor, the adapters and AgentNFT all replaced by reverting code, for the floor of every held token's slice or a credit (M-13); the supply is the shares; nothing moves a token but a flow, a claim or a trade; a holder's shares change only through their own flows and no deposit or exit by anyone lowers the price per share for the others (no sequence of calls moves value between depositors); the leader's stake holds whenever the rule applies; locks end within seven days and a locked holder cannot leave early; in handover nobody manages, nobody deposits, nothing trades and everyone may leave; the Executor and the router keep nothing; the 20-trade window is never exceeded; credits, the held list and the platform total stay consistent. The real-token spikes: the lockup holds, then one depositor leaves in USDC at the feeds' value and the others in kind with everything else dead (the seven Chainlink feeds included); the leader cannot leave below the stake while others remain, cannot withdraw or trade directly, and the Executor refuses a trade above 10% and buys past 40% in one token on real fills; a sale refuses every trade and deposit, lets everyone leave, and ends only with the buyer's stake and acceptance after three days; a deposit and an in-kind exit never lower the price per share on real tokens; and the gas of each exit over real tokens (M-31).
Summary: chains/monad (src/fund/StrategyVaultV3.sol; test/fund/StrategyVaultV3ExecutorInvariant.t.sol, StrategyVaultV3ManyTokensInvariant.t.sol, StrategyVaultV3Modes.t.sol, StrategyVaultV3.t.sol; test/fork/StrategyVaultV3Fork.t.sol); packages/domain (address-book.ts); evidence/p7-u3.
- Two contract bugs the runs found, fixed: an incoming leader's stake could not leave the vault (L-224: `_burn` spends the leader's stake, then the incoming stake, then ordinary shares); a gift to an empty vault let later depositors buy it below its value through the virtual offset (L-225: the first deposit into an empty vault mints at the virtual rate alone, and deposits open only with one USDC's worth of shares, `MIN_SUPPLY_TO_OPEN`; A-88). Regression tests: `test_TheIncomingOwnerCanLeaveWithoutAccepting`, `test_AGiftToAnEmptyVaultGoesToTheFirstDepositor`, `test_DepositsOpenNeedAtLeastOneUsdcOfShares`; the P7-U1 inflation tests seed at least one USDC now.
- Also found and recorded: the reference band refuses deposits and USDC exits after a gift that jumps the share price by more than its day's move (a griefer pays the gift to freeze deposits, never exits); a handler whose clock refreshes only some feeds hides most of its coverage (the first many-token run reached two deposits), so both handlers refresh every feed the valuation reads.
- The address book: the vault's code changed twice this unit, so the factory, the PersonalAccountV3 implementation and the StrategyVaultV3 implementation moved with it (L-185): AccountFactoryV3 0xBb5C5283e216d8F9ed6e267a12877A124dFA90e9, PersonalAccountV3 0x5C81C3D94B814Cbe91eA5cE50675858175124557, StrategyVaultV3 0x3F3217Dc03D534EEcAcfE7F15B20E3354f252948 (61,812 bytes); the Executor set is unchanged from P7-U2.
- Checks on the final tree: forge build and `forge fmt --check` clean; forge 663 passed, 12 skipped (the fork and testnet tests, the new vault fork test among them when no fork is named); `pnpm test:fork` 54 passed, 0 failed on a fresh fork of the pinned block (the four real-token vault spikes among them), the address book check PASS for executor_v3 (41,210 bytes), account_factory_v3 (13,026) and strategy_vault_v3_implementation (61,812), twice on separate forks; typecheck clean; the domain tests pass on the book (171); Slither 373 results against 371 after P7-U2; the two new ones are zero checks by design in `_deposit` and `acceptManagement`, justified in evidence/p7-u3/SLITHER.md. The TypeScript suites beyond the domain's were not rerun: this unit changes no TypeScript but the book's three addresses, which the forks project read in P7-U2's run on the same code paths. evidence/p7-u3/GAS.md: on seven real tokens the USDC exit 1,179,315 gas, the in-kind exit 790,288 for a depositor and 833,830 for the leader leaving last, 840,326 in handover (M-31).
- Model spend of this unit: none; no model was called.
- Dev state: the dev services stayed stopped; the playtest fork on 8545 untouched; the fork tests on 8546 and the address readings on 8567.
Notes for the owner:
- `pnpm deploy:executor-v3` on the playtest fork now lands the P7-U3 factory and implementations beside the F-U4 and P7-U2 ones (the Executor set is P7-U2's); the address book names only the current ones.
- The invariant suites run in the normal `forge test` (about 30 seconds together); the real-token spikes run in `pnpm test:fork`.
Open issues and suggestions:
- M-34's "abdication permanence" has no subject in the vault (no admin abdicates; the factory's renounce is disabled); "previews equal results" is checked in the Executor-path handler for deposits and USDC exits.
- The reduce-only exception for the guardian or the sentinel during a handover (FINAL_PLAN 4.7.7) is still not built; the invariant states the stricter rule the Executor enforces.
- An invariant run against a real fork would take minutes per run; the real-token spikes are scripted instead. A nightly job could run the handler against the fork with a handful of runs.

## 2026-10-12, P7-U4 Vault creation, caps, allowlists and the vault UI
Status: done. (1) AccountFactoryV3 holds the per-vault cap (`vaultCap`, lowered at once and raised through the 9-day timelock) and the public-deposit launch flag (`depositsEnabled`, Q-29: while off, only an allowlisted wallet may deposit into a vault, whether or not the allowlist is on; off at once, on through the timelock), marks every vault (`isVault`), and binds a vault's principal by its own cap rather than the personal one; exits never consult it. The deploy script and the local deploy pass both (500 USDC per vault, public deposits off); five factory tests and the timelock test over all nine actions cover them. (2) MV-S15 is `pnpm assert:state`: 70 values of the deployed v3 set (every role, delay, oracle, route, fee, cap and allowlist flag of the factory, both implementations, the Executor with its launch policy, the RouteAdapter, ProtocolRegistryV3, OracleAdapterV3 and the TokenRegistry) against the intent the deploy libraries build, failing on any mismatch; a unit test of the comparison and a run against a fresh fork. (3) `rpcVaultReaderV3` and `GET /v1/agents/:id/vault?wallet=` serve the vault to anyone with a named wallet's position (shares, the lock, the value, the in-kind slices, the claimable credits, the room to deposit, whether the factory would take the deposit), its performance from the snapshot rows recorded for the vault's address, and the funding address for "Fund this agent". (4) The vault components on /design first (the panel with its notices, the position with the lock in plain words, the deposit form with the top-up warning, the withdraw form with the USDC and in-kind paths, the contribution panel, the create card), then the public vault page: the owner creates the vault, stakes and opens deposits; allowlisted wallets deposit and withdraw in dialogs; holdings and performance; "Fund this agent" for non-owners (D-242). (5) The screenshot suite at 1440px and 380px with axe: the whole web suite 279 passed and 2 skipped at 1440px and 380px (one research capture failed on a spinner baseline that predated Step 0's animation freeze; refreshed, the research spec passes 12 of 12 on a plain rerun); vault.spec.ts 16 of 16 (8 tests at each width, 7 captures per width, axe on every captured state, the dialogs included); the design page's full capture and scan with the new Vault section. (6) The live run on a fork of its own (port 8569, `pnpm test:vault:live`): every one of 23 checks passed on a fresh fork on port 8569 (MV-S15 over 70 values, the vault created, staked and opened, two deposits of 30 USDC, every position read through the API's reader with a stranger refused, the vault armed and 5 USDC traded into WMON through the agent's pipeline and Executor v3, two snapshot rows and the live point in the performance series, after the lockup one depositor out in kind across USDC and WMON and the other paid 29.76 USDC exactly as the reader previewed, the leader's stake staying); no model was called; evidence/p7-u4/live-2026-10-12T03-11-49.276Z.json.
Summary: chains/monad (src/fund/AccountFactoryV3.sol, script/DeployCustodyV3.s.sol, test/fund/AccountFactoryV3.t.sol and the test bases); scripts (assert-state.js, lib/state-assert.js, lib/account-factory.js, lib/custody-v3.js); packages/domain (address-book.ts); packages/trading (vault-v3.ts); apps/control-api (app.ts, main.ts); packages/ui (components/agent/vault.tsx, window.tsx); apps/web (the design page's Vault section, agent/vault-v3.ts, agent/use-vault.ts, agent/custody.ts, api/client.ts, components/vault/vault-page.tsx, components/credits/contribute-credits-section.tsx, app/agents/[id]/vault, the portfolio's vault link, e2e/vault.spec.ts, e2e/fake-vault.ts, e2e/fake-api.ts and the baselines); services/orchestrator (live-vault.ts); evidence/p7-u4.

## 2026-10-12, Step 0 of the SK-U1 session (SK-U1, BK-U1, UI-U1): the owner's playtest fixes
Status: done, in four commits before SK-U1, each ending [P7-U4]. (1) The activation path with a vault (A-90): an agent with a vault manages it or its personal account as its owner chooses when turning automatic trading on. The portfolio page asks in a small dialog (`ManagedAccountChoice`, on /design first): its vault, whose trading permission has no fixed expiry (D-241), or the personal account, whose permission lasts 30 days; an account that does not exist cannot be chosen and says what to do first. The control API builds the grant for the choice (`GET /v1/agents/:id/arming?account=vault|personal`, the response naming both accounts and which one the grant serves), records the choice with the arming (`POST { account }`, `platform.managed_accounts`, migration 0030) and judges the grant for that account; every chain reader (the chain tools' and the control API's) resolves the agent's account from the stored choice through one rule, `managedAccountOf`, with the vault as the default once it exists (A-87), so the activation sweep, the proposals, the runner, the snapshots and the sandbox's chain tools follow the chosen account; the console's arm (`POST /v1/agents/:id/arm { "account": "vault" }`) takes the same choice and registers the vault's grant; the arming card says which account the agent manages and that a vault grant has no fixed expiry. (2) The console's Trades page names a vault grant as one with no fixed expiry and what ends it (`grantWords`, L-231) instead of formatting 2^53 - 1 as a date. (3) Every local deploy re-adds the owner's playtest wallets, listed in chains/monad/local-wallets.json, to every deposit allowlist it creates or finds (`ensureLocalAllowlist`, as the admin, instant, D-231), so a redeploy never drops them again (L-230); the MV-S15 intent includes them. (4) The local fork's caps are 10,000 USDC per account, personal or vault, and 100,000 USDC across the platform (`LOCAL_CAPS`); the beta's assumed caps, 1,000 and 25,000 USDC, are `BETA_CAPS` beside them for the owner to confirm at PB-U1 (A-90); the v2 factory keeps its caps so its address stays. The v3 factory and both implementations moved with the caps (L-185): AccountFactoryV3 0xFd758fd5f950E022fD10FF040DF6d605f5185338 (14,231 bytes), PersonalAccountV3 0x2804c216BE8b170762f718238264eF534b159cAA, StrategyVaultV3 0x56cfa61Aa46119ba13E8178136C32fA94B5B14C3; the Executor set is unchanged. On the playtest fork: the Executor was already bound to the P7-U4 factory (the owner had run `pnpm deploy:executor-v3`), and since the Executor binds once, the deploy now moves the binding on a local fork alone by writing the Executor's `factory` slot (L-229); `pnpm deploy:executor-v3` landed the new factory there, `pnpm assert:state` passes with 71 values, 0x683eE842A16f85e69883F433745263BFe8D55f76 is on the allowlist, and `createPersonalAccount(1)` simulated from it passes (341,932 gas); agent 1's earlier v3 account (on the F-U4 factory) stays an older account.
Summary: scripts (lib/account-factory.js, lib/custody-v3.js, lib/executor-v3.js, lib/state-assert.js, lib/paths.js); chains/monad/local-wallets.json; packages/domain (managed-account.ts, address-book.ts); packages/db (migrations/0030_managed_accounts.ts, schema.ts); packages/trading (arming.ts, chain.ts, store.ts); services/chain-tools (reader-v3.ts); services/orchestrator (main.ts, api.ts, orchestrator.ts, live-vault.ts); apps/control-api (app.ts, main.ts); packages/ui (components/agent/portfolio.tsx); apps/web (api/client.ts, agent/arming-flow.ts, agent/activation-flow.ts, agent/use-portfolio.ts, components/portfolio/portfolio-page.tsx, the design page's Portfolio section, e2e/fake-trading.ts, e2e/fake-api.ts, e2e/portfolio.spec.ts); apps/console (app/trades/trades.ts, trades-panel.tsx); README.
- Three bugs fixed: L-229 (the once-only binding on a long-lived fork), L-230 (the allowlist dropped by a redeploy), L-231 (the no-expiry marker shown as a date).
- Tests added: the domain rule (3), the trade store (1), the control API's arming routes (3), the orchestrator's arm (1), the activation service starting a sweep for the vault under the no-expiry grant (1), the web flows (2), the components (2), the portfolio screenshot suite (3 tests at 1440px and 380px, two captures each, axe on each), the console's grant words (1), the state assertion's intent (updated).
- Checks: see the SK-U1 session's handoff message; typecheck, lint and format clean; the portfolio spec 44 passed at both widths and the design spec 10 passed after its baselines took the new specimens.
- Model spend: none.
- Dev state: the dev services stayed stopped; the playtest fork on 8545 carries the new factory, the rebound Executor, the owner's allowlist entry and the raised caps, nothing else changed; the throwaway deploy on 8567, the fork tests on 8546.
Notes for the owner:
- `pnpm dev:all` as before: the fork is ready. "Set up trading" on an agent opens its personal account on the new factory; with a vault, "Turn on automatic trading" asks which account the agent manages.
- A fresh fork (`pnpm dev:reset`, then the deploys) allowlists the wallets of chains/monad/local-wallets.json on its own; add a wallet there, not by hand.
Open issues and suggestions:
