## 2026-10-12, SK-U1 Private skill upload
Status: done. An owner uploads a skill package for their own agent only, in the existing skill format, through every existing check, mounted read-only beside the built-in skills, never shown to other owners, versioned by content hash. (1) packages/skills: the format rules F1 to F8 run over files in memory as over a folder (`checkPackage` shared by `loadPackage` and the new `loadPackageFiles`; the F2 path rules shared through `pathIssues`), so an upload takes exactly the checks the built-in set takes; `checkOwnerSkill` adds the owner's two rules (an id that is a platform skill's, a publisher who is not the owner's wallet, as F7 until P6-U3's signing) and the static audit S1 to S15, with the publisher's own wallet exempt from S13 (L-232); `RULE_WORDS` says for each of the 23 rules what it means and what to do next. (2) `platform.owner_skills` (migration 0031) keeps one row per package version by content hash, every file's text in the row; `OwnerSkillStore` refuses the same version with another hash as the version lock does, treats the same hash as a no-op, lets a new version take over and keeps removed rows as history. (3) The sandbox: `materialize` places the owner's packages under the equipped directory as `aa-<id>` with their generated frontmatter; the boot writes them as root, makes the mount read-only and probes the owner's folder as it probes the playbooks (H-11), refusing to start when it is writable; the task context reads the agent's current owner skills at every sandbox start and re-checks each; every stage's prompt names the owner's skills as it names the playbook (A-91 item 7), since the first live run showed the agent loads only what a prompt names or what fits the step it is on; the console's skills route lists them with kind owner. (4) The control API: `GET /v1/agents/:id/skills` to the owner alone, `POST` with every finding named and its words (422), a passing package mounted with its warnings (201, 200 for the same content), `DELETE`. (5) The design system: SkillTile and SkillGrid, SkillUploadForm (one input, the skill's folder), SkillFindings and OwnerSkillsEmpty, on /design first. (6) The page at `/agents/:id/skills`, linked from the portfolio and My Agents: the owner's own skills as tiles marked Yours, the built-in set beside them, the upload in a small dialog with the folder input (the browser reads the files, the shared root is dropped), the findings or the mount, Remove with one question; another wallet sees nothing. (7) The screenshot suite at 1440px and 380px with axe on every captured state, over a fake skills backend and a committed package folder. (8) The live run, `pnpm test:skill:live`: every one of 11 checks passed on a stack of its own (two agents minted and provisioned; the owner's watchlist package passing every rule and recorded by its content hash; listed by the orchestrator as mounted with kind owner and absent from the other agent's mount; a Scan whose sandbox booted with the skill under the read-only mount; the agent loading aa-owner-monad-watchlist with skill_view beside its Scan playbook and writing the OWNER WATCHLIST section the skill asks for and the prompt never names; 0.173 USD of model spend under the 10 USD cap); evidence/sk-u1/live-2026-10-12T08-12-38.267Z.json. The first run passed 9 of 11: the agent loaded only its playbook, so every stage's prompt now names the owner's skills (A-91 item 7); its evidence was dropped.
Summary: packages/skills (packages.ts, audit.ts, owner.ts, store.ts); packages/db (migrations/0031_owner_skills.ts, schema.ts); services/orchestrator (hermes/materialize.ts, noop.ts, orchestrator.ts, api.ts, scan.ts, cycle/cycle.ts, live-skill.ts); apps/control-api (app.ts); packages/ui (components/agent/skills.tsx); apps/web (agent/use-skills.ts, agent/skill-upload.ts, api/client.ts, components/skills/skills-page.tsx, app/agents/[id]/skills, the design page's Skills section, e2e/skills.spec.ts, e2e/fake-skills.ts, e2e/fixtures/owner-notes); evidence/sk-u1.
- One bug fixed: L-232 (S13 judged the package's own publisher address).
- Found in the first live run and fixed within the unit: the agent did not open the owner's skill when only Hermes's index showed it; every stage's prompt now names the owner's skills (A-91 item 7), and the second run passed every check.
- Checks on the final tree: packages/skills 58; the orchestrator's mount, boot and prompt tests with the scan, cycle and noop suites; apps/control-api 3 new routes' tests in 140; pnpm typecheck, pnpm lint and pnpm format:check clean; vitest node 137 files, 1648 passed (vitest exits 1 on the pre-existing unhandled rejection of scan.test.ts); ui 21 files, 570 passed; web 25 files, 212 passed; the forks project 17 files, 67 passed one file at a time; Playwright: skills.spec.ts 8 passed at 1440px and 380px with axe on every capture, design.spec.ts 10 passed with the Skills section, the whole web suite 272 passed and 2 skipped with 22 captures of the portfolio, the v3 portfolio and My Agents differing only by the new Skills link and button, refreshed and 82 passed on a plain rerun of those three specs; `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` passes on the regenerated lockfile. Not run: forge (no contract changed) and `pnpm test:fork` (the address book is unchanged since Step 0).
- Model spend of this unit: 0.291 USD over the two live runs (0.118 for the first, 0.173 for the second), both on the Scan model; no other model call.
- Dev state: the dev services stayed stopped; the playtest fork on 8545 untouched since Step 0; the live runs on their own fork (8571) and database; the Playwright runs on their own stacks.
Notes for the owner:
- Open an agent's portfolio and follow "Skills"; upload a folder with skill.json and SKILL.md (publisher.address must be your wallet); the findings say what to fix. A package that passes mounts at the agent's next task.
- apps/web/e2e/fixtures/owner-notes is a small package in the format to start from.
Open issues and suggestions:
- The skill mounts at the next sandbox; a running sandbox keeps what it booted with (as for a changed built-in set).
- The console's Agents page lists the owner's skills but has no upload; the web page is the owner's.
- W-1's skill marker leak test (B-04) now covers the owner's skills beside the built-in ones.
- The lockfile was regenerated by pnpm for the skills package's two dependencies (viem and @alpha-agents/db) and carries pnpm's current peer resolutions beside them; `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` passes.

## 2026-10-12, BK-U1 Bring your own key
Status: done. An owner adds their own Dune API key (and the same mechanism takes the next paid source), stored encrypted, used only for that owner's agent through the platform's tool servers, never placed in the sandbox or shown again, deletable, and metered apart from the platform's own keys; with the owner's key present, the Dune tool works for that agent alone. (1) packages/domain: `PAID_DATA_SOURCES` names Dune with what the key gives, where to get one and the tool it switches on; `keyHint` shows a stored key by its last four characters; `ownerKeyProblem` refuses an empty, padded, spaced, short or long key with the reason. (2) packages/trading: `sealSecret` and `openSecret` are the platform's one AES-256-GCM envelope under ORCHESTRATOR_SECRET (the orchestrator's stored gateway keys use the same; its encryptSecret and decryptSecret now delegate), so the control API seals what the orchestrator's tool servers open; `OwnerKeyStore` keeps one sealed row per agent and source (`platform.owner_keys`, migration 0032), lists by hint alone, opens for the tool servers and deletes outright; `tool_calls.paid_by` records whose key paid. (3) The data tools ask the platform for the calling agent's own key before a dune_query and, with one, run the saved query on it through the research sources with daily budgets counted under the agent's own scope, charge the agent's credits nothing (A-92) and meter the call as paid by the owner; without one, and without a platform key, the tool says Dune is not configured and that the owner can add a key in the agent's settings; the orchestrator opens the key from its sealed row for the tool servers only and registers it with the redactor the moment it is opened; the console's research page marks calls on an owner's key. (4) The control API: `GET /v1/agents/:id/keys` to the owner alone, `PUT /keys/:provider` sealing the key and answering with the hint and nothing else of it, `DELETE`; the API now requires ORCHESTRATOR_SECRET. (5) The design system: DataKeyCard and DataKeyGrid (one tile per source), DataKeyForm (one password input), on /design first. (6) The page at `/agents/:id/settings`, linked from the portfolio, the skills page and My Agents: the tile per source, Add or Replace key in a small dialog whose typed key leaves the page's state before the save's answer returns, Remove key with one question; another wallet sees nothing. (7) The screenshot suite at 1440px and 380px with axe on every captured state, over a fake keys backend that records what was sent and never serves it back.
Summary: packages/domain (owner-keys.ts); packages/trading (sealed.ts, owner-keys.ts); packages/db (migrations/0032_owner_keys.ts, schema.ts); packages/market (research.ts); services/data-tools (server.ts, research-tools.ts); services/orchestrator (secrets.ts, tools/meter.ts, tools/servers.ts, orchestrator.ts, main.ts, api.ts); apps/control-api (app.ts, main.ts); apps/console (research page); packages/ui (components/agent/settings.tsx); apps/web (api/client.ts, agent/use-keys.ts, components/settings/settings-page.tsx, app/agents/[id]/settings, the design page's Settings section, e2e/settings.spec.ts, e2e/fake-keys.ts, the links); evidence/bk-u1.
- No bug fixed in this unit; one test timing noted below.
- Tests: the registry, hint and key problems (3); the envelope and the store (2); the data tools with a recorded Dune response (1 new): one agent's owner key serves that agent's calls and no other's, the key in no answer and no meter record, the call priced at 0 and metered as the owner's, the next call refused once the key is gone; the control API's routes (2 new): the sealed row opening to the key under the secret, hint-only answers, another owner refused, replacement, deletion; 2 component tests; settings.spec.ts 8 passed with axe on every capture.
- Checks on the final tree: pnpm typecheck, pnpm lint and pnpm format:check clean; vitest node 139 files, 1656 passed (vitest exits 1 on the pre-existing unhandled rejection of scan.test.ts); ui 22 files, 577 passed; web 25 files, 212 passed; the forks project 17 files, 67 passed one file at a time; Playwright: settings.spec.ts 8 passed at 1440px and 380px with axe on every capture, skills.spec.ts and design.spec.ts 18 passed after their baselines took the Settings link and section, the portfolio, v3 portfolio and My Agents specs 82 passed after theirs took the link and button, holdings.spec.ts 8 passed after its My Agents capture took the buttons, the whole web suite 301 passed and 2 skipped. Not run: forge and pnpm test:fork (no contract and no address changed).
- Model spend of this unit: none; no model was called.
- Dev state: the dev services stayed stopped; the playtest fork on 8545 untouched since Step 0; the Playwright runs on their own stacks. Migration 0032 applies when the API or the orchestrator next starts.
- Noted: portfolio.spec.ts's "declining a waiting proposal" test failed once on a text timing (the status line after Decline missed its 5-second window while the plan card refreshed) and passed on a plain rerun; the same shape as L-227 (a status on the card the action removes), worth folding into UI-U1's message rules.
Notes for the owner:
- `pnpm dev:all` (the control API now needs ORCHESTRATOR_SECRET, which .env has), then the agent's portfolio and "Settings": add your Dune key and run a research task; the console's Research page marks the dune_query call "Owner's key" with no charge.
- Removing the key makes the next dune_query answer that Dune is not configured, naming the settings.
Open issues and suggestions:
- X and CoinMarketCap stay on the platform's keys; each next source needs its row in PAID_DATA_SOURCES and its own path in the data tools.
- The console's Research sources page shows the platform's daily Dune use; an owner's scope is counted apart but not shown there.
- A key saved while a sandbox runs is used from the agent's next tool call, since the tool servers read the store per call.

## 2026-10-12, UI-U1 The owner's main pages, redesigned
Status: done. My Agents, the portfolio and the trading setup rebuilt from the design system to the owner's UI direction (A-93): visuals over forms, squarer tiles about three across, every checklist item, error and empty state saying what it means and what to do next with the action beside it. (1) packages/ui, on /design first under "Owner pages": TradingTile (the account's value, allocation donut, small performance line, the agent's state and one primary action, or what the account is and "Set up trading"), CreditsTile (the balance against the beta cap, Add credits, the refund and Research now with its one line), PositionTiles (every held token as a tile with amount, value, share and gain), PortfolioHero (total value, mode, state, donut, performance line, the prices' state, Deposit and Withdraw), SetupFlow (the four steps with done, next, later and blocked states), PageLoading and ComingLater; ActivationChecks takes an action and plain words per unmet item; HoldingsPanel is a grid of tiles; FundAgentPanel keeps the funding address and QR code alone; the allowlist badges say what to do. (2) My Agents: each card leads with the trading tile and the credits tile (Add credits in a small dialog, Refund credits, Research now direct with no confirmation), then spend, activity, the funding address and every holding; the hook reads the account's own holdings for the donut, the window for the line and the arming for the state. (3) The portfolio: the setup flow while any step is undone (the first undone step carries its action; a first step blocked by the allowlist keeps its button disabled with the words to act on), the hero, automatic trading right under it with each unmet check's action beside it and the permission item carrying the Turn on button, the activation panel's Add credits opening the dialog, positions as tiles, the plan, the trades, why the agent did not trade, and credits, goal, limits and gas as tiles; deposit and withdraw in dialogs whose amount and message clear on success; a standing block on deposits named on the page under the Deposit button. (4) Messages: "not yet allowed to deposit during the beta, ask the platform to add it" and "not yet invited to mint" replace every allowlist phrase the owner reads; the research action's words follow its new name. (5) Navigation: Home, My Agents, Mint and Configure; the home page and every unbuilt or unknown path show the coming-after-the-beta page with the live pages. (6) Loading: My Agents and the portfolio show the standard loading block. (7) Tests: 12 component tests, the setup flow's rules (4), the words and v2 rows (4), and the screenshot suites at 1440px and 380px with axe on every captured state, including the new captures of the credits tile, the add-credits dialog, a deposit done, and the v3 deposit and withdraw dialogs; the whole web suite re-baselined, since the nav changed every capture.

## 2026-10-12, NFT-U1 The real art, the new supply, and two mints per wallet
Status: done. The first unit of the four-unit session (NFT-U1, BC-U1, UI-U2, F-U13). (1) The supply and the species: AgentNFT holds 34 species at 500 base, 300 medium and 200 pro (D-372, D-373): the 25 of D-179, the owner's Snake, Hummingbird and Fish, and six animals the incoming art holds that no listed species names (Ladybug base; Grasshopper, Butterfly and Leaf insect medium; Crab and Orchid mantis pro), all nine appended after Cicada so every earlier index keeps its meaning (the owner's bee stays 14, the tick 4); a 2-bit tier table replaces the index ranges and the deck packs 7 bits per species; the counts split each tier as evenly as it divides, the remainder to the tier's first species in onchain order (A-94: base 84, 84, 83 x 4; medium 28 x 3, 27 x 8; pro 1 x 4, 16, 15 x 12). (2) Two mints per wallet (D-374): `mintsOf` against `MINTS_PER_WALLET` with `MintLimitReached`; holding more through the escrow spends no mint. Every other rule stands: the two-step mint with Pyth Entropy, claim signatures, ownership epochs, escrow-only transfers, onchain metadata, royalties, `contractURI`. The unit, fuzz and invariant suites follow, including minting all 1,000 under any randomness for exactly the new counts with every agent's tier its species' table entry, and the domain test checks the contract's table, deck, tier table and mint limit against `SPECIES`. (3) The art: `scripts/assets/incoming.json` maps every file of the owner's `assets-incoming/` folder (gitignored, 1.2 GB) to its species by sha256, with the inventory in evidence/nft-u1/HANDOFF.md; `pnpm assets:build-images` writes 25 images as 1024px WebP (3.2 MB); `pnpm assets:build-models` turns the 24 unrigged Meshy exports (0.6 to 2.6 million triangles, 25 to 90 MB) into 38,000-triangle models with 1024px WebP textures, scaled to the bee's 3.4 units standing on the platform, each with the eight `BOUNDS_SOCKETS` placed on its shell by direction, meshopt-compressed (0.7 to 1.0 MB each, 20.8 MB for 24); the manifest lists every image and model with its provenance, the nine species without art keep the placeholder, `pnpm assets:check` passes every model, and the viewer slides an unrigged model in with a pop and gives it an idle breath (`ANIM_CONFIG.unrigged`), the hover lifting it as it lifts the bee. A visual check of every model rendered headless from the viewer's camera, the side and the top confirmed each faces the camera as exported and its sockets sit on the shell. (4) The web, the API and the UI: the domain ABI reads `mintsOf`; the control API judges eligibility by the wallet's mints read fresh from the chain and answers with the mints used and the limit; the mint page shows the new supply and odds, says up to two per wallet, offers the second mint beside the agent minted first and, at the limit, both agents with their links; the design page's specimens, the fake chain and API, and every spec follow; migration 0033 widens the steered reveal's species check to 34. (5) The playtest fork, no reset (evidence/nft-u1/REDEPLOY.md): the new AgentNFT (0xAabdac6bF7B25F0a2190241D735A7014fDf92F14), and since AgentNFT is a constructor argument of the Executor and both factories, the Executor v3 set, AccountFactoryV3 with both implementations (rebound, L-229) and the v2 custody set moved with it; the owner's agent 1 minted again as the bee and agent 2 as the tick to the same wallets, each paying its own gas; every transaction sent was mined (1, 6, 8 and 7); MV-S15 passes with 71 values; the address book names the new set and `pnpm test:fork` matches every verified entry on a fresh fork.
Summary: chains/monad (src/AgentNFT.sol; test/AgentNFT.t.sol, AgentNFTBase.sol, AgentNFTReveal.t.sol, AgentNFTInvariant.t.sol); packages/domain (species.ts, species-assets.ts, agent-nft.ts, address-book.ts and tests); packages/db (migrations/0033_reveal_steers_species.ts, index.ts); scripts/assets (incoming.json, incoming.ts, incoming.test.ts, build-images.ts, build-models.ts, model-check.test.ts); scripts/lib (agent-reveal.js, custody.js); apps/web/public (species/*.webp, models/*.glb); apps/web (components/viewer/animated-model.tsx, rig.ts; agent/mint-page-state.ts, use-mint-page.ts, owned-agents.ts, use-agents.ts, mint-flow.ts, supply.test.ts; components/mint/mint-page.tsx, my-agents/my-agents-page.tsx; api/client.ts; the design page; e2e fake-chain.ts, fake-api.ts, mint.spec.ts, portal.spec.ts, my-agents.spec.ts, live.spec.ts and the baselines); apps/control-api (chain.ts, claims.ts, app.ts, queries.ts, app.test.ts); packages/ui (components/agent/mint-panel.tsx and its test); services/orchestrator (hermes/schema.ts, testing.ts, reveal-tiers.fork.test.ts); docs/assets.md; README; Planv2 (DECISIONS D-372 to D-375 and A-94, FINAL_PLAN 4.1.1 and the customization passage, BUILD_PLAN 4.0.1 rows 20a to 20c); evidence/nft-u1.

Migration 0033 applies when the API or the orchestrator next starts; the indexer re-projects agents 1 and 2 from the new contract's events then.

## 2026-10-12, BC-U1 Beta completeness
Status: done. The second unit of the four-unit session. (1) The audit (evidence/bc-u1/AUDIT.md): every item of FINAL_PLAN 0.10's definition of done inside the owner's beta scope is working end to end on the fork (items 1 to 7, 9 and 10, and class A through the attestor), item 8 is after the beta by D-371 and the reveal moment is UI-U2's; every unit of BUILD_PLAN 4.0.1 up to F-T2 is done with its open items inside the beta scope listed; the registry's 30 live tools resolve and its 24 deferrals each belong outside the beta scope, three of them (P3-U6's proposal and Test tools) with reasons corrected to say what superseded them (F-U9's proposal in complete_stage's decision, the orchestrator's Test stage of D-282), so the registry check passes with no unexplained deferral in the beta scope. (2) The gaps closed, smallest first: the console's Arm takes the account the agent manages (the personal account for 30 days or the vault without a fixed expiry, posted to the orchestrator's arm route as the web's dialog does, A-90) and names it in its toast; the console's Trades setup cards say 30 days on a personal account and no fixed expiry on a vault; the skills route reports a running task (an active sandbox lease) and the page shows a notice that a change joins the agent's next task, in the mount and remove lines too; the orchestrator counts the day's calls on owners' keys and the console's Research sources page says so beside the platform's caps; X and CoinMarketCap join Dune as owner-key sources on the same mechanism (BK-U1): two rows in PAID_DATA_SOURCES, migration 0034 widening the provider check, the research sources taking an owner's bearer token for X and the market service an owner's key for CoinMarketCap with daily budgets under the agent's own scope, the data tools asking the platform for the calling agent's key before x_search and coinmarketcap_prices, charging nothing and metering the call as the owner's, the settings page listing the three tiles unchanged. (3) Creating a skill in the app: SKILL_TEMPLATES in packages/domain (Owner notes and Watchlist, each passing every check with the owner as publisher); a new check route (`POST /v1/agents/:id/skills/check`) that runs the upload's rules without saving; SkillEditor and Textarea in packages/ui, on /design first: a template choice, a text area per file, the live check's line, the findings in plain words, and Save that opens only once the check passed; the skills page's "Create a skill" dialog on the useSkillEditor hook, which checks the text after a pause in typing, drops answers for older text and saves through the existing upload path. ## 2026-10-12, UI-U2 Polish and fast iteration
Status: done. The third unit of the four-unit session. (1) The reveal moment: the onchain reveal stays automatic and fair, and the app hides the result until the owner clicks Reveal. RevealMoment in packages/ui (on /design in four states): while the chain has not drawn, the pre-reveal placeholder with the keeper's time remaining counting down (15 seconds on the fork, 90 elsewhere) and "any moment now" at zero; once drawn, the placeholder with a pulsing frame and Reveal; the click shakes the placeholder, bursts the real art in with the tier's rays, and says the rarity plainly (the tier's share of the 1,000, the species' count), a one-of-one marked "1 of 1" with gold rays and "no other agent will ever be this species"; reduced motion skips the animation. The seen flag lives in the browser per environment and agent (apps/web/src/agent/reveal-seen.ts); the mint panel takes a `reveal` prop and keeps its status line to "revealed" until the click; My Agents shows the moment in place of the art until seen, and keeps it for the rest of the visit after the click. (2) The performance window: the $100 figure and the three scores as four square tiles (two across on a phone), the holdings as tiles with the table a click away (MakeupSwitch, with the cost basis in the table), and friendly empty states with the next action (the chart's and the makeup's, each a dashed block with an icon, plain words and a link). (3) The owner's UI direction on the remaining pages: fact tiles and a tile grid in the design system (packages/ui/components/agent/tiles.tsx); the Goal page opens with four tiles (aggressiveness, tokens, research, plan changes) from the form's current values, each with Change beside it that jumps to its section; the Research page opens with four tiles (research is, next Scan, spent today, cycles) with Pause or Resume on the status tile; the vault's holdings as tiles with the table on request and its two scores as square tiles; the console's Agents page as agent tiles (species and tier, runtime and state, credits and spend, the last action in plain words, owner and funding address, the actions) in place of the eight-column table. (4) The replay tool, in the dev console: every stage's prompt and every cycle's SOUL.md are stored from now on (migration 0035: `stage_runs.prompt`, `research_cycles.soul`, `replay_of`, `replay_overrides`, the REPLAY kind, `platform.playbook_overrides`); a REPLAY cycle runs one stored stage again on the same prompt, caps, ceiling and model in a sandbox that mounts an edited playbook or owner skill text under the package's own frontmatter (materialize's SkillTextOverride; saved playbook versions mount for that agent at every start); inside it every data, chain and platform tool answers from the original stage's stored results through a replay hook (tool-server's ReplayHook, before any meter, log or upstream), free and marked `replay`, and refuses any input the stage never sent ("Not among the stored inputs of the replayed stage"), so only the model call is paid; no universe, proposal or narration. Routes: `GET /v1/stages/:id/replay` (the prompt, the stored inputs, the texts, the replays), `POST .../replay` (202 with the cycle), `POST .../replay/save` (a playbook as the agent's next version, an owner skill as its next patch version through the upload's checks). The console: Replay on every model stage of a cycle; the replay page with the prompt, the inputs (answered from the store against runs again), each text editable with a changed marker, Run replay, the old and new output side by side with the cost and what changed row by row, every replay of the stage, Save as new version. (5) Tests: the mount override, the data tools answering only stored inputs with nothing metered or fetched, the engine's replay (the stored prompt word for word, the edited playbook in the sandbox, the original SOUL.md with the replay's marker, nothing fetched, every call free, the unknown input refused, the brief accepted, no proposal or activity), the routes, the console's helpers and page; the playbook override store; the reveal moment's words and phases, the seen flag; the goal and research tile words; the makeup switch; the mint, My Agents, window, design, cycles and the new replay page at 1440px and 380px with the accessibility scan. (6) A live replay on the dev stack (evidence/ui-u2/LIVE_REPLAY.md): a review cycle on agent #1, its Zoom out replayed in a real sandbox with an edited playbook (0.19 USDC of model, every tool answered from the store, the one call that had failed refused as not stored), the edit saved as the agent's playbook version 1.
Summary: packages/db (migrations/0035_replay.ts, index.ts, schema.ts, db.test.ts); packages/tool-server (conventions.ts: ReplayHook, replayed); services/data-tools (server.ts, market-tools.ts, research-tools.ts, token-tools.ts and the test); services/chain-tools (server.ts); services/platform-tools (briefs.ts); services/orchestrator (cycle/replay.ts, cycle/playbook-overrides.ts and tests, cycle/cycle.ts, cycle/store.ts, cycle/stages.ts, cycle-routes.ts, orchestrator.ts, noop.ts, hermes/materialize.ts and the test, tools/servers.ts, tools/platform-store.ts, terminal.ts, cycle.test.ts, terminal.test.ts); packages/ui (components/agent/reveal-moment.tsx, tiles.tsx, mint-panel.tsx, window.tsx, owner-pages.tsx, goal.tsx, research.tsx, their tests, styles.css, index.ts); apps/web (agent/reveal-seen.ts and test, components mint, my-agents, window, goal, research, vault, the design page, e2e mint, my-agents, window specs, portal-capture.css and the baselines); apps/console (cycles/replay.ts, replay-actions.ts, replay-panel.tsx, replay/page.tsx, cycle-view.tsx, agents/page.tsx, e2e fixtures, fixture-api.mjs, pages.spec.ts and the baselines); evidence/ui-u2; Planv2 DECISIONS (A-95).

## 2026-10-12, F-U13 Real chains for the new contracts
Status: done. The fourth unit of the four-unit session: the mainnet canary rerun, then the testnet v3 stack deployed and run end to end once the owner funded the deployer (the unit stopped at its fourth testnet step for funds and resumed the same evening). (1) The mainnet canary rerun of the current trading contracts on P2-EC's CanaryAgent (`pnpm deploy:canary:v3`, D-316's rules): the fund set (TokenRegistry with USDC and WMON on the real Chainlink feeds, ProtocolRegistryV3 with the three USDC/WMON core pools, OracleAdapterV3, RouteAdapter), Executor v3 with its own RouteAdapter, ProtocolRegistryV3 and OracleAdapterV3, AccountFactoryV3 with its two implementations (caps 10 USDC, the canary owner alone on the allowlist, public deposits off) and the binding: 9 transactions, 7.4289 MON, every step simulated and held to the budget first, each step finding what an earlier run deployed. The twelve contracts verified on Sourcify as exact matches (`pnpm verify:canary:v3`; the four made inside a constructor from forge's broadcast record) and entered in the address book's canary environment with their code hashes, transactions and links. The class F round trip (`pnpm canary:mainnet:v3`, evidence/f-u13/canary-run.json): the PersonalAccountV3 opened, 4.99 USDC deposited, the session key granted on Executor v3 for under 24 hours, 0.45 USDC bought 18.2313 WMON through the signer's v3 outbox path (F-U4's gas rule, simulation, fenced nonce, chain pin, fee caps) on the Uniswap v4 native MON/USDC pool and sold back for 0.44955 USDC, both reconciled into the canary ledger and settled at finalized (1.4 and 1.8 s); the oversized buy refused TRADE_SIZE_EXCEEDED and the swap after the guardian's pauseAll refused PAUSED, both in simulation with nothing signed; the owner's 1 USDC withdrawal while paused, revokeSession, withdrawAll, the keys swept: 14 transactions, 0.5727 MON, 8.0016 MON of the rerun's 10 MON in all, 0.00045 USDC lost to the two swaps. (2) The testnet v3 deployment (`pnpm deploy:testnet:v3`, idempotent, a balance guard before every step that simulates it and stops with the exact shortfall and address): two testnet tokens (TALPHA, TBETA) with TestnetFeeds at 1.00 USD and seeded native MON v4 pools, the new AgentNFT (34 species), the fund set, Executor v3 and its set, AccountFactoryV3 with its two implementations and the binding, and the AttestationVerifier with the TokenRegistry's SET_VERIFIER proposal (executable 2026-10-21 after the nine-day timelock): 21 transactions, every one mined, 5.94 MON of gas and about 1 MON of pool seed; the guard stopped step 5 at 4.0950 MON against about 6.9 needed, and the run continued from it after the owner sent 4 MON. All 18 contracts verified on Sourcify as exact matches (`pnpm verify:testnet:v3`) and entered in the address book's testnet environment (the new AgentNFT in place of P2-EC's, which stays on testnet unused with the v2 set reading it, and the nine v3 ids). The testnet stack (`pnpm testnet:up`, on a reset database with the mint allowlist filled again, A-96) then ran the end-to-end flow on v3 (`pnpm testnet:e2e`, evidence/f-u13/e2e-run-*.json): the mint on the new AgentNFT (agent 1, 338k gas), the real Pyth Entropy reveal in 75 seconds (species 28 Fish, pro), provisioning, credits credited 4 seconds after the transfer, a Scan, the PersonalAccountV3 opened (344k), 0.5 USDC deposited (409k), the grant on Executor v3 (111k), the chain check's 0.02 USDC proposal approved and settled through the signer and Executor v3 on the P2-EC USDC/MON pool at the finalized tag (1.95M limit, 7.5 s from approval, 0.019988 WMON), the over-limit proposal rejected (TRADE_SIZE_EXCEEDED, CONCENTRATION_CAP, GAS_UNFUNDED), disarm, withdrawAll, the account empty. The end-to-end script reads the v3 portfolio now (L-248) and writes to evidence/f-u13; the agent's testnet gas target is 0.9 MON (A-96). (3) Fork dry runs of both deployments: the testnet fork test (both v4 routes, the vault's round trip, the nine-day timelock) and the canary fork test (v4 and Uniswap v3 round trips, pause, withdrawals). (4) The measured differences in evidence/f-u13/REAL_CHAIN.md: gas per contract on both chains (the custody core v3 at 28.9M, a first v3 deposit at 481k, a v3 swap at the rule's 1.95M, all charged as limits), sizes equal to the fork's, finality 0.6 to 0.9 s to receipt and 1.2 to 1.8 s to finalized, the pool 17 bps from Chainlink at the swaps, the USDC/USD feed's hourly cadence against its 3,900-second bound, the nine-day timelock that no real chain can warp (no class A round trip on the canary), testnet's missing v3 venues (placeholder factories, the quoters optional).
Summary: chains/monad/script (TestnetToken.sol, DeployTestnetTokens.s.sol, DeployFund, DeployExecutorV3 and DeployAttestor keeping what they deployed), chains/monad/test (testnet/TestnetDeployV3.t.sol, canary/CanaryDeployV3.t.sol), chains/monad/.gitignore; scripts (deploy-testnet-v3.js, deploy-canary-v3.js, verify-canary-v3.js, canary-mainnet-v3.js, lib/testnet.js); services/chain-tools (reader-v3.ts: the v3 quoters optional); packages/domain (address-book.ts: the canary v3 block, and its test); package.json (the four commands); .gitleaksignore, .prettierignore; evidence/f-u13 (canary-deployment.json, canary-verification.json, canary-run.json, testnet-run.json, REAL_CHAIN.md, HANDOFF.md); Planv2 DECISIONS (A-96); .env gained the testnet screener and attestor keys (generated, throwaway).

Not run: the Playwright suites (no page changed in this unit; UI-U2's runs stand).

Open issues and suggestions:
- The canary's verifier and SET_VERIFIER proposal could be sent now (about 0.3 MON) so a class A round trip can run nine days later; not sent, by A-96.
- The USDC/USD feed's 3,900-second bound against its hourly heartbeat gives a stale window every hour; PB-U1 should set the bound from the feed's documented heartbeat plus a margin.

## UX-U1 part 1: the testnet activation error, and two small portfolio changes (2026-10-13)
Status: done, with the owner's own check pending (the Turn on automatic trading press). 
- The error: every owner route for the owner's agents on testnet failed with a hidden 500 because the testnet RPC provider rate limits the stack (429 Too Many Requests) and the control API read the chain through the primary provider alone; viem reports the refused call as ContractFunctionExecutionError, which is all the log said (L-258). The probe of the readers against the provider showed the same calls succeed or fail at random, with "Too Many Requests" in the details. Not the suspects: the owner session, the orchestrator secret, the agent's gateway key and budget (set to 8 USD at the credits deposit), the funding address's gas, or the testnet feeds (their 15,000-second age is inside testnet's 30-day bound and the oracle priced USDC and WMON).
- The fix: off the fork the control API starts devenv's retrying relay over the primary and the second provider (MONAD_RPC_URL_SECONDARY as config maps it on testnet) and points every reader at it; the relay retries 429, server errors and dropped connections with backoff, alternating providers, and logs by method and reason only. The API's error handler answers 503 with the real reason in plain words for a refused chain read ("The chain's RPC provider is refusing reads right now…") or a reverted one (naming the function), and 500 "The control API hit an error" only for anything else; the web shows the API's message as it is.
- The restart: `pnpm testnet:down` and `pnpm testnet:up` (the stack's own commands; the indexer, the API, the orchestrator and the web restarted together, since the stack has no per-service restart). After it, twelve reads of agent 2 and six of its why-not-traded route answered 200 each, with no failed route in the log. The owner's press of Turn on automatic trading could not be done here (it needs the owner's wallet); the arming read behind the card now goes through the relay.
- The portfolio page: a Set goal (or Change goal) button sits in the hero's actions beside Deposit and Withdraw; the lower one stays.
- The My Agents dashboard: each agent card with a trading account shows its positions as the same tiles as the portfolio (MakeupSwitch over the window's holdings, with the chain tag), under its three tiles.
Summary: apps/control-api (main.ts: the relay; app.ts: the error handler; package.json: the devenv dependency), apps/web (portfolio-page.tsx, my-agents-page.tsx), packages/skills/src/quick-check.ts (the rule words restored verbatim after CL-U1 shortened them; the control API's test caught it).
- Bugs fixed: L-258.
- Checks: pnpm typecheck, pnpm lint and pnpm format:check clean; the control API's tests (78, on Postgres), the web's (226) and the skills package's (63) pass. Playwright not run, as asked.
- Model spend: none.
- Dev state: the testnet stack restarted at the fix and running; the Base stack and the playtest fork untouched.

## HOST-U1: the public link to the testnet app (2026-10-13)
Status: done for the submission, temporary until the beta's real hosting; two parts wait for the owner's restart of the stack (below). Playwright skipped, as asked.
- The web app runs on Vercel at https://vivarium-testnet.vercel.app (project vivarium-testnet, root directory apps/web, framework Next.js), built here from the repo root with APP_ENV=testnet, CONTROL_API_URL set to the tunnel's URL and PRIVY_APP_ID, the only three values the build and its server get; the prebuilt output alone is uploaded (L-259). scripts/check-web-build.js passed every line: no claim signer key, no Privy secret and no mock wallet in any browser bundle. `pnpm host:vercel` repeats the deploy (it sets the three values on Vercel, builds, uploads, and records the deployment's origins for the guard).
- The backend stays on the owner's computer behind a guard and a Cloudflare quick tunnel (`pnpm host:up`, `pnpm host:down`, `pnpm host:status`; scripts/host-tunnel.js). The guard on 127.0.0.1:4190 forwards only /health and the control API's /v1 routes (public and owner) to 4100; the orchestrator, LiteLLM, Postgres, Redis, the signer and every fork are not reachable (the orchestrator's path answers 404 through the tunnel). CORS allows localhost:3000 and the origins pnpm host:vercel records in .dev/host-origins.json, read live, so a deploy never needs the tunnel restarted; a stranger origin's preflight gets 403. Rate limits per client address a minute: 10 on mint claims, eligibility and sessions, 30 on owner actions, 240 on reads; the eleventh eligibility call in a minute answered 429 in the check. A quick tunnel needs no Cloudflare account and gets a new hostname at every start, so `pnpm host:vercel` follows every `pnpm host:up`.
- The demo spending guard (D-383): research (a sweep, a Scan, a cycle) runs only for agents whose owner is on DEMO_RESEARCH_ALLOWLIST (the testnet stack preloads the owner's three playtest wallets and the test wallet; .env overrides) and only while the day's model spend in platform.model_calls is under DEMO_DAILY_MODEL_BUDGET_USD (5 USD on the testnet stack, inside the gateway's 15 USD). A refused run says why in the owner's words through the control API. Trading, deposits and withdrawals are not gated.
- Testnet minting open to any wallet within the per-wallet limit (MINT_OPEN=on on the testnet stack, D-383); the mint allowlist is not read there. New wallets open a trading account because the registrar (`pnpm testnet:depositors once | watch`, started by pnpm host:up) adds every agent owner the indexer records to the AccountFactoryV3 depositor allowlist with the deployer's key, since the factory's allowlist itself switches off only through its timelock; the caps stay testnet's. Its first pass found both current owners already allowed.
- Pending the owner's restart: MINT_OPEN and the demo guard are environment values the control API and the orchestrator read at start, so they apply after `pnpm testnet:down` and `pnpm testnet:up` (the stack has no per-service restart); the tunnel survives it. No service was restarted in this unit.
- The owner's step: add https://vivarium-testnet.vercel.app to Privy's allowed origins (the Privy dashboard, the app, Settings, Allowed origins), or the login on the public site is refused by Privy.
Summary: scripts (host-tunnel.js, host-vercel.js, testnet-depositors.js new; testnet-stack.js), apps/web (vercel.json, .gitignore), apps/control-api (main.ts, app.ts), services/orchestrator (orchestrator.ts, api.ts, main.ts), packages/config (variables.ts, config.test.ts), .env.example, .gitignore, package.json.
- Bugs fixed: L-259.
- Checks: pnpm typecheck, pnpm lint and pnpm format:check clean; config (122), control API (78) and the orchestrator's api, scan and activation tests (31) pass; scripts/check-web-build.js passed; the deployed page answers 200 with its bundle naming the tunnel host; health, a preflight and a read from the Vercel origin pass through the tunnel. Playwright skipped.
- Model spend: none.
- Dev state: the testnet stack running as the owner left it; the host tunnel and the registrar running (pnpm host:status); the Base stack and the playtest fork untouched.

## P-SWEEP: why the testnet activation sweep stopped, and the research universe (2026-10-13)
Status: done. Part A of the owner's two-part session.
- The diagnosis: agent 2's two sweeps (06:14 and 06:29 UTC) each completed the Scan, then the Dive was skipped with BUDGET_SHORT and the rest skipped; the panel read "The sweep stopped at a limit … Try again". Not the sandbox (the lease ended "cycle finished"), the gateway keys or budgets, the demo guard (not yet loaded then), credits (9.68 USDC), the RPC, the tool servers or the feeds. The cause was the budget rule (L-260).
- The fix: the activation cycle's stages budget against the sweep's ceiling (A-101); a stalled sweep ends as STALLED after an hour, with words the owner reads.
- The live proof on testnet, after `pnpm testnet:down` and `pnpm testnet:up` at 07:34 UTC (the tunnel stayed up; the restart also loaded HOST-U1's MINT_OPEN and demo guard): activation act-773b84aa for agent 2 ran Scan (0.174 USDC, 4 themes), pools and prices (3 of 3 priced), two Dives (0.549, LARGE_CAP_CORE_LANE and MON_SUPPLY_UNLOCK), the Challenge (0.085, both theses stand), the Test, and the Zoom out (0.218, no plan: the limits bind on a 6 USDC account); 1.026 USDC charged of the 3.80 ceiling.
- The universe (D-384): that cycle recorded 150 tokens, 3 buyable on the account's testnet registry, 47 Monad mainnet tokens research only, and 100 Base tokens research only, every one with why it was or was not researched; the Scan read list_tokens with chain all twice. Base's tokens come from the research discovery process (102 tokens, 10 class F, on its first pass), a testnet stack service from now on.
- Also fixed: the fork tests broken since CL-U1 (L-261).
Summary: services/orchestrator (cycle budget, store, universe, config prompt, activation watchdog, tool source, research-discovery.ts, main and orchestrator wiring, tests), services/data-tools (list_tokens chain), packages/trading (STALLED words), packages/domain (deployed feed bounds), packages/config (index export), scripts (testnet-stack service, fund.js, deploy-canary-v3.js).
- Bugs fixed: L-260, L-261.
- Checks: pnpm typecheck, lint and format:check clean; the full vitest run 2,661 passed and 20 skipped with six fork files failing on L-261, then those six passing (20 tests) after the fix; the domain tests 180. Playwright not run: the owner's testnet stack is hosted publicly on 3000 and the Heavy work rule forbids a suite beside a dev server; Part A changes no page.
- Model spend: about 0.81 USD at the gateway's cost for the live sweep (1.026 USDC charged to the agent's credits); the day's window 2.29 USD.

## RP-U1: the research report (2026-10-13)
Status: done. Part B of the owner's two-part session, after P-SWEEP's gate.
- The report step: when a research cycle ends, the orchestrator gathers its accepted briefs, the universe and every stored tool result, and one model call on the agent's own gateway key (research-medium) writes a structured report: the executive summary and recommendation, the market overview and snapshot, the universe considered, a section per researched token (what it is, the fundamentals checklist, evidence for and against with sources, risks, the thesis and its kill condition, confidence), the skeptic's objections and answers, and the recommended portfolio or why the plan should not change. The universe, the stages and every source by class are stored from the records, never from the model.
- The guardrails: every text field goes through the brief validator's checks, now shared as textReasons (every number traces to a stored result, every URL was retrieved, no skill or stage prompt wording, no internal marker) plus a new one, no twelve-word passage pasted from a page, a snippet or an X post; a sentence that fails is removed and counted, a source not retrieved is dropped.
- Cost: its own 0.20 USDC ceiling (A-102), part of every cycle's shown maximum (a Medium sweep shows 4.00, A-51's cap), checked before it runs against the sweep's ceiling or the day's budget and the credits, skipped with its reason otherwise.
- Storage and access: migration 0036 (platform.research_reports); the control API's /v1/agents/:id/reports and /v1/agents/:id/reports/:cycleId, owner only, with the activation's and the plan's report links; the orchestrator's POST /v1/cycles/:cycleId/report for the operator.
- The web app: the report page at /agents/:id/research/reports/:cycleId, a document built from packages/ui's new ResearchReportDocument and ReportList (on /design first), with Download as PDF (the browser's print under the report's own title; print styling on paper tokens hides the app's header, navigation, banner and actions and keeps a token's section on one page); the report list on the Research page; links from the plan and from the finished activation sweep. Deployed to https://vivarium-testnet.vercel.app.
- The live report on testnet: for activation act-773b84aa's cycle cycle-dda23a23-df0b-44ea-91c7-f577a58c1a74 (agent 2), written by research-medium for 0.162 USDC of 0.20, nothing removed by the checks: one researched token (WMON, low confidence), six snapshot figures, four objections answered, the 150-token universe, 18 sources, the stance "no change". The first attempt failed on a reply cut off by its output limit (L-262).

## CQ-U1: cleanup after the sprint (2026-10-13)
Status: done. Part A of the owner's session; on the owner's change of plan, UX-U2 (Part B) was not started (front-end work moved to the ui-lane worktree).
- Orientation: the machine had rebooted, so Docker Desktop, the testnet stack, the host tunnel and the registrar were down; with the owner's yes, Docker and LiteLLM were started, then pnpm testnet:up and pnpm host:up (detached) and pnpm host:vercel (the new tunnel URL); the public page answered 200 and /health through the tunnel answered testnet, chain 10143.

- Loose ends connected: identical helpers shared (packages/ui lib/format.ts usdcCents and percentFromBps for eight copies; scripts import alive() from lib/dev-all.js); the window page's links into the portfolio's tabs open their tab again (L-264); the e2e fake API serves RP-U1's reports route; the web specs follow UX-U1's tabbed layout (L-265).

## ITER (2026-10-13): Iteration mode
- CLAUDE.md gains an "Iteration mode" section, the default until the owner calls a cleanup pass. The testnet stack was restarted; the primary testnet and mainnet RPCs answer 429 (monthly capacity), so the signer failed at startup, and the stack now runs with MONAD_TESTNET_RPC_URL set to the secondary provider in the process environment only (.env unchanged).
- Agent #4 (8.00 USDC) holds SIMULATION_FAILED: the testnet MON/USDC v4 pool (0x40ab892d) quotes at most about 0.466 USDC in before the quoter's not-enough-liquidity revert, below the 0.50 USDC minimum, so its 0.796 USDC WMON buy cannot fill until the pool gets more MON liquidity.
- .env: MONAD_TESTNET_RPC_URL is the former secondary provider now (the old value kept as a comment), so a plain pnpm testnet:up works. The Vercel web build reads no RPC variable (APP_ENV, CONTROL_API_URL, PRIVY_APP_ID only; the browser's RPC is the public one in packages/config/src/chains.ts).
- Testnet MON/USDC pool: the deployer added 3.45 MON and 3.67 USDC through the PoolSeeder in the seeded range [-276420, -276220] (USDC transfer 0x8935277a533ba14ca0565a6f554171bc5d05e729fa9398e0dc8dc92622d48239, addLiquidity 0xd9e8ab6e63f53e83b774810befe858345a8c34edd4fd67ab46d66be2ab51e64e, both mined); liquidity 0.96e14 to 8.10e14; a 0.80 USDC buy quotes 0.798 MON. Agent #4 still holds: the sentinel set INCIDENT (GAS_LOW, funding address 0x42c1e4e05924478e3a29efa39970ce0da6f8be7f at 0 MON, 0.2626 MON needed a trade) and a goal saved at 21:46Z made its epoch-2 plan stale (STRATEGY_EPOCH_STALE).
- Agent #4 traded on testnet: its funding address was funded, the sentinel's GAS_LOW incident cleared by operator after review, an ACTIVATION cycle (0.79 USDC of credits) proposed an epoch-3 plan the owner approved at 22:22Z, and two WMON buy legs settled: 0x110bc6d514a1c281fe462135e530eae19bcc0c73c211b91b7e3fd5161b78955e (0.796 USDC to 0.794 WMON) and 0x4a92492573a22c7f1745e8d49465b6d3c35685638a313066943a976c611ea10d (0.796 USDC to 0.793 WMON); the runner then holds on COST_HURDLE.
- Vault "Open deposits" from the owner's wallet reverted with LeaderStakeTooLow(0, 0): agent #4's vault (0x5F75589Ca0eC1Ff0800cdb41bAa864805D4fb1cE) has no leader stake, and setDepositsOpen needs one of at least 1 USDC and 5% of the vault; the wallet's gas estimate reverted, so OKX could not confirm (the app sets no gas limit; the wallet holds 1.44 MON). The contract works as designed. The Vault tab now lists the steps in order (a new StepList on /design), names the blocking reason, and simulates each vault step before the wallet opens.
- Testnet trading profile (packages/policy/src/trading-profile.ts): on APP_ENV testnet alone, no minimum trade and a 0.5% cost limit that counts each leg's gas at mainnet Chainlink MON/USD; the testnet profile must never apply to mainnet or the beta. Onchain limits are unchanged: the Executor policy loosens only through the deployer's 9-day timelock, and CustodyCoreV3's 12%, 45% and 1% backstops and ExecutorV3's 20-trade ring are constants. BUILD_PLAN's beta gate notes record the owner's direction for the mainnet limits and the gas limit from an estimate.
- Trading tab: Automatic trading folds to its state and toggle (Details opens the rest); a Live trades panel (a new LiveTrades on /design) lists the approved plan's legs newest first with status and transaction, refreshing every 2.5 s while one is on its way, and a hold as one line; the plan cards follow it.
- Step 6 (agent #4 to its 30% target) is blocked: the testnet MON/USDC pool sits 0.46% above the feed (about 1.9 MON of sales would bring it back), and with the onchain 10% leg cap agent #4's legs are at most about 0.80 USDC, where the runner's gas reserve (0.26 MON, about 0.006 USD at MON's market price) alone is about 0.8%, over the 0.5% limit. The stack restart's gas step (scripts/testnet-gas.js) topped the reveal keeper up to 0.35 MON from the deployer, leaving the deployer 0.021 MON, under the 0.3 MON floor the owner set; its feed-writer transfer failed.
- Trading tab: a Reports list (the Research tab's newest written reports). The owner trade panel was not built: an owner-requested trade needs a new owner route that quotes and pre-checks the trade (assessTradeV3) and proposes it, and a migration widening intents.source beyond 'agent' and 'template'; the trade flow, the Executor and its limits would carry it unchanged (no contract work).
- Research tab: a Next research strip with Research now (the owner's Scan route, its 0.15 to 0.30 USDC cost shown first, A-31), a branch chart (the agent's art as root, a line per stage that ran and per source used, a sub-branch per call, live), and What happened written from the report's structured facts (snapshot figures, universe, evidence counts, objections, stage outcomes, positions); no model call.

The owner trade panel is on BUILD_PLAN's later list (OT-U1) with what it needs.
- TALPHA and TBETA (testnet) have no USDC pools; each has one native MON pool on the unofficial v4 that fills about 0.5 MON either way, so a USDC buy routes USDC to MON to the token, and any leg over about 0.5 USDC reverts in the quote. The stack restart's gas step again sent a feed-writer transfer from the deployer that failed on chain (about 0.002 MON of gas).
- Goal: optional allocation (Degen under 100M USD, Growth 100M to 1B USD, Conservative top assets; 5% steps summing to 100%) and maxPositions (at most the style's) join the goal schema; the brief states the split and the positions, the envelope and the Test enforce the owner's positions; Conservative holds up to 5 (was 8; the portfolio-construction skill bumped to 1.0.2). The Set goal page is rebuilt (style tiles, allocation sliders, positions, Safety, Plan changes and two stricter limits folded, a live preview with the save that returns the owner where they came from); the model tier, intensity, budget and reserve move to a Research settings panel on the Research tab. Fields no longer on the page (excluded tokens, the other three stricter limits) keep their saved values. The e2e goal specs (heavy suite, not run) still drive the old page.
- Set goal: every section folds as one CollapsibleCard (the full card, full-size title, its value when closed); Stricter limits lists most trades a day, largest trade, largest position, slippage, minimum USDC kept and excluded tokens, each its own row; no minimum trade size.
- Activity: GET /v1/agents/:id/money-events (read only, public) lists the trading account's and the vault's deposits and withdrawals from their own event logs (scanned in 1,000-block chunks from each contract's first block, cached in memory; the indexer does not record these accounts yet) and the credit top-ups the indexer recorded, with when each account was created. A shared Activity panel (deposits, withdrawals, trades, research runs with cost, credit top-ups, plan approvals; tabs All, Trades, Deposits, Research) shows on Overview and the Vault tab; the trades, runs and plans show for the owner only. Vault tab: the chart lists its readings until there are two (the 12 earlier snapshots had no value, from before the vault had shares), About is a plain list with the next step, the Deposit tab opens first, the credits panel is Add research credits. Suggestion: index PersonalAccountV3 and StrategyVaultV3 events so the first read is not a chain scan (about 10 to 25 s cold).
- Orchestrator "stopping on its own" was testnet:down's race: it listed only the services still alive 500 ms after signalling the supervisor, which stops services itself, so ones the supervisor stopped first looked dead; the one real stop in the kept logs was a SIGTERM (a hand restart). Fixed the list; the supervisor now restarts the orchestrator on exit (5 s, doubling to 60 s, gives up after 5 quick exits; verified with a SIGKILL) and keeps the last 5 runs' logs.
- Research map (agent #4's activation run, 3 min 47 s, 0.79 USDC): Scan (Low, 0.16) read the market snapshot, the portfolio, list_tokens twice and new_pools; two Dives (Medium, 0.24 and 0.15), one per theme the Scan named, the second screening WETH; Challenge (Medium, 0.07) and Zoom out (Medium, 0.16) read only the platform's context; Test (no model) free. No web search, no page reads and no X call in any stage. Research picks Monad mainnet tokens, which have no code on testnet, so testnet can trade only its own USDC, WMON, TALPHA and TBETA.
- Research, tonight's version: the Scan reads the market with a required web search and X search, lists about five candidate tokens per category of the goal's split (CoinMarketCap rank stands in for market cap) and chooses tokens to Dive; each Dive researches one named token with a required web and X search (complete_stage refuses until both ran; a failed call counts); Dives per run by tier, Low 2, Medium 3, High 4; stage ceilings Scan 0.30, Dive 0.30 (0.60 on High), Challenge 0.10 (0.20), Zoom out 0.25 (0.50), report 0.20, so a Medium run is at most 1.75 USDC (about 1.2 expected); the Zoom out plans only tokens tradable here, USDC may be held, WMON is never padding. Playbooks: scan 2.2.0, dive 2.1.0, zoom-out 2.4.0. Not done in the time box: the market overview at the top of the report and the pitch-format report page (both in RS-U1).
- Run review (cycle-e5527ca0, 01:23 to 01:28 UTC, 0.77 USDC): the Scan made its required web and X searches, read list_tokens once and chose one token, WETH (not three); the WETH Dive made its web and X searches and stopped at its 0.30 ceiling; the Scan also used its whole 0.30; the plan proposed WMON at 30% (WETH is not tradable on testnet). An X search costs 0.0625 USDC, six times a web search. Suggestion: raise the Scan and Dive ceilings (about 0.45 each) so three Dives can run.
- Research tab: a run status strip (running with stage, timer and cost against the tier's most; writing the report; done with time, cost and View report; a New report notice), a research record, What happened written live from each call, clickable branches (a step panel with what it looked up, what came back and its sources; a line filters What happened), New on the newest report with the run it came from. Overview's trade history links to the explorer; the Trading tab labels plans and trades with their run; the report leads with its main decision across the real tokens researched, with a testnet box for how the plan executes there.
- Scan chooses exactly the run's Dive count in tokens (Medium 3) across the split, or gives fewerTokensReason (complete_stage refuses otherwise; the report carries it); Scan and Dive ceilings 0.45 (Dive 0.90 on High): a Medium run is at most 2.35 USDC, High 4.95, Low 1.90. Scan playbook 2.3.0.
- Trading and Configure: Review plan from a finished run; after approval the Trading tab scrolls to Live trades with a notice, per-token progress bars and each leg's state; a collapsible Trade history; an Overview banner while a plan is being traded; the Configure page's 2D or 3D view with a collapsible viewer, quick links under the art, a skill-slot toggle on the model, and catalog skill cards with filters and a detail panel. Agent #4 sits in INCIDENT (sentinel:STALE_FEED since 23:10 UTC); its approved plan (WMON 20%) is already met (IN_BAND).
- Plan card: Buy, Sell or Hold now come from the account's share against the plan's target and range (it compared with the previous plan's target, so the 30% to 20% plan showed Sell WMON while the account already held 20% and the runner held IN_BAND); a plan the account already matches shows as Hold with Approve (no trades). Agent #4's INCIDENT (STALE_FEED) cleared after the sentinel showed no active breach and MON/USD was re-dated at 02:03. - Assets: the Vivarium logo in the nav, as the favicon (app/icon.png) and centered in the home hero's reserved spot; the goal icons on the style tiles (moderate as Balanced; growth unplaced, three styles); the intelligence SVGs on the model tier in Research settings; the hero gains Mint an agent, a four-sentence description and larger flow pills. Restarted the stack (both testnet RPCs answer chain 10143); the supervisor now restarts the indexer and the API too, with the orchestrator's backoff. The tunnel was down: host:up gave https://discounted-amazing-grams-avenue.trycloudflare.com and host:vercel redeployed https://vivarium-testnet.vercel.app with it.
- The depositor registrar was stalled again on the deployer's MON (0.0020 against 0.0063 an add) with 0x9155...9E1C (agent #7's minter) queued; moved 0.1 testnet MON from the throwaway test wallet to the deployer (tx 0x3dea185f...646a, mined) and the wallet was added. Suggestion: a deployer balance alert, since every testnet operator wallet is near empty (only the reveal keeper 0.197, the feed 0.107 and the test wallet 0.26 MON hold any).
- Cloud hosting (2026-10-14): the testnet backend moved off the laptop to a hardened DigitalOcean droplet (161.35.99.236: key-only SSH, ufw 22/80/443, security updates applied and automatic, 4 GB swap) under Docker Compose (infra/server: Postgres, Redis, LiteLLM, API, orchestrator, indexer, depositor registrar, Base research, the guard and Caddy at https://api.161.35.99.236.sslip.io, each with restart always and a health check); the laptop's testnet database and LiteLLM schema were restored there with identical counts (7 agents, 5 reports, 7 cycles, 4 intents, 17 gateway keys); Vercel was redeployed on the new address; an ntfy monitor (services, public API, disk, operator balances), nightly backups and docs/HOSTING.md were added. Fixed on the way: the image needed anvil for the token screens, and a test fork that gave up crashed the orchestrator by logging after its log closed (mainnet RPC 429s). The hosted owner routes and Research now were checked with the test wallet; agent #4's cycle ran on the server (sandbox, gate tools, gateway) and failed NO_STAGE_RECORD (the model never called complete_stage).
- Suggestions: the laptop's network (and this session's web fetcher) filters *.sslip.io as safebrowse.io, so a browser there cannot reach the API; serving the API through a Vercel rewrite on the site's own origin, or a real domain, would avoid such filters. The deployer (0.096 MON), screener, attestor and sentinel (0 MON) are under their alert floors.
- Hosted API through the site (2026-10-14, HOTFIX): networks that filter *.sslip.io could not reach the API, so the browser now calls the site's own /api/*, which a route forwards to the API with the visitor's IP and PROXY_SHARED_SECRET (Vercel production env and the server's secrets file); the guard limits by that IP only when the secret matches. Checked live: a laptop through the site hit the mint limit as its own address while another address through the site did not, and a direct call with forged IP, secret and forwarding headers was limited by its sender. Before the fix, six session requests for agent #4 were refused at 18:07 UTC from one Vercel address. The live site is built from metropolis-hotfix (the tag plus the two hotfix commits).
- Demo research open to every owner (2026-10-14, UI-C): infra/server/compose.yaml no longer sets DEMO_RESEARCH_ALLOWLIST (unset, every agent whose owner funded credits may run research) and DEMO_DAILY_MODEL_BUDGET_USD defaults to 25; LiteLLM's proxy-wide max_budget (infra/litellm/config.yaml) is 25 USD a day too, so 25 USD is the one daily cap. Applied on the server (litellm, orchestrator and research-base restarted; the orchestrator logs "research for every owner, under 25 USD of model spend a day"); research-run for agent #5 (owner never allowlisted, no credits) now answers credits_exhausted instead of demo_refused.

## CO-U1: Colosseum, Base Sepolia and Arbitrum Sepolia beside Monad testnet (2026-10-14)
- APP_ENV=testnet runs on Monad testnet, Base Sepolia or Arbitrum Sepolia by TESTNET_CHAIN (Monad by default); each runs as its own stack on the server (infra/server/chain.yaml, its own database and Redis database, the shared Postgres, Redis and LiteLLM, its guard at its own Caddy host), and the Metropolis Monad stack stays on its pinned image (VIVARIUM_REVISION=d641f68). Contracts: AccountFactoryV3 takes its implementations (initcode under EIP-3860), an l2 build profile (via-IR, runs 1, no metadata hash) fits Executor v3 in 24,139 bytes, AgentCopyNFT (a Monad agent's platform-minted copy), the owner's limits (25% a trade, 60% a position, 5% USDC, 1.5% slippage with a 2% backstop, 48 trades a day; parity keeps F-U4's values as PARITY_POLICY), and AgentNFT's deck at the final 25 species with no one of ones. The trade flow, runner and snapshots run on the v3 set alone; L2 trades settle at the safe block.
- Base Sepolia, live: TestUSDC (faucet), AgentCopyNFT, a WETH/tUSDC v3 pool at Chainlink's price (7 bps off at seeding, about 0.04 WETH in a narrow range), the fund and Executor v3 sets and the factory (blocks 48,119,589 to 48,119,699); copies of agents 1 and 4 minted; the test agent opened its account, deposited 50 tUSDC, armed, and traded 1 tUSDC for 0.000412 WETH (tx 0xe5118351...c9ad, reconciled); https://vivarium-base.vercel.app serves it through /api. Research ran on Base's market (Scan, Dive, Challenge) and failed NO_STAGE_RECORD at Zoom out on the Low tier; a Medium run followed.

- CO-U1 2026-10-17, Part A (research behavior): the Zoom out playbook (3.0.1) is the portfolio manager's method: per researched and held token an expected return over the horizon, the main risks and a confidence 0 to 100, sized by expected return times confidence under the goal's mindset, the cash level with its reason, every holding reconsidered; no anchoring to the plan in force (the research context carries the holdings with their thesis and exit, never weights; the SOUL.md goal block and get_goals_and_limits no longer carry the two-asset WMON target and range) and no caution gates (ADD only on a standing thesis, the evidence gate, "prefer few, small moves"). The Challenge (2.2.0) informs confidence and never vetoes or caps; the Dive (2.3.3) treats a token's registration on this stack as the platform's concern, never a risk or a reason for no thesis; the Scan (2.5.3) on a testnet Dives on the run's full count of real tokens (WETH, cbBTC, gMON, AUSD, the top Monad mainnet tokens), never WMON, TALPHA or TBETA, with no fewer-tokens exit; portfolio construction 1.0.6 follows. - CO-U1 2026-10-17, the plan card bug (the Research tab said "Review plan" and the Trading tab showed no plan), for every agent. Pipeline map. (a) Where a run writes its outcome: Cycle.run (services/orchestrator/src/cycle/cycle.ts) runs the Scan, the Dives and the Challenge, then zoomOut(): the Test stage (a TEST stage_run; its outcome holds the envelope and one checked entry per draft the Zoom out offers through complete_stage) and the Zoom out (a ZOOM_OUT stage_run; its complete_stage decision lands in platform.stage_records.decision). Once the Zoom out ends with a record, ProposalService.fromZoomOut (cycle/proposals.ts) writes one platform.plan_proposals row: NO_CHANGE, or a PROPOSE equal to the plan in force, is kind no_change, status recorded (plan kept); any other PROPOSE is evaluated (Test v2 for target_portfolio@1, the goal's limits for rebalance_bands@1) and stored pending_owner (waits for approval, expires after 24 hours), applied (plans.set by the agent: a platform.strategy_params row, agent_states.strategy_epoch plus one, READY to RUNNING), or rejected (failed the evaluator, or plans.set threw); the narrator adds an activity entry. A draft the Test refuses inside complete_stage writes no proposal, only its checked entry; a run that stops, fails or is capped before a decision writes nothing; a routine run with no Dives ends NOTHING_MATERIAL without a Zoom out. (b) Wait or apply: ProposalService.approvalNeeded answers ask_first with no goal or planChanges ASK_FIRST, first_plan_after_arming while an arming is open and no plan was set since it began, and null otherwise, so APPLY_AND_TELL applies at once (also before any arming). (c) The Trading tab's card: GET /v1/agents/:id/plan-card (apps/control-api/src/app.ts, owner session, owner.epoch from the chain) ran planCard (apps/control-api/src/proposals.ts): expireDue, plans.active, pending = the newest pending_owner row for the chain and agent with no epoch, owner, account or expiry filter, then the last eight rows for recent and noChange. plan_proposals carries no account id or type: a proposal is per agent, and the Test reads the agent's one managed account (the vault unless the owner chose the personal account), so no proposal can be written for an account the card cannot show. The approve route refused STALE when the strategy epoch differed from agent_states.strategy_epoch, which every goal save and plan set bumps. (d) apps/web/src/components/trading/trading-page.tsx waits for the portfolio read, then showed the pending proposal, else the latest applied one among those eight rows, else the latest decided one of any status under "In force since"; with none, "No plan yet: the activation sweep makes the first one." (e) apps/web/src/components/research/research-page.tsx built the finish strip from the research read (status and stages) and the card's newest no_change: any finished newest run said "Review plan" unless it was the run that kept the plan.
  Where they disagreed: D1 the strip said Review plan for runs that made no proposal (stopped for credits: agent #2 at 10-16 05:30, 17:50 and 17:54; drafts refused then the Zoom out capped: agent #6's cycle-af9b6bee today; NOTHING_MATERIAL; failed steps) and for plans applied at once (agent #2 on 10-15), L-275. D2 the card showed a rejected, declined or expired proposal as the plan in force, lost the applied one after eight newer rows, and badged an agent-applied plan Approved, L-276. finishView and planCardShows. packages/trading plan-card.ts: the card moved here with inForce, the proposal that set the plan in force, and latestRun, the newest run with its outcome; pending is the newest unexpired one for the current owner whatever the strategy epoch; storing a waiting or applied proposal marks every other waiting one superseded, kept as status expired with reason_code SUPERSEDED so no migration is needed and older images still read it). 340454e (orchestrator: a capped Zoom out with a recorded decision is stored; an approval is no longer stale on the strategy epoch, only for an earlier owner, and it supersedes the other waiting proposals; D-291 untouched). c3c0d01 (web: the strip from the run's outcome, "Plan ready: it waits for your approval" with Review plan only when its proposal is the one the card shows waiting, "Plan applied: your goal applies plan changes at once" or "Plan approved and in force" with View plan, "Plan unchanged" with View current plan, otherwise "No plan:" and the reason, plus "Review the waiting plan" when another run's plan waits; - CO-U1 2026-10-18, plans are never refused for size (agent #6). Code: on a testnet the Test never refuses depth, turnover or the cost of moving in one go; it records the runner's legs on the checked draft and in the plan's cost (estimateLegs, "about 11 legs over about 22 minutes"), and refuses only what the contracts refuse (registry, per-token cap, hard limits) (44226a3, L-282). The DEPTH refusal's "about X% of this account fits in one go; size at or below that" guidance is removed everywhere. The Zoom out's ceiling is 0.75 USDC on every testnet (a Medium run is at most 2.85 USDC, Low 2.40, High 5.95; the Research tab's text says so) (64b92bf). Whatever ends the Zoom out, a run with no recorded decision writes its best checked draft (newest passing, else least refused), evaluated like any proposal; the Trading tab shows a refused one read-only under "Refused by the platform's checks" when no plan waits or is in force (2cd15fb, L-283). The runner quotes first and sizes each leg to what the pools fill within the slippage floor (15 bps headroom) and the cost limit, down to a sixteenth of the leg the plan wants; a buy no size of which fills gives way to the next underweight buy (two more tried); a leg the trade flow refused for SLIPPAGE_TOO_HIGH or SIMULATION_FAILED is sized again at the next check, not after 10 minutes (f29f1cd). Tests: test-stage-v2 13 (3 new: no size refusal on a testnet with the legs estimate, contract refusals kept, a buy that fills nothing), runner-v2 21 (6 new: fitLegValue, a leg sized to the pool then the next leg, the next buy when none fills, a size refusal resized at once), proposals 11 (bestCheckedDraft), cycle 34 (the refused run now writes its least refused draft refused, never offered), stages, activation, api, plan-rules, test-stage 107 in all; web plan-card 9 (refusedDraft); tsc for orchestrator and web; eslint and prettier on every touched file.

  Fixes, owner's five items: (2) approving any plan arms an agent whose permission waits for its first trade (replaces D-291's sweep-only rule; d04d30c); the plan card's button is "Approve and start trading": with no permission on chain it sends the one wallet transaction first, then approves, which arms (plan-approve-flow.ts); a plan in force without trading says so on the card with "Start trading", which grants the permission and arms at once (POST /arming with startTrading) (7d2f2c0). (3) a v3 swap's gas limit is its plain eth_estimateGas from the session key plus 20%, capped at the rule's most, the table's figure only when there is no estimate (e484072). (4) the testnet top-up sends what the trade flow asks less what the key holds, the transfer at 21,000 gas; the ask is one swap at its most cost plus two at what they are billed (e484072, then 92209a7 after agent #8's top-ups parked 1.35 MON). - CO-U1 2026-10-18, the deployer burned MON too fast (owner's five items). Fee audit: Monad testnet's base fee sat at 100 gwei on every sample (40 over about 100k blocks), eth_maxPriorityFeePerGas 2 gwei. The signer signed every swap at maxFeePerGas 202 gwei (2 x base + tip, A-38) and tip 2 gwei; Monad billed 102 gwei (base + tip) on the limit, so a leg cost limit x 102 gwei (agent #8's legs 0.182 to 0.274 MON, 2.18M gas on average, about 0.22 MON), while the node held limit x 202 gwei against the key (0.36 to 0.54 MON) and the top-ups were sized to that for three legs. The leak was MON parked, not MON billed: agent keys held 8.78 MON above what their next leg needs.
  Fixes: (1) on Monad testnet the signer's max fee is the base fee plus the tip (maxFeeBaseMultiple 1; 2 elsewhere), and the trade flow's swap cost matches; a top-up funds two swaps at that price (a3e35c4, de6d2e0, 3251b99). Per leg: billed the same, limit x 102 gwei (about 0.22 MON for a 2.2M-gas leg) before and after; what a key must hold halves (0.22 instead of 0.44), and a top-up for a 2.2M-gas leg is 0.45 MON instead of 0.90. (2) Signer.sweepGas: testnet only, to the deployer only, under the key's lock, never with a transaction in flight or within 30 s of the key's last one (L-270), and the recorded nonce moves past it; a sweep pass every minute keeps each key one leg at its largest recent limit (a3e35c4, de6d2e0). First pass at 07:26 UTC, all at max fee 102 gwei: agent #1 0.1999 MON (0xc1c5db9040991786830019ea6bcbfff565823461a7c45d4e32ce1d46e3629f77), #2 5.0466 (0x4be449e131c62d8de7e7426bbf11e1d190a8db06ccbb6fc7245c9a90b4cd7da6), #6 2.4597 (0xc6b595b27ec60e8fbf9101e5fd70837a16cc21d9cdeee459aedc962182fbe777), #8 1.0776 (0x2e759dc439ed801d699285ab8bf4e6f7e11b18c5b4d4de40ade089139852f9cd): 8.78 MON back; the keys now hold 0.1347, 0.2958, 0.2958 and 0.2740. Three of them logged "failed" (a 429 on the receipt poll after the broadcast); 2b12ad6 waits out RPC errors there. (3) pool-recycler.ts: every 5 minutes on Monad testnet, when MON/USDC sits more than 30 ticks above the oracle or holds under 3 MON within 1.5% above its price, it sells the deployer's TALPHA and TBETA into their MON pools at par (99.5% or better: only the MON agents' buys left there), sells that MON into MON/USDC down to the oracle's tick and adds any rest above the price; the deployer pays gas only and a run that recovers under 0.3 MON sends nothing to MON/USDC (de6d2e0). TestnetToken has no mint (contracts off limits), so it sells from the deployer's 950k of each. Its first passes failed: reads hit 429s (2b12ad6 retries them) and every send asked the node to sign (a bare address as the account, L-289; 6d1517c). The fixed pass run once from the container at 08:19: sold 2.7406 TALPHA (0x730a84cf4e1aa6a8a95e67058f68fb60154fa4f15c2431f704dcaabff1f8142f) and 1.5157 TBETA (0xb5a2b5cc9217272ce4ed71fda72ef2c173823f3d9d35b8a29314da4ef6b87add) for 4.2351 MON, sold it into MON/USDC (0x822fc784363ae24bba7277f1493ed11110c1fe9b17e52bc5e49e680c4e5bf0cb): tick -276203 (1.012) to -276309 against the oracle's -276325, buy side 0.87 to 5.10 MON, a 1.5 USDC MON buy 0.39% over the oracle (before: 1.29% at 0.25 USDC, nothing above 0.75); 0.169 MON of deployer gas for its 6 transactions. (4) BalanceAlarm in the Monad orchestrator: deployer under 2 MON, reveal keeper under 0.5 MON, every 5 minutes, to the owner's ntfy topic (NTFY_SERVER and NTFY_TOPIC copied from monitor.env into chain-monad.env, mode 600, values never printed), again every 6 hours while low, once on refill (3251b99). Fired at 07:26:58 for the keeper (0.299 MON); the keeper was then sent 0.3 MON from the deployer (0x551290486f1849c11e18c5998b851166413c77a5a8ecc8fa00d46bc50d81a727), now 0.599. The deployer and keeper go through one queue in the process (deployer-queue.ts: one send at a time, 8 s apart).

  Suggestions: the recycler cannot refill MON that agents hold as WMON (only what their stand-in buys left in the stand-in pools); each agent holding WMON takes that MON out of MON/USDC for good, so the pool needs a one-time deposit sized to the agents' WMON targets, or a testnet-only MON stand-in; a 10% gas margin instead of 20% would cut each leg about 8%; fewer, larger legs cut the gas most, since a leg costs the same at any size; the host monitor's broken balance check (L-288) for the base stack.
